# Model Plane and chat/agent build plan, 2026-10-14

**Status:** DRAFT FOR APPROVAL. Planning only: no product code, configuration or live-tree file was changed, nothing was committed or pushed. This document is the only artifact written into the repository.
**Plans for:** the Model Plane (Rust and Go) and Verevon's web chat and agent pages (`apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3`), per the audit `C:/dev/verevon-audit-2026-10-14/03-model-plane-chat-and-agents-audit.md` (read-only; copied from the scratch folder, SHA-256 identical).
**Base:** worktree fast-forwarded to commit `afb83928` (the audited HEAD; it was 61 commits behind). The audit also read the live tree `C:/dev/CoresSystem`, which has 324 uncommitted or untracked entries on top of that HEAD. Files that exist only as that uncommitted work are tagged `[live-only]` throughout; the first workstream slice (WS01-S0) lands them before anything depends on them.
**Sibling plans:** (a) Verevon Space, (b) Quarry v2 and browser. Shared pieces and proposed owners are in section 5; items marked "FLAG FOR THE USER TO RECONCILE" need a decision across the three planning sessions.
**How it was produced:** 6 common digests (Haiku 5.5 max, plus two Sonnet 5.5 overlap maps), 27 per-workstream code sweeps (Haiku 5.5 max), 27 file-level designs (Sonnet 5.5 high), plan compilation (Sonnet 5.5 high), then an adversarial review (3 Sonnet 5.5 high skeptics, 81 findings) and a Haiku 5.5 max path/line check of about 3,900 citations (almost all failures were planned NEW files or untagged live-only files). Mechanical fixes are applied in place; the rest are binding amendments in Appendix B, which also records the path conventions found. Per the user's instruction during the run, no Opus and no Fable were used; two early overlap digests ran at Sonnet xhigh before that instruction arrived. Everything read from files was treated as data. Design inputs and sweeps are kept in `.plan-work/` (untracked).

## Executive summary

**What this plan is.** 27 workstreams (WS01-WS27) that resolve 21 of the audit's 22 Priority items (F-t3-019, the Agents and Automations hubs, is a W3 epic in 3.3) plus the Wave 1 and Wave 2 Recommended items they depend on (F-t3-016, 025, 028, 029, 030, 032, 033, 039, 049, 063, 066, 067), each with a file-level change list, contracts, flags, tests, definition of done, evidence and effort; ~12 epics for Waves 3-5; 40 decisions (DEC-01..DEC-40); one dependency graph, proto-tag and migration-number reservation, hot-file matrix, merge trains and lane assignment.

**The shape of the work.**
1. **Phase 0 is landing, not building.** About 78 Model Plane entries and the ZDR/session-core wave exist only in the working tree. Nearly every design names WS01-S0 (eight atomic commits, tag `mp-baseline-0`) as a precondition. The taxonomy gate is red at HEAD today.
2. **Finish what is built but dark before building new.** Deploy and prove the audit outbox fix and the ZDR design (WS01), make MCP tools callable through the signed gate (WS05), put the run context on Do runs (WS02), connectors (WS23) and the browser grant half (WS21) on their already-built sides. Every slice carries a `[FINISH]` or `[BUILD]` tag (see the "Dominant type" column below and each workstream's slice table); WS06, WS10, WS17, WS19, WS22 and WS25 are mostly new.
3. **The spine is the durable loop.** WS02 (run context) then WS03 (checkpoint, resume, park) then WS13 (approvals v2), WS14 (ask_user, plan-continue), WS20 (scheduled Do runs), WS21. It runs as one serial queue by a fixed pair of engineers because it all edits `execution-core` `agent.rs`, `runtime_loop/mod.rs` and `execution.proto`.
4. **Decisions first.** 40 decisions; section 2.2 lists the slices that can start with no decision, and each register row says whether it blocks building or only specific slices. The ones that most change the build: loop host and plan-continue semantics (DEC for WS03/WS14), entitlement ownership (ADR for WS10), audit-chain trust anchor (WS06), unattended-authority contract (WS20), shared proto/scope vocabulary with the Space session (WS13/WS14/WS17), thread workspace vs the 2026-08-22 SandboxBackend deferral (WS22).
5. **Scale.** About 400-520 engineer-days for Wave 1 and 620-850 for Wave 2 (design day-ranges, excluding owner rulings, the external ZDR provider contract and the sibling sessions). With 8 engineers: Wave 1 in 14-19 weeks (spine-bound 10-11), Wave 2 overlapping, whole programme ~37-49 weeks; with 5 engineers ~60-80 weeks. Cutting scope shortens Wave 2 more than adding engineers.
6. **Honest corrections to the audit** found while designing (the code wins where they differ): e.g. the audit's "no proto change" for cause codes is false (`CreateApprovalRequest` has no context channel); durable send receipts do not exist for user messages (WS04 is larger than M); `agentic_run_stream` is not registered with the cancel registry at HEAD; the audit's gateway-pulls-entitlements design needs inference-core as the single Model Plane puller; the ratchet in F-t3-067 is suppression-based, not hash-based; CI has effectively never run on the integration branch. These are listed per workstream under "Verified starting point".
7. **The first three pull requests** (section 7b): PR-1 land the live baseline; PR-2 make CI and the `buf breaking` gate real; PR-3 the run-context proto contract on `RunAgentRequest` (tag 18; about 17 literal sites, 2 exhaustive) with no behaviour change.

**What I need from you:** approve or adjust the plan, answer the ranked questions in section 7a (the first five unblock the most work), and reconcile the flagged cross-session items in section 5.

**Reading order.** 1 Goal and criteria -> 2 Decisions -> 3 Workstreams (3.0 index and finish-vs-build split, 3.1 Wave 1, 3.2 Wave 2, 3.3 Waves 3-5 epics) -> 4 Sequencing -> 5 Cross-session interfaces -> 6 Verification and regression -> 7 Open questions and first three PRs.

## 1. Goal, success criteria and scope

### 1.1 Goal

One web interface in which a user chats, writes and runs code, produces and edits files and images, researches with citations, and delegates multi-step work to an agent that uses a sandbox computer and a governed browser, at the capability level of manus.im, perplexity.ai, chatgpt.com and claude.ai. The differentiator is kept, not traded: tenant isolation, signed fail-closed tool authority, Zero Data Retention (ZDR) by construction, and honest outcomes (audit section 8: "protect it"). Everything lives in Model Plane plus the chat and agent pages of Verevon v3, with only the Control, Application, Ingestion and Data slices the workstreams name.

Method: Wave 1 (WS01-WS12) makes what exists trustworthy and provable; Wave 2 (WS13-WS27) closes the agent-loop and governance gaps. "Done" means the repo evidence rule of section 6.2: rebuilt images, a running stack and a verified end-to-end flow, never source plus a unit test alone.

### 1.2 Measurable success criteria

Numbers are taken from the designs. Where a design says "proposed" the number is a starting trigger that the first measuring slice replaces; the criterion is then "met against the recorded baseline". Every criterion has a named evidence artefact; none is accepted from code reading.

| # | Criterion (measurable) | WS | Verified by |
|---|---|---|---|

| C2 | Prompt cache measured and reported: Usage frame carries cache read/write legs; hit rate = `cache_read / input_tokens` shown per model/provider/day in Insights; providers without cache legs show "No cache activity reported"; two consecutive turns with a changed volatile block show `cache_read_input_tokens` > 0 over Stable+Context where today it is 0; owner target is set after the S9 A/B table (hit rate, write share, eval pass rate), then hit rate non-decreasing | WS02 S1, S2, S8, S9 | Insights capture; staging A/B table |

### 1.3 Explicitly out of scope

- Channel Plane: future/docs-only (root CLAUDE.md); no runtime built or built against (F-t3-089, DEC-40).
- User-desktop control and any Endpoint Plane work: computer use means the sandbox desktop only (DEC-22); "Chrome control" means the hosted governed browser (owner ruling 2026-09-28).
- Building an IDE or editor surface: file tools and the artifact panel only; no code-editing product.
- A second definition store: agent, skill, policy and instruction records keep one owner each (Convex `agents` for definitions; session-core `agent_skills`; capability-core policy). No parallel registry (audit F-t3-019 warning, M626).
- Anything outside Model Plane plus chat/agent pages, except the slices the designs name: Control (org-core policy, audit-core, auth-core step-up, user-core decisions), Application (notification-core, provisioner row 268), Ingestion (Quarry executor contract, integration-corev2 custody), Data (retrieval contracts only, never databases).
- Production release authorization. All evidence is source-verified or live-verified on the dev/proof stack; `CAPABILITY_PROMOTION_LEDGER.tsv` rows stay `source_only` until WS01 S12 produces a signed candidate (`MODEL_PLANE_STATUS.md:7-8`).
- Parked topics with no builder (DEC-40): share model, LLM reviewer on the permission path, Gemini/Vertex EU, wide-research gate, BYO provider metering, unattended browser, user-desktop control.
- Sibling-session work: Space features F-t1-* and Quarry F-t2-* are dependencies listed in section 5, not deliverables here.

### 1.4 Waves 3-5

W3 (research, creation, personalisation: deep/wide research, artifact canvas, decks, Agents and Automations hubs, takeover, goals, skills lifecycle, scoped memory, model record, egress tiers, microVM), W4 (computer-using platform: sandbox desktop, computer panel, MCP Apps, sharing, event triggers, replay, image tool, custom connectors, plugins, public Agent API, BYO providers) and W5 (backlog) are handled as epics, not decomposed. Each epic gets one paragraph of intent, the W1/W2 slices it depends on, and its DEC/ADR gates (DEC-22 S8 spike for microVM, DEC-40 parked list). An epic is promoted to a workstream design only after (a) the W2 exit gate of section 6.3 is green and (b) its blocking DEC is recorded. Seams that W3/W4 need are built in W1/W2 on purpose and kept narrow: `LoopFoldV1.extensions` (WS03), `ApprovalCause` additive enum (WS13), `OrgAgentPolicy` read contract (WS25), `RouteNote` (WS11/WS27), lease-bound guest executor (WS22 S7), `Authority::narrows` (WS03). No W3/W4 code lands before its seam has a golden test.

## 2. Decisions first

Compiled 2026-10-15 from "## 3. Decisions and open questions" and "## 6. Cross-session interfaces" of the 27 designs in `.plan-work/designs/`, audit sections 9.3 and 10.2 (`C:/dev/verevon-audit-2026-10-14/03-model-plane-chat-and-agents-audit.md`, L1679 and L1722), `.plan-work/common/rules-ledger-adrs.md`, and sections C and D of `common/overlap-space.md` and `common/overlap-quarry.md`. Condensed, not redesigned. Paths are as the designs give them; `[live-only]` = exists only in `C:/dev/CoresSystem` working tree, not at HEAD afb83928. MP = `apps/Model Plane`, CP = `apps/Control Plane`.

### 2.0 Vocabulary

- **DEC-nn**: global decision id used by every workstream section of this plan. WSnn Dn / Qn = the local id in the design (map in 2.3).
- **Decider** codes: OWN = owner ruling (the product/system owner; recorded in the owner-rulings file `apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/apps/verevon-web/plans/system-audits/program-2026-10-03/owner-rulings-and-defaults.md`, or as a dated ledger/ADR entry); MPO = Model Plane owner/integrator; CP = Control Plane team; APP = Application Plane team; PROD = product; CNS = counsel; OPS = operator/release; SPACE / QRY = sibling Verevon Space / Quarry-browser session.
- **OD / D10 / F12** are labels from `CHAT_GAP_CLOSURE_PLAN_2026-10-01.md` section 7 (OD1-OD20 at L417-L512). Each has a recommended default but, except where noted, no recorded ruling. Namespace caution: `program-2026-10-03/build-manifest.md` reuses OD4/OD7/OD8/OD10/OD11 for other questions; read OD numbers against the chat-gap plan only. CHATGAP L195 cites "OD5" for D10 go/no-go but D10 is OD9 (L457-459).
- **Owner rulings in force** (do not re-open): 2026-09-28 "scheduled runs are draft-only, personal connections are owner-only, Chrome control means the hosted governed browser, mail-to-task is Postmark self-forward" (CHATGAP L68; NOT in the ledger); 2026-10-03 rulings 1-6 and defaults D-A..D-E (ZDR switch gated on attested provider; waived runs skip memory indexing/dreaming/learning review; background budget alert-only); rule R17 "no admin lock". Ownership rules R1-R18 of `apps/master-ownership-matrix.md` section 3 bind every DEC (R9/R10 membership, R12 provider-write receipts, R17 waiver, R18 service-principal allowlist).
- **Ledger** = `apps/Model Plane/docs/decisions/ledger.md`. States: proposed, implemented, deferred, rejected, archived. Supersede with a new entry, never rewrite. "Blocks building?" means blocks starting/merging code, not enabling a flag.

### 2.1 Decision register

Recommendation text is the design's recommendation with its one-line reason. Where designs disagree, both are kept and marked [RECONCILE] with the settling DEC or section 2.6 row.

| DEC | Topic | Question | Options | Recommendation (reason) | Decider | Gates | Blocks building? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DEC-01 | Loop host | Which service hosts the durable Do/Plan loop (pause, resume, fold)? | (a) execution-core hosts the loop, session-core holds the fold; (b) Temporal workflow re-enters RunAgent | (a): Temporal drives ExecuteStep, has no RunAgent client, needs a fresh user bearer per activity it cannot persist, and its loop activity is not resume-safe. Temporal stays host for ExecuteStep-driven runs. New ledger entry; no existing entry covers the chat-loop host (Temporal refs at ledger :2943/:3119-3129/:4247 not reversed) | OWN (MPO) | WS03 S1 text, S6, S7 | Only WS03 S6/S7 merge (code can be written) |
| DEC-02 | OD8 renewable delegated bearer / credential window | How do runs outlive the ~300 s delegated bearer? (audit 9.3 #5, 10.2 Q3) | (a) wait for a renewable bearer; (b) user-present resume with click-time bearers, park instead of fail; (c) service-principal mint | (b): every WS03 trigger has a user present, so no bearer is stored; scheduled lane carries service credentials only and is bounded by a 240 s wall clock; browser mints lazily and parks for a refresh click (`browser_credential_expired`). Reject (c): widens execution-core authority and breaks delegated identity (never use the `approval:deliver` worker principal for user runs). OD8 itself stays open and gates only unattended auto-resume. CHATGAP OD8 rec: longer bearer (1500 s, cap 1800 s) for wide-research turns only, after security review | OWN (OD8) + CP security review | WS20 S9; WS21 unattended resume; WS03 none | No (only unattended auto-resume) |
| DEC-03 | Resume model and fold storage (OD6 core, overlap C1) | How does a run continue after an approval or input pause, and where is the fold stored? | Resume: (a) cold worker for everything; (b) loop-originated approvals consumed by the loop, cold worker only for ExecuteStep approvals and `tickets.create`/owner actions; same run_id with `segment_no`; re-enter via `RunAgentRequest.resume` vs extend `ResumeRun`. Fold: reuse `checkpoints` vs new `run_loop_checkpoints` | (b), same run_id new segment, `RunAgentRequest.resume` (`ResumeRun` stays the user-pause latch; `checkpoint_id` documented superseded). `continuation_mode` ("" cold, "segment", "loop"); single-use `ConsumeApproval`; reserved owner actions stay cold. New table `run_loop_checkpoints`: one row per run, latest-wins CAS, AEAD ciphertext, purged at terminalization; key = `SESSION_CORE_CONTINUATION_DESCRIPTOR_KEY` with distinct AAD domain `loopfold:` (separate key later if rotation diverges). Waived runs refuse fold persistence (see DEC-32). Settles overlap C1 as loop-level; Space must not add kinds to the cold worker | OWN (OD6 recorded as resolved) + MPO/security for key reuse | WS03 S2, S3, S4, S7; WS05 (MCP resume); WS13; WS14 S5 | No: S1 (draft), S4, S5 build dark; S2/S3 need acknowledgement |
| DEC-04 | Plan approval starts a new run (OD6 plan gate) | Does approving a plan resume the plan run or start execution? | (a) SPA sends an explicit execute turn with `approved_plan_id`: new Do run, same thread, durable grant + plan block; (b) server route starts the run; (c) same-run resume | (a): a plan run is read-only and WS03's rule is that authority narrows, never widens; (a) reuses the whole send path (run context, history, attachments, cost, cancel). (b) later for unattended (WS20). Amends audit wording "start the run on approval". CHATGAP OD6 rec: each effect still asks, manual "Fortsett planen" in v1 | OWN (OD6) | WS14 S2 | No (S2 only) |
| DEC-05 | ask_user: question store and delivery (overlap C2) | Is a question an approval kind or its own record? Pause in place or end turn? | (a) approvals table + new `ApprovalKind`; (b) own `run_questions` table sharing the pause machinery. Delivery: `next_turn` vs `resume` | (b): approvals are authority grants (cold worker delivers `granted` rows, gateway quarantine keys on granted status), an answer must never read as a grant. One schema, two delivery values: `next_turn` ships first (needs no WS03; equals Space `request_input`), `resume` via `/continue` in S5. ZDR: tool never offered, routes refuse. ### 2.5 ADR filing

**Convention (observed, no README or template exists in the repo; a decision is still needed on which series is canonical, rules digest 3b):**
- Series A, cross-plane product decisions: file `apps/<TOPIC>_ADR_<YYYY-MM-DD>.md`; heading `# ADR-NNNN: <title>`; fields `**Date**`, `**Status**`, `**Deciders**`, optional `**Scope**`, `**Supersedes / extends**`; sections `## Context`, `## Decision`, `## Alternatives considered` (one `###` per option), `## Consequences` (`### Positive`, `### Negative`, `### Risks and release gates`). Sequence is shared: ADR-0001..0004 exist (`SPACE_AUTHORITY`, `CROSS_SPACE_AGENT_REGISTRY`, `AUTHORED_INSTRUCTIONS` = ADR-0003, `GRAPHITI_ADOPTION`); next free is ADR-0005 (inferred). WS06 D9 and WS10 D1 already chose Series A.
- Model Plane internal tradeoffs: append a new entry at the top of `apps/Model Plane/docs/decisions/ledger.md` (states proposed, implemented, deferred, rejected, archived; supersede, never rewrite; check it first per MPCLAUDE L26). Root-cause write-ups: `apps/Model Plane/docs/postmortem/NNNN-<slug>.md` (next 0003).
- Series B (`docs/architecture/adr-<kebab>.md`, no number) and learning-plane `ADR-001` are not used. `docs/adr/` per plane is only planned (Studio Plane README L177).
- Numbers below are proposals; the integrator (DEC-39) assigns them serially in the order drafts merge. Use Series A when the decision binds another plane or a sibling session; a ledger entry when it is Model-internal.

| Proposed id / location | Title | DEC | Drafter | Notes |

## 3. Workstreams

### 3.1 Waves 1 and 2: workstream sections (WS01-WS27)

Sections below are in audit-wave order: WS01-WS12 are Wave 1 ("make what exists trustworthy"), WS13-WS27 are Wave 2 ("close the agent-loop and governance gaps"). The finish-vs-build split is the `[FINISH]`/`[BUILD]` tag on every slice and the index above. WS01-S0 (land the live baseline) precedes everything; see section 4.1. **Read Appendix B (binding review amendments) alongside every workstream section: where a row there conflicts with the section text, the row wins.**

Plan section 3 (workstreams, Wave 1, part a): WS01 Ops closure, WS02 Run context and cache, WS03 Durable loop, WS04 Replica-safe run control. Compiled from `.plan-work/designs/WS01..WS04-*.md`; decisions cite DEC ids of `plan-parts/02-decisions.md`; audit = `C:/dev/verevon-audit-2026-10-14/03-model-plane-chat-and-agents-audit.md`.
Conventions: WT = `C:/dev/CoresSystem/.claude/worktrees/epic-shaw-4d613b` (HEAD afb83928); LIVE = `C:/dev/CoresSystem` (+~324 uncommitted entries); MP = `apps/Model Plane`, CP = `apps/Control Plane`, FE = `apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3`, RS = `MP/rust/services`. `[live-only]` = exists only in LIVE; `[RECONCILE]` = designs disagree, DEC/2.6 row settles it. Nothing here was built or deployed by the designers; every DoD requires rebuilt images, a running stack and a verified end-to-end flow.
Order: WS01 S0 lands the LIVE ZDR/session-core wave first (DEC-39, 2.6 row 20); WS02, WS03, WS04 branch from it. Proto tags and migration numbers are reserved by the integrator (DEC-39, 2.6 rows 1-2); never take session-core 0044/0045; next free is 0046.

**Resolves:** F-t3-004 Phase 0, F-t3-005, F-t3-039 | **Owner:** Model Plane (session-core publisher, inference-core attestation, release tooling) + Control Plane (audit-core, auth-core) + operator | **Wave:** W1 | **Type:** per-slice mix: [FINISH] S0-S3, S6, S7, S12-S14; [BUILD] S4, S5, S8-S11 | **Effort:** XL, ~45 engineer-days (critical path S0, S6, S7, S12, S13 = 20-25 working days; audit track S1, S2, S3 ~5 days parallel; release track S8-S11 parallel with two engineers) | **Depends on:** none (WS01 is the root; S0 first) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-33 (WS01 D5, D6, D7, D13, D14) gates S5, S7, S12, S13, S14; DEC-34 (D1, D2, D3, D8-D12; D4 local) gates S1 (file location only), S4, S9-S12. Free to start: S0, S1 (with option A), S2, S3, S6, S7, S8; S5 and S13 need DEC-33 recorded.

- Publisher fix b9ed9197 is an ancestor of HEAD but not of running model-gateway rev 2f2c3e83 (2026-09-30); fix at `RS/session-core/src/audit_publisher.rs:135-180`, counters/gauges `:432-489, :740-770`, requeue fn `MP/rust/services/session-core/migrations/0043_audit_outbox_requeue.sql:28-75` (path as design gives: `migrations/0043_audit_outbox_requeue.sql`). - Prometheus (`controlplane-prometheus`, CP/docker-compose.yml:1067-1100) has no `rule_files`; no Model Plane scrape (session-core :8081, model-gateway :8080); no Alertmanager. auth-core outbox bug (postmortem row 269): `CP/auth-core/src/auth/organization-events.plugin.ts:828-829` (`?.toISOString()` on a non-Date, root cause undetermined), claim query `:732-763` has no backoff. First candidate needs a baseline artifact built non-release (`:1537, 1578, 1593-1600`). sqlx refuses unknown applied migrations (`session-core/src/store.rs:33-36`).
- Gate `scripts/coresystem-conformance.sh:63-68, 106-121, 123-144` can never read ready on a dirty tree or with 28 `source_only` ledger rows; thresholds TSV `MP/docs/CORESYSTEM_ACCEPTANCE_THRESHOLDS.tsv:7-15` all `proposed`. No CI runs `release-*.sh`, nats-provisioner or audit-core tests, or promtool.

Hot spots: `session-core/src/{grpc,terminalization,auth,main}.rs`, `sessions.proto` (WS01 S0 first, then only `store.rs`); `model-gateway/src/{sse,tool_loop,retrieval}.rs` (S6 first, thin); `inference-core/src/{config,provider/*}` (S5 first); compose files (S1 alias, S8 overlay first); `release-artifact.sh` WS01 sole editor.

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR: audit rows are content-free (`tool_action_final.json`: tool, data_category, zdr, ids); replay read must show 0 rows with `details.zdr = true`; a ZDR run writes no audit row; lapse never grants ZDR, refusal stays `zdr_unavailable`; waiver minting OFF; snapshots synthetic, logs names/counts only.
- Strengths touched (audit section 8): ZDR by construction (`zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`, `zdr-contract-schema.test.mjs`, refusal inventory, S5/S7 tests, `zdr_residual_content_bytes == 0`, live proof pass 1/2); honest outcomes (`session_core_replay_test.go`, `audit_publisher.rs` fixtures); multi-tenant outboxes (S9 lint + rehearsal, `manifest_contract_test.go`); signed fail-closed gate (`release-contract-test.sh`, S8 overlay assertion); credential isolation (`release-input-security-test.sh`).

**Top risks**
1. Operator-state unknowns (what is deployed, keys, re-arm row) -> every slice opens with a dated read-only evidence read; nothing claimed from code alone.
2. Landing the 324-entry LIVE wave entangled with other sessions -> S0 first, hunk-level staging, per-group gates, never reset/clean LIVE.
3. Wrong deploys (dirty-tree builds, order violations) and false assurance from the gate -> clean detached worktree builds, `rebuild-service.sh` rollback tags, BUILD_PASS order, S8/D10 `runtime` profile, document that `runtime` ready is not promotion.

### WS02 - Run context for Do/Plan/scheduled runs and prompt-cache discipline
**Resolves:** F-t3-001, F-t3-022 | **Owner:** Model Plane (model-gateway composes; execution-core consumes; inference-core provider wire; cost-core ledger/view; session-core run record; FE display only) | **Wave:** W1 | **Type:** per-slice mix: [FINISH] S1, S2, S8; [BUILD] S3-S7, S9-S12 | **Effort:** XL, ~34-50 engineer-days over 11 scheduled slices (4-5 calendar weeks with two engineers plus S8 window; critical path WS01 S0, S4-S7 = 17-25 d) | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline) for S1, S3, S4, S6, S7; S5 none; S9 needs S2 data | **Gating decisions:** DEC-18 (WS02 D1, billing) gates S3 enablement only; DEC-28 (D3) none; DEC-06 (D4) S6 Space behaviour; DEC-32 (D5) S6 `full` for waived; DEC-39 (D8) merge order for S3, S4, S9, S10; local D2, D6, D7, D9, D10. Free: S1-S5 build, S9, S11 (S6/S7 build; S12 unscheduled).

**Verified starting point**
- Ask composes ADR-0003 instructions, history, memory (`RS/model-gateway/src/sse.rs:6243-6296`, `:4677-4776`, `:5219-5425`, `:5060-5138`); the agentic branch returns before any of it (`sse.rs:1381-1446`, `:1423`; loaders start `:1491`). `build_run_agent_request` (`sse.rs:8758-8835`) sends goal only; `RunAgentRequest` tags 1-17 used, next free 18 (`MP/proto/model_plane/v1/execution.proto:76-209`). BFF already injects `org_instructions`, `space_instructions`, `persona_instructions`, `agent_*` (`FE/apps/gateway/src/domains/ag_ui.rs:83-124`) and model-gateway drops them for Do.
- `run_rounds` seeds `[system preamble, user goal]` then inserts skills at index 1 (`RS/execution-core/src/runtime_loop/agent.rs:1168-1221`); reentrant for subagents (`:3100-3152`); `InferRequest` literal `:1331-1381` with `thinking_budget_tokens: 0`; executor boundary `execution-core/src/grpc.rs:1203-1250` (check at `:1237`); `goal` logged at INFO `agent.rs:866-878`.
- Audit stale: "measurement plumbing built end to end" holds for gateway turns only: the only USAGE_ENVELOPE producer is model-gateway; cost-core accepts only `producer == "model-gateway"` (`go/services/cost-core/cmd/main.go:54, :501`); Do rounds only log tokens, agentic Usage frame hard-coded 0 (`sse.rs:9706-9734`). Cache metric is hidden when 0, not zero (`FE src/features/chat/components/ChatPanels.tsx:928-937`). Compaction markers name `reattach_context`, which the Do loop lacks. Thread id never changes across compaction, so lineage root = thread. `go/go.work` exists (build cost-core per module).
- Anthropic joins all system messages into one string (`inference-core/src/provider/anthropic.rs:180, :284-376, :667-728`), so reorder is a no-op there; stable-prefix order exists but is off and effective only for turns pinned to OpenAI/Azure (`prompt_context.rs:23-26, :59-86, :305-348`). No `prompt_cache_key` anywhere.
- [live-only] anchors shift: `sse.rs` (+883 lines, agentic return `:1491` [live], authored fn `:6375` [live]), `zdr_features.rs` (refuses agentic/skills on ZDR, `:305-306`), `zdr_posture.rs`, `sse_events.rs` (Usage `retention_class`), `execution-core/{agent,grpc,agent_route}.rs`, `sessions.proto` (+42), `docs/sse-event-taxonomy.md` (at HEAD `gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check` fails). WS02 needs no session-core migration (JSONB merge); cost-core needs one in its own numbering.

**Slices**
Common commands: `cd "apps/Model Plane/rust" && cargo fmt --all -- --check && cargo test -p <crate> --lib`; `cargo test -p model-gateway --test e2e_invoke_chain_test`; `cd "apps/Model Plane/go/services/cost-core" && go test ./...`; `buf lint "apps/Model Plane/proto"` and `buf breaking "apps/Model Plane/proto" --against "apps/Model Plane/proto/baseline.binpb"` (`buf breaking` is vacuous until WS12-S2 commits a non-empty `baseline.binpb` (it is 0 bytes at HEAD); until then prove additivity by diffing tag numbers against `git show HEAD:<proto file>` and attach the diff; never record `buf breaking` as passing); `python3 "apps/Model Plane/scripts/gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py" --check`; `cd "apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3" && pnpm vitest run <files>`. After proto edits regenerate Go (`buf generate` via buf image) and force Rust proto rebuild (`mp-contracts/build.rs:74-94`). **Provides / consumes**
- Provides: WS03 `RunAgentRequest.run_context = 18`, reusable `run_context::compose`, `seed_messages` callable from a resume path, fold messages must be scrubbed (`scrub_checkpoint_test.rs`). WS20 `RunContext` + composer; scheduled lane must load its own signed instruction snapshot and set fields 5+. WS27 `ChatEvent::Usage.cache_*` (S1), `RunAgentResponse.usage` tag 9 (S3), `cost_entries.run_id` for Do. WS07 same memory helper (request: typed skip reason for `SKIPPED_BY_POLICY(memory_off)`). WS08 `RunContext` fields 5+ [RECONCILE] with WS08 dedicated `RunAgentRequest` field: DEC-39/2.6 row 1 (integrator assigns; attachments not gated by WS02 flag). WS11 `InferRequest` tag 17. WS15 `runs.metadata.run_context`. WS17 `list_conversation` behind `run_context::load_history`. WS24/WS25 `reattach_context` for Do and tier vocabulary.
- Consumes: WS01-S0 (live wave, staging deploy path); WS05/WS24 read-only class for `reattach_context`; WS07 memory mode; WS26 decision on Do-goal PII redaction (`pii_redaction_required` computed `sse.rs:963` but agentic path passes `req.content` unredacted); Control billing attribution (DEC-18); Space session audience predicate (DEC-06) and Space composer sending `agentic`.
- Hot spots: `sse.rs` (WS01, WS03, WS04, WS27: new modules `run_context.rs`, `cache_telemetry.rs`; S1/S6 merge before WS27); `execution.proto` (WS02 S3/S4 first); `inference.proto` (17 WS11, 18/19 WS02); `sessions.proto` (WS01 first, WS02 appends one RPC); `agent.rs` (WS02 S7 touches seed/literal/response sites; WS03 owns checkpoint/resume).

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR: agentic ZDR is refused before this code (`sse.rs:976-994`; `zdr_features.rs:305-306` [live-only]); `validate` refuses ZDR runs with history or memory (instructions allowed); defensive `compose` returns `SKIPPED_BY_POLICY(zdr)`; run record stores enums and counts only, text never logged; ZDR sends no `cache_control` (`anthropic.rs:287`), no `prompt_cache_key`; ZDR envelopes never persisted (`cmd/main.go:492`); waived runs persist like Ask (DEC-32).
- Strengths touched: two loops separate (`cross_service_loop_contract.rs`, `compaction_parity_contract.rs`, new `prompt_tier_contract.rs`; no gateway import in execution-core); ZDR (`run_context_posture.rs`, S9/S10 ZDR goldens, `cache.rs:245`, `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`); context management (`leading_system_messages_are_never_compacted_by_budget`, eval cases 13-14); honest outcomes (UI "No cache activity reported", Do cost stays `None` until WS27 prices); typed ChatEvent taxonomy (`gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check`); memory provenance (`memory_provenance.rs`).

**Top risks**
1. Heavier Do prompts raise per-round cost before cache levers land; subscription route re-sends uncached -> flag default off, `instructions` first, pinned route instructions-only, S2/S3 baseline before `full`.
2. Enabling S3 starts billing Do runs (cost-core `cost_billing_outbox`) -> flag off until DEC-18, deploy executor then gateway, idempotency key per dispatch.
3. Merge pressure on LIVE-modified `sse.rs`, `agent.rs`, protos, plus authority drift/context leakage (Space-origin memory, instruction text mistaken for authority) -> WS01-S0 first, new modules, add-only protos with `buf breaking`, memory skipped on Space runs, test that instruction text cannot change `mode`/rung.

### WS03 - Durable agent loop core (pause, resume, fold, park)
**Resolves:** F-t3-002 (core slice; `ask_user` itself is WS14) | **Owner:** Model Plane: session-core (fold store, approval consume, park rows, recovery worker, ledger), execution-core (loop owner: fold writer, resume branch, closers, released-call gate), model-gateway (`/continue` route, seed brief, `RunAgentRequest.resume`), FE gateway/BFF + SPA (relay route, client method, action contract only) | **Wave:** W1 | **Type:** per-slice mix: [BUILD] S1, S2, S4-S9; S3 = [FINISH]+[BUILD] (replaces the dead-end `resume_durable_approval_continuation` and the gateway quarantine) | **Effort:** XL, ~63 engineer-days (S1 3, S2 5, S3 10, S4 7, S5 6, S6 8, S7 12, S8 7, S9 5; two engineers: stage 1 in ~3 weeks, all in ~8-9) | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline) before S3 and every edit of `agent.rs`/`grpc.rs`/`sessions.proto`; WS02 field/builder ordering (S3); WS05 classes (S5, stub first); WS04 for cross-replica cancel (S7 risk) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-01 (WS03 D1, loop host) gates S1 text, S6, S7 merge (code can be written); DEC-03 (D3 fold store, D4 cold worker/continuation mode, D5 same-run segment, D6 `RunAgentRequest.resume`) gates S2, S3, S4, S7; DEC-02 (D2) gates none of S1-S9 (removes unattended auto-resume from scope); DEC-32 (D7) S4 default refuse so nothing waits; DEC-06 (D8) S3 typed refusal ships. Can start with no ruling: S1 (draft), S4, S5 (dark behind flags, additive migrations); S2/S3 need DEC-03 and DEC-02 acknowledged.

**Verified starting point**
- The fold is a local `Vec<ChatMessage>` in `MP/rust/services/execution-core/src/runtime_loop/agent.rs:1168-1197` with locals at `:1224-1245`; tool results are a framed user message (`format_tool_context`, `:2120-2125`), so a dangling call has no history marker; round structure `:1255-2125`; pause `pause_for_approval` `:4469-4545` (idempotency key `{run_id}:{step_id}` `:4502`; descriptor refuses ZDR `:4558-4562`).
- No gate path releases a granted call in the loop: `execute_step_inner` (`runtime_loop/mod.rs:454-561`) returns `awaiting_approval` for capability Ask (`:506-509`), hook Ask (`:548`), permission AwaitApproval (`:559`); `resume_run` approval branch always returns unavailable (`execution-core/src/grpc.rs:691-698`, `:1068-1132`); gateway `decide_approval` (`model-gateway/src/http_routes.rs:1493-1582`) ends in `quarantine_granted_approval_continuation` (`approvals.rs:254-277`) which always errors; cold worker (`approval_delivery_worker.rs:1-130`) re-executes only `execute_provider_action`, `book_shipment`, `tickets.create` and every grant inserts an outbox row (`orchestration_store.rs:797-806, 835-841`): running worker and in-loop resume together would double-execute.
- Substrate: `checkpoints` table (`session-core/migrations/0001_init.sql:46-53`) is plaintext BYTEA, no reader, `SaveCheckpoint` has no ZDR check (`session-core/src/grpc.rs:4701-4777`); AEAD pattern `continuation_crypto.rs:14-107` (key `SESSION_CORE_CONTINUATION_DESCRIPTOR_KEY`); effect-ledger primitives `session_tool_audit_intents` (`migrations/0013_tool_audit_intents.sql`) with `ReserveToolAction`/`FinalizeToolAction` called only by the gateway loop; recovery worker `terminalization.rs:1601-1643` terminalizes after 15 min and spares runs with requested/granted approvals (`:1617-1623`).
- Temporal drives ExecuteStep only, never RunAgent, holds no bearer, its loop activity is "NOT resume-safe" (`orchestrator-core/cmd/workflows/step_loop.go:62-84`, `interactive_run.go:28-36, 128-149`, `cmd/activities/activities.go:610-620`). RunAgent needs five user-bound bearers minted per request by the BFF (`sse.rs:8296-8346`; `FE/apps/gateway/src/domains/chat/shared.rs:630-720`); none persisted.

**Provides / consumes**
- Provides: WS13 `ConsumeApproval` single-use release, `ApprovedRelease` gate (only way a granted call passes `execute_step_inner`), `continuation_mode`, `/continue`; WS14 `PendingPause{kind: input}`, reserved `/continue` field `answer`, ask_user budget in `extensions`; WS15 `OrchestrationEvent.RunParked=24`, `RunResumed=25`, `RunDetail.resumable=17`, `park_reason=18`, raw `run_parked` frame; WS16 content-free park/pause triggers; WS20 parked/claim contract (unattended resume needs OD8 or a Control reauthorizer, not built, DEC-02); WS22 lease not released on pause (`agent.rs:929-932`), `WorkspaceStateLost`; WS25 `LoopFoldV1.extensions`; WS06 new outcomes `not_run`/`unknown_outcome` and events `CHECKPOINT_SAVED/RESTORED`, `RunResumed` in the chain schema; WS27 per-segment usage under same `run_id`.
- Consumes: WS02 shared context resolver and tag/literal ordering (27 `RunAgentRequest` sites; fold messages from `RunContext` must pass `scrub_checkpoint`; `seed_messages` callable from resume path); WS05 declared effect classes replace the stub, no exactly-once for MCP; WS04 shared cancel/queue state (execution-core `StateStore` is per-replica memory) and `/continue` registering with the cancel registry; WS01 rebuild harness; WS12 CI gates for the FE action contract.
- Hot spots: `execution.proto` (WS02 first, then WS03); `agent.rs`/`grpc.rs`/`mod.rs` (land live set first; S6 LoopState refactor merges as one early PR); `sse.rs`/`http_routes.rs` (WS02 first); `sessions.proto`/`orchestration.proto` append-only, integrator numbers; `terminalization.rs` (S8 owns park-vs-terminalize); `FE streams.rs`/`chat.rs`/`orchestration-client.ts` (WS03 first, cards consume).
- Cross-session: Space: `/continue` for personal Do runs only; Space runs refused `continue_space_scope_unsupported` until Space delivers `space_scope_for_thread(thread_id)` (DEC-06); Space must not add resumable kinds to the cold worker (2.6 row 3). Quarry: `browser_agent` never resumed (`NotResumableTool`); durable browser grants and `human_assist` are QRY/WS21.

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR: every new store/route refuses ZDR and waived runs (DEC-32 rule: waived treated as ZDR for fold persistence); `consume_approval` and `/continue` refuse with feature `approval_continuation`, fold with `agent_run`; ZDR and waived runs never park (they fail with existing typed refusals); fold is ciphertext only, purged at terminalization, GDPR erasure and thread delete; ledger rows carry tool/class/digest only; no bearer persisted. [RECONCILE] WS02 D5 (waived persistent like Ask) vs WS03 D7 (no fold for waived): DEC-32.
- Gates: signed capability gate (release-gate unit tests, `autonomy_ladder_contract.rs`); autonomy ladder (`Authority::narrows` test, `check_autonomy_rung` `agent.rs:1685-1706`); two loops separate (`durable_loop_contract.rs`, `cross_service_loop_contract.rs`); ZDR (`loop_fold_zdr_matrix.rs`, `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`); honest outcomes (closer fixtures, terminalization receipt tests); credential isolation (`scrub_checkpoint_test.rs`, `assert_no_credentials`); taxonomy (`gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check`); multi-tenant (DB-level claim race test); context management (`compaction_parity_contract.rs`).

**Top risks**
1. Hot-file conflicts with large uncommitted work (`agent.rs`, `terminalization.rs`, `sessions.proto`, `sse.rs`) -> WS01-S0 first, early LoopState refactor PR, add-only proto, integrator-assigned numbers.
2. Double execution (cold worker plus in-loop release, or replay after crash) and rollback stranding paused runs -> `continuation_mode` + outbox skip, single-use consume, ledger + closers, MCP/browser excluded from exactly-once; `continue_disabled` kill switch, park/approval TTL terminalizes as `outcome_unknown`, flip-forward-only after dated proof.
3. Fold correctness/size and authority drift on resume -> message-equality test, in-fold compaction, stage-1 fallback on any restore failure, narrow-only authority, bearers minted per click and never stored, S8 parks at the credential margin.

### WS04 - Replica-safe run control (cancel, queue, resume, grants, receipts)
**Resolves:** F-t3-016 (dependency of F-t3-002/WS03, 011/WS14, 012/WS17, 015/WS16, 064) | **Owner:** Model Plane: model-gateway (hot-path cancel, queue, resume liveness, turn record), session-core (durable grants, message receipts); Frontend Plane only for queue strip, send-id mint, BFF pass-through; Control Plane unchanged | **Wave:** W1 | **Type:** all [BUILD] except S9 [FINISH]; S8 = [BUILD, decision-gated] | **Effort:** L, ~30-41 engineer-days without S8 (core S1-S3 + S5 = 11-15 d fits audit "M"; critical path S1, S2, S3, S9 = 12-15 d; S4/S5/S6 need only the live session-core wave) | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline) for S2 onward (live `zdr_posture.rs` gate must be committed before S3 carries queue text); WS01 for NATS ACL deploy and replica count; WS03 for loop-level cancel observation; WS17 for S8 | **Gating decisions:** DEC-07 (WS04 D1 NATS request/reply, D2 down-behaviour 503, D3 persist-before-deliver ledger correction, D4 editable queue) gates S1-S3, S6, S8, S9 (ops sign-off on ACL change via WS01 for S1); DEC-08 (D5 plan-grant carrier = session-core columns) gates S4; D6, D7, D8 local. Free: S1-S5, S7, S9 (S1 needs ACL sign-off); S6 reorder needs DEC-07; S8 not scheduled.

**Verified starting point**
- Everything run-control is per-process in model-gateway (`MG` = `MP/rust/services/model-gateway/src`): `cancel_registry.rs:47-133` (route `http_routes.rs:140`, handler `:6873`), `queued_input.rs:121-226` (handler `http_routes.rs:7308`, drain `tool_loop.rs:6528`), `chat_turn_registry.rs:45,175,186,255`, `idempotency_registry.rs:161`, plan grants `coordinator.rs:69-208` (writer `:327` via `plan_approval` `http_routes.rs:7041`; readers `sse.rs:8491, 8663`). A Stop or queue on the wrong replica answers 404; the SPA has already aborted, so the producer runs to the end (`sse.rs:6477`); queue 404 becomes `resend_as_new_turn` and duplicates work. A plan approval on A leaves the next Do run on B with rung UNSPECIFIED (`sse.rs:8663`, fail-open for the graded ladder, `ledger.md:2611`).
- Audit premise wrong: session-core has no message dedupe. `append_message_in_tx` (`session-core/src/grpc.rs:2430-2670`) inserts unconditionally (`:2569`); events idempotency (`0002_events_and_ordinals.sql:35-38`) covers events not messages; `AppendMessageRequest.idempotency_key = 16` (`MP/proto/model_plane/v1/sessions.proto:303`) is a Control Space-decision field. The SPA never sends `idempotency_key` (`FE/src/shared/api/chat-client.ts:1090-1135`), so the idempotency registry is dormant for the product.
- Code vs ledger contradiction: ledger 2026-08-24 (`MP/docs/decisions/ledger.md:1663-1666`) says persist before deliver; handler enqueues first then persists (`http_routes.rs:~7351`, `session_flow.rs:1602`).
- ZDR turns never buffer, claim or register (`sse.rs:976, 1069, 1152, 1180, 1978`; `MP/docs/ZDR.md:96`); stream buffer already Redis-backed with 250 ms breaker (`stream_buffer.rs:9-14, 288, 528`). NATS ACL `MP/deploy/nats.conf:14-35` allows no runctl subject (ships in artifact, `release-artifact.sh:92`); async-nats 0.49.1 and redis 0.27.6 already in `MP/rust/Cargo.toml:73,79`; no `replicas` key under `MP/deploy`.
- `agentic_run_stream` (`sse.rs:9167-9800`) registers nothing with the cancel/queue registries; execution-core loop never reads the cancel latch (only `state.rs:197` sets it). [RECONCILE] with WS03 section 5 assumption that `/continue` registers: WS04 does not own this; WS03 must register and make the loop observe cancel.
- [live-only]: `MG/zdr_posture.rs` (`DURABLE_MUTATIONS` includes `invoke_queue_input`, `plan_approval`, `cancel_run`, `L:zdr_posture.rs:825-840`); `http_routes.rs` (+190), `sse.rs` (+814), `tool_loop.rs` (+370), `chat_turn_registry.rs`, `session-core/src/grpc.rs` (+817), `sessions.proto` (+42), migrations 0044/0045; FE `chat-client.ts`, `ChatMessages.tsx`, `use-chat-controller.ts` modified. Symbols shift (`invoke_cancel` 6873->6979, `invoke_queue_input` 7308->7416, `cancel_run` 1602->1606, register 1978->2088, drain 6528->6635).
- `PreviousTurnRegistry` and `global()` exist at HEAD (the sweep said live-only: wrong). TeamWorkerStore, ApprovalStore, `artifact_versions`, `tool_results`, `doc_ready` stay per-replica (out of scope).

**Provides / consumes**
- Provides: WS03 `RunControl::attach(request_id, run_id, org, user)` and run-keyed subject `mp.v1.runctl.run.{run_id}.cancel` so `/continue` segments are cancellable once WS03 registers `agentic_run_stream`; `ThreadAutonomy.GetThreadGrant` for resume seeding. WS14 durable thread grant (`SetRunModeRequest.approved_plan_id = 6`, `GetThreadGrant`), `queued_input.ids` + `client_message_id` receipts for the docked tray, correct `was_in_plan_mode` (WS14 owns approve-then-continue). WS17 `AppendMessageRequest.client_message_id = 18`, `AppendMessageResponse.message_id = 2`, `deduplicated = 3`, unique `(thread_id, client_message_id)`; S8 needs WS17 versions. WS16 liveness probe, undelivered-queue metric, shared turn record. WS15 additive `queued_input.ids`. WS01 ACL change, compose env, `docker-compose.replicas.yml`, deploy order ACL -> gateway -> flag. Integrator: migration numbers for `<next>_thread_autonomy_grant`, `<next>_message_client_id`; proto numbers 6 / 18 / 2-3 reserved here.
- Consumes: live ZDR wave (`zdr_posture.rs` before S3; 0044/0045 numbering); WS01 rebuilt images, running stack, ACL deploy; WS03 loop-level cancel observation and agentic stream registration; WS17 message versions; WS02 composer edits to `sse.rs` land first.
- Hot spots: `MG/sse.rs`, `http_routes.rs` (live set first, WS02 second; WS04 single-line call-ins and one early refactor PR for S7); `MG/state.rs`/`lib.rs` (S1 first, one field); `sessions.proto`/`session-core/src/grpc.rs` (append-only); `deploy/nats.conf` and compose (WS01 owns deploy ordering); FE `chat-client.ts`, `use-chat-controller.ts`, `ChatMessages.tsx` (live set first; S6 touches send path and strip only). Cross-session: Space member steering/cancel (F-t1-040, C10) comes after shared cancel/queue; bus will accept an `authorized_actor` once Space ships the "run steer" decision (DEC-07); Quarry browser runs keep their own control path (`MG/browser_run.rs:725`); Dragonfly capacity shared (cache-mode, 256 MB, `docker-compose.yml:117-118`), grant-store decision must not assume Dragonfly durability.

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR: ZDR turns never register, buffer or claim, so Stop for a ZDR request id is a 404 and nothing persists; queue text only after `require_non_zdr_durable_mutation` (refuses ZDR and waived tokens); plan grant cannot originate from a ZDR turn (stores rung label, justification already persisted, plan id; erased with thread); client message id omitted by SPA for ZDR and never stored for them; shared Redis keys identity-scoped, ids only, never bearers or content.
- Gates: ZDR (`zdr_turn_registers_no_run_control_listener`, `queue_bus_message_is_never_built_for_zdr_or_waived_claims`, live inventory test `every_durable_mutation_refuses_a_zdr_or_waived_token_before_it_runs`, matrix rows `e_zdr_token_queue_input_refused`, `e_zdr_token_plan_grant_refused`, `zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`); two loops separate (`tool_loop.rs:6528` still drains the local queue; `cross_service_loop_contract.rs` unchanged); honest outcomes (404/peer/503 distinct, `cancel_run_logs_one_outcome_line_per_call` `http_routes.rs:10725` extended); typed taxonomy (`gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check`); multi-tenant/resume across replicas (every shared key identity-scoped like `scoped_stream_key`); credential isolation (test that `RunControlMsg` has no credential field).

**Top risks**
1. ACL/ops drift (nats.conf ships with the release artifact; flag on before ACL = silent permission violations) -> boot self-test falls back to off and `/readyz` degraded, ACL contract test, deploy order in S9 evidence.
2. Hot-file conflicts (`sse.rs`, `http_routes.rs`, protos) and scope growth from the false dedupe premise -> live set first, single-line call-ins, S6/S7 separately schedulable, re-estimate F-t3-016 as L/XL when S4/S6/S7 included (WS04 D6).
3. Core NATS is at-most-once with unverified no-responders semantics; cache-mode Dragonfly eviction for S5/S7 -> ignored live test in S1 before S2, explicit 503s, one safe retry via `op_id`, ids-only best-effort Redis, authority-bearing state (grants) in Postgres.

Conventions: migration and proto-tag numbers named below are purpose names or "next free at HEAD"; the integrator assigns real numbers (DEC-39, section 2.6 rows 1-2: take 0046+ in session-core, never 0044/0045, no third capability-core duplicate prefix, add-only protos, never renumber). Preserve CRLF where the worktree file is CRLF. Go migrations re-run every boot, so they must be idempotent.

### WS05 - Declared tool classes and MCP through the signed gate
**Resolves:** F-t3-003 | **Owner:** Model Plane: capability-core (Go; registry + policy engine, system of record), execution-core (Rust; gate, permission, MCP dispatch), model-gateway (MCP discovery + transport only), `mp-contracts`; Frontend Plane BFF route set + action contracts only | **Wave:** W1 | **Type:** S0 FINISH, S1 BUILD, S2 BUILD, S3 BUILD, S4 FINISH, S5 BUILD, S6 FINISH | **Effort:** L, upper end, 31-44 d (S0 1-2, S1 4-6, S2 6-8, S3 6-8, S4 7-10, S5 5-7, S6 2-3); critical path S0-S1-S3-S4-S6 about 25 d with S2/S5 on a second engineer; becomes XL only if resumable MCP approvals or Ask-mode MCP are pulled in | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline) before S4; WS03 (`waived: bool` signature in `execute_step*`) before S3/S4; WS02 (`merged_tool_defs` order/size); WS04 (replica-safe MCP hydration, supersedes D4(a)); WS06 audit emitter later (today `registry_audit_log` only) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-12 (WS05 D1-D5): S1 and S3 proceed with no decision; read-class MCP in S2/S4 proceeds; S2 needs the security nod on the HMAC observation-write route and the owner call on `annotation_trust` (ships default untrusted meanwhile); S4 `MCP_DO_TOOLS=all` blocked on resumable MCP approval (WS03/WS13); S4 e2e reliability needs the cold-cache fix (D4). DEC-08 (WS05 D6 per map): S5 relax semantics (`allow` only for `mcp_tool`, once-only classes never relaxed); S1-S4 do not need it. DEC-10: flag flips (`MCP_DO_TOOLS`, `TOOL_CLASS_GATE=enforce`) ship dark. DEC-39: proto tags and migration numbers.

**Verified starting point**
- Risk is guessed by name substring: `rust/services/execution-core/src/permission/mod.rs:229-285` (every `mcp__` name risky at 280-282); `is_risky_call` 195-200; rung from the same classifier (`rung_required_for` 100-108, `check_autonomy_rung` 124-143), not from `risk_level`. Never-auto is two predicates (`requires_durable_owner_approval` 77-79 `tickets.create`, `requires_consent_to_disclose` 160-162 `read_subagent_result`), not one. Six call sites: `permission/mod.rs:43-70,100-108`, `runtime_loop/agent.rs:1639-1661,1685-1706,1800-1822`, `runtime_loop/retry.rs:150`.
- `mcp__` dynamic names have no binding, so every call is refused before the permission gate (`capability_policy.rs:836-838,361-362`; `runtime_loop/mod.rs:488-539`). The signed decision binds (capability id, version, org, run, agent, scope, decision) but not tool, class or user (`go/services/capability-core/internal/server/decision_proof.go:31-44`; Rust verifier `capability_policy.rs:461-537`). `EvaluatePolicyRequest` has no tool name or user id (`capabilities.proto` fields 1-5); engine decides from `risk_level` only (`internal/policy/engine.go:97-154`); a 5-minute health attestation per row (`internal/models/availability.go:35-41`) would make per-tool MCP rows unusable.
- Discovery, annotation parse and `McpSideEffect` exist in model-gateway with no production consumer (`mcp_jsonrpc.rs:64-97,108-124,223-261`; `runtime_registries.rs:497-558,743-866`); the wire drops annotations (`inference.proto:178-185`). The per-server allowlist is not human approval: connect auto-allowlists every reported tool (`http_routes.rs` `resolve_tool_allowlist` 2786), so annotation trust cannot rest on it. Offering to Do runs depends on a warm gateway cache (`list_mcp_tools` passes `capability_bearer: None`) and breaks above 40 tools (synthetic `mcp_catalog`/`mcp_call`, `runtime_registries.rs:566-677`). Approved MCP calls cannot be cold-resumed (`approval_delivery_worker.rs:16-27` resumes only `execute_provider_action`, `book_shipment`, `tickets.create`).
- [live-only]: `rust/crates/mp-contracts/src/zdr_refusal.rs` (feature `mcp_execution`) and `api/contracts/zdr-refusals.json` untracked; `model-gateway/src/grpc.rs` (M, `proxy_mcp_tool` LIVE 1782), `execution-core/src/runtime_loop/mod.rs` (M +18, `waived: bool`), `runtime_loop/agent.rs` (M 207), `tool_loop.rs` (M) modified. Gate bodies in `permission/mod.rs`, `capability_policy.rs`, `mcp_gateway.rs`, `runtime_registries.rs`, `retry.rs`, all capability-core, `capabilities.proto`, `gateway.proto` are clean in LIVE.
- Audit/doc staleness: card says reuse `risk_level` at `permission/mod.rs:100-200` (absent there); never-auto is not only `tickets.create`; "MCP offered to Do runs" hides the cold-cache and 40-tool breaks; `docs/capability-ownership-matrix.md:142` says stdio MCP wired but code quarantines all but `http`; `mcpIdentifier` allows `__` in server ids (`registry_apis.go:376`) while `parse_mcp_tool_name` splits on the first `__`, so a registry lookup (never parsing) must decide the binding. Existing test patterns: `tests/autonomy_ladder_contract.rs` (source-text pins), `agent.rs:3805` `every_offered_tool_has_a_capability_binding`.

**Provides / consumes**
- Provides to WS13/WS14: `approval_floor` + registry never-auto set; `policy_source` and `side_effect_class` in the signed decision for cause codes and step-up set; single-use for `owner_effect`. To WS20: non-delegable set (`owner_effect`, floor != none) and class ceilings (`read` only by default) for an unattended allowlist. To WS23: `capability_tools` MCP rows, `ResolveToolBindings`, `annotation_trust`, `tool_policies` API, `MCP_DO_TOOLS`. To WS24 (and the workspace-file-tool item the design calls "WS30" [RECONCILE] with section 2.6 row 23: that item is WS24's F-t3-030): contract "a new tool is not offered until it has a `capability_tools` row, a class and an attestor" enforced by the S1 parity test. To WS25: `tool_policies` store; hooks may only narrow (class floor and `never` always win). To WS21/WS22: class rows for `browser_agent`, `browser.*`, `process_*`, `code_interpreter` (declarations only; splitting `browser.observe` off `cap.browser.open` is WS21's).
- Consumes: WS03 `waived: bool` signature and later resumable approvals (could unlock all-class MCP); WS02 `merged_tool_defs` order; WS04 replica-safe MCP hydration; WS01 deployed stack and `SESSION_CORE_CONTINUATION_DESCRIPTOR_KEY`; WS06 audit emitter. Order on hot files (`agent.rs`, `runtime_loop/mod.rs`, `permission/mod.rs`, `capability_policy.rs`, `server.go`, `engine.go`, `grpc.rs`): WS03 lands `waived` first; WS05 S1/S3 first (class lookup is the seam), others add floors/rows not name lists; capability-core migration numbers and `plane-service-principals.json` edits collide with the S4.3 Watch work (ledger 2026-09-14, not reversed), coordinate via the integrator.

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- Under ZDR MCP is never offered and never executed (typed refusal key `mcp_execution`); observation write skipped for a ZDR caller and a ZDR principal cannot mutate (`authz.go` non-read branch); policy writes refused (WS05 D8, read and evaluation allowed); evidence and observations carry digests, classes and hints only, never descriptions, schemas, arguments or outputs. Golden cases: `mcp_tool_observation_under_zdr`, `mcp_tools_under_zdr_not_offered_not_executed` (`api/contracts/zdr-posture-matrix.json` + Rust fixture), `tool_policy_write_under_zdr_refused`; `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`.
- Audit section 8 strengths: signed fail-closed gate (unknown/forged/unbound `mcp__` denied; `capability_policy.rs:921-937` tamper/expiry tests kept; `bindings_are_exact_and_unknown_or_dynamic_tools_fail_closed` kept); Ask/Do switch and autonomy ladder (`autonomy_ladder_contract.rs` re-pointed, not deleted; S0 oracle parity); two loops separate (inline loop untouched, `tool_loop.rs` tests 8929/8966/9430 unchanged plus a source-text test that `tool_loop.rs` never references `tool_class`/`ResolveToolBindings`); honest outcomes (drift, withheld and class-changed paths fail typed; `mcp_tools_withheld_total` counted); credential isolation (no tokens persisted; admin only from signed scopes; observation payload no-token test); Model proposes, Quarry executes (browser rows are declarations only).

**Top risks**
- Mis-declared class is silent over-permission: annotations untrusted by default, TOFU + digest pin + drift quarantine, derivation only tightens, class never loosens a seeded row, user relaxation bounded and digest-pinned.
- Hot-file collisions with uncommitted live work and WS03/WS24: land after WS01-S0 and WS03, hunk-minimal edits, S0 oracle + `shadow` divergence metric + two-week soak, keep `off` path for one release.
- Approved MCP writes dead-end (no cold resume) and the gateway cache is cold or per-replica: `MCP_DO_TOOLS=read` withholds writes; forwarded `capability:read` bearer then WS04; the offer filter requires registry approval so a cold cache fails to offer rather than to authorise.

### WS06 - Audit tamper evidence: hash chain, compliance API, SIEM export
**Resolves:** F-t3-004 (tamper-evidence half; phase 0 stays WS01) | **Owner:** Control Plane: audit-core (chain, retention, compliance API, SIEM exporter), org-core (SIEM destination config); Frontend gateway proxies admin-gated. Model Plane: no code change | **Wave:** W1 | **Type:** S0 FINISH, S1-S8 BUILD, S9 FINISH | **Effort:** about 38-52 d (upper L, near XL): S0 1-2, S1 2-3, S2 5-7, S3 5-7, S4 4-5, S5 4-5, S6 3 (+2 UI), S7 5-6, S8 7-9, S9 2-3; critical path S1-S2-S3-S4-S5-S6 about 24-32 d; S7-S8 parallel (12-15 d) joining at S2; S0 and S7 start immediately | **Depends on:** WS01 phase 0 (publisher fix deployed, 4,992 rows replayed, Model-side terminal-row alert; operational, not code; the chain can be enabled before or after replay); Space session F-t1-031 role-gate on `GET /v1/audit` lands first on `api.go`/`service_auth.go` | **Gating decisions:** DEC-35 (WS06 D1/D3/D4/D5/D6/D8 per map): S0, S1, S2 free; S3, S4 need D1 (trust anchor, Ed25519 in env plus external witness); S5 needs D3 role ruling (owner/admin) and S7 the commercial plan gate `feature.siem_export` (owner ruling); S7 D5 (seal in org-core); S8 D4 (separate `siem-exporter` binary); S6 UI only needs D6 (Space owns the admin view). WS06 D2, D7 local. WS06 D9 per DEC-39 (ADR number integrator-assigned; proposed ADR-0013, section 2.5; the design's "next ADR-0005" is superseded [RECONCILE] with section 2.5). Migration numbers: audit-core after 006, org-core after 020 (section 2.6 row 2: org-core 021, audit-core 007 needs the `//go:embed` list edit).

**Verified starting point**
- One insert chokepoint: `apps/Control Plane/audit-core/internal/store/store.go:77-111` `insertAudit` (called by `InsertAudit` 43, `InsertAuditFromSource` 52 HTTP, `InsertAuditFromStream` 64 JetStream); single `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING id`, no tx, no per-org lock; `payload_hash` (88-92) is replay idempotency only. Schema `internal/store/schema.sql:11-33`: no chain column; `ingested_at DEFAULT NOW()` = transaction start, so not commit-ordered (retention must prune by contiguous `chain_seq`, not timestamp). Migrations are an explicit `//go:embed` list (`migrate.go:12-28,36-43`).
- Retention is `Purge` hard-delete by `ingested_at` (`store.go:243-269`), run at boot then daily by every replica (`cmd/server/main.go:157-187`, `AUDIT_RETENTION_DAYS` default 365). Read `ListAudit` (`store.go:299-346`): limit <=0 or >500 resets to 100 (audit text "capped at 500" is loosely wrong). The HMAC delegation verifies role (`service_auth.go:263-274,283-351`, `UserRole` at 47) but no handler consults it (F-t1-031 defect).
- Ingest authority binds subject to payload (`subscriber.go:287-295`); malformed or mismatched events are dead-lettered, so the chain attests only to persisted rows. Container builds four binaries from one module (`Dockerfile:34-49`), audit-core compose service 128M/0.25 CPU (`CP/docker-compose.yml:806-870`); `audit-nats-provisioner` (767-781) is the pattern for a second service on the same image. No CI runs audit-core Go tests. Existing metrics only `audit_core_events_total`, `audit_core_consumer_pending`, `audit_core_nats_connected`; `CP/observability/prometheus.yml` has no `rule_files` and no rules dir exists in Control.
- org-core: no at-rest encryption primitive and no outbound webhook/SSRF guard anywhere in Control Go; precedent for a narrow internal read with contract test is `GET /internal/orgs/:orgId/retention-posture` (`retention_posture_handler.go`, `retention_posture_contract_test.go`); `feature.audit_logs` exists, computed plan-gated precedent `InteractiveZdrEntitlementKey` (`types.go:276`). Gateway `domains/audit.rs:28-105` is session-only with no role gate; `proxy_json` is JSON-only (no NDJSON), so export is paged JSON. Secrets precedent: `scripts/run-control-plane.sh:171-205` Ed25519 + `persist_if_missing`.
- Audit stale: card's "capped at 500", "reuse gateway audit.rs proxy" (no role gate), declared egress is the `scan-egress.mjs` VENDORS catalogue not a registry. Model-side [live-only] `session-core/src/retention_receipt.rs` `run_started_zdr_waived` is content-free and chains like any event; nothing must land first in code.

**Provides / consumes**
- Provides to every workstream adding audit events (WS13 approval decisions, WS14, WS25 policy changes, WS10 entitlement denials, WS05 class denials): an append-only per-org chained sink; contract is a flat `verevon.audit.v2.<plane>.<producer>.<event>` body with stable `event_id` and content-free `details`, no registration step; `details` size/key guard observe-only then enforced. To WS01: the Control-side `observability/rules/*.yml` mechanism (S0) and chain-activation evidence for the trust claim. To F-t3-062 and F-t1-051: `Purge` is the only delete path and is chain-safe (`retentionFor(org)` seam, legal hold plugs in). To the Space session: `requireComplianceRole`, the compliance client and action descriptors, `GET /api/v1/audit/compliance/events` as the data source for its admin view; Space's retention TTL must call the chain-safe purge. WS07 emits `memory.state_changed`/`memory.reset`/`learning.proposal_decided` only after this schema exists.
- Consumes WS01 phase 0 (publisher deployed, requeue migration, 4,992 rows replayed, Model-side alert location); Control org-core, `run-control-plane.sh` secrets, gateway delegation (membership role). Quarry events enter via integration-corev2 `POST /v1/audit` (`audit:write`, plane pinned `ingestion`) and are chained with no WS06 change. Hot spots: `api.go`, `service_auth.go`, `store.go` with F-t1-031 (Space first); `CP/docker-compose.yml` one compose PR per slice; `run-control-plane.sh` credential lists append alphabetically; `observability/rules/` one file per owner; `docs/compliance/evidence/*` S9 last. Model Plane needs no code change (`git diff --stat` shows none in execution-core or model-gateway).

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR: no content stored or copied; hashes of already content-free columns only; ZDR-derived envelopes are suppressed today (`docs/ZDR.md:95,259`) and stay so; exporter logs never carry URL query, headers or body; secrets only in memory. Golden: NEW `audit-core/internal/testdata/export-content-free.golden.json` (S2) asserting session-core wire fixtures hash and export with only `details` keys `tool, data_category, zdr, phase`; D7 guard metric; exporter log-leak test; `go test ./internal/store/... ./internal/siem/...`. The shared posture matrix evaluates route decisions not stores, so no matrix row is added unless its owner adds a "stores" section (not determined).
- Strengths: ZDR by construction (above); honest outcomes (verifier reports `verified_with_legacy`/`partial`/`key_unavailable`, gap markers, completeness not claimed; S4 attack table, S8 gap test, S9 honest-limits); credential isolation (sealed write-only secrets; `validate-release-credentials.sh`); multi-tenant lease-based outboxes (publisher untouched, per-org chain/cursors/RLS; `audit_publisher.rs:1038` fixture and `session_core_wire_fixtures_test.go` stay green, S2 multi-org test, S7 RLS negative test). Keep green: `TestReplayedSessionCoreAuditEventIsStoredExactlyOnce` (`session_core_replay_test.go:26`), `TestLogicalAuditIdentityIsBoundToProducerAndRejectsConflictingReuse` (`store_test.go:243`), `TestAuthorizeAuditReadRejectsTamperingExpiryAndReplay` (`service_auth_test.go:91`), `jetstream_inbox_contract_test.go`, nats-provisioner `manifest_contract_test.go`.

**Top risks**
- Purge destroying evidence or breaking the chain (S3): verify-before-delete, incident stop, dry-run flag, batch txs with a signed checkpoint per batch, racing-purger tests.
- False sense of security (a DB-owner attacker can rewrite the chain): signed attestations with a key outside the DB, SIEM/customer export as external witness, honest-limits text, no claim broadening before evidence (S9).
- Deploy-order lockouts (an image that predates a scope refuses the whole credential registry: audit-core at S5, org-core at S7) and SSRF/secret leakage in S8: image and compose roll together via `run-control-plane.sh`, contract tests read the real compose block, never bare `docker compose build`; dial-time guard, no redirects, separate process, sealed secrets, production refusal of the dev flag.

**Verified starting point**
- Memory has six write sites, not one: inline extraction at append `SC/src/grpc.rs:2630-2673` (skipped only for `source_scope='conversation'`); Dreaming loop `SC/src/dreaming.rs:145-165` (`load_pending_messages` 1226-1246 filters only `extract_after`; `persist_candidates` 738-784, `apply_grounded_candidates` 797-840; spawned `SC/src/main.rs:157-162`); `IndexMemory` gRPC `SC/src/memory_grpc.rs:441-513` -> `dreaming::index_agent_memory` (1516-1570), no state check; Letta mirrors; capability-core `/api/v1/memory` writes `agent_memory` directly (`CC/internal/api/workplane_apis.go:28-35`, INSERT 374, UPDATE 445, DELETE 468) in the shared `session_core` DB, reachable via model-gateway `/v1/memory` proxy (`http_routes.rs:185-190,365-370`; no callers found in this repo); operator CLI `session-core memory-dedup`.
- Five read sites: Ask prefetch `model-gateway/src/sse.rs:1581-1611`, `fetch_chat_memory_context` 5036-5138; gRPC twin `grpc.rs:365-402`; inline `recall_memory` `tool_loop.rs:3151-3207` (returns `[]`, cannot say "paused"); context assembly inside session-core `SC/src/grpc.rs:4138-4186`; `SearchMemory`/`ListMemory` (`memory_grpc.rs:347-596`); Do-loop `execute_save_memory`/`execute_recall_memory` (`execution-core/src/runtime_loop/mod.rs:2154-2220,2347`) which call the same RPCs. `user_memory_controls` holds `extract_after` only (`SC/migrations/0039_memory_user_control.sql:3-16`; `memory_control.rs:5-34`).
- Control: user-core `memoryEnabled` default false has no reader and the stored `false` is a struct-patch artifact (any PUT writes it: `CP/user-core/internal/http/handlers.go:1020-1027,1093-1102,1405-1447`), so it must never be read as user intent. org-core precedent for org policy: `metadata.supportAi` jsonb_set (`CP/org-core/internal/org/repository.go:412-428`), machine route `/internal/orgs/:orgId/retention-posture` and scope allowlist (`service_auth.go:30-50,360-361`); new scope must end in `:any`; `org_quota.rs` fails OPEN (`model-gateway/src/org_quota.rs:106-157`), memory policy must not copy that.
- Skills: learned skills written `Enabled: true`, no scope/owner (`CC/internal/skillsink/sink.go:46-68`); the RPC conflict arm overwrites `enabled` (`SC/src/grpc.rs:4934-4943`); `agent_skills` is unique on `(org_id, name)` (`0004_tasks_cron_hooks_skills_memory.sql:204-205`) so with scope `user` two users collide and B would overwrite A's private skill (audit omits this); `SessionRef` has no user id (`CC/internal/sessionreview/trigger.go:40-44`) though the envelope has (`learning_events.rs:113-147`); gateway skill cache pulls once per org per 60 s with the triggering caller's bearer (`model-gateway/src/skills.rs:37-101`) with a third consumer the audit omits (`tool_loop.rs:3291-3325`), `replace_org_kind` wipe and fail-open `usable_or_untracked` (`ownership.rs`). Compose default loop OFF (`MP/deploy/docker-compose.yml:1429`), ON in code (`CC/cmd/main.go:750-752`).
- Audit text stale: "Dreaming, prefetch, save_memory, recall_memory, skill review" misses five sites; "Ask turns cannot save" is a deliberate boundary (do not lift `inline_tool_allowed` `tool_loop.rs:615-620`); "stored setting says off" is an artifact; "loop on by default" is code-only. UI today: `FE/src/features/settings/components/MemorySection.tsx:69-85` list and two-step delete only; no skill or learning action exists (`memory.delete` at `action-registry.ts:2365`; precedent `org.update_support_ai_mode` at 2772).

**Provides / consumes**
- Provides to WS02: `SearchMemoryResponse.skip_reason` closed tokens (`user_paused`, `user_off`, `org_disabled`, `zdr`) and `GetMemoryControls`; Do, Plan and scheduled runs reach memory only through `SearchMemory`/context assembly so the gate applies once and WS02 stamps `SKIPPED_BY_POLICY(memory_*)`. To WS20: scheduled runs follow the owner's state. To WS26: hook `learning.Screen` before the sink. To WS25: `metadata.agentLearning`, the internal route and the `org_agent_policy` mirror are the first members of an org agent policy (see the DEC-14 [RECONCILE] above). To WS06: content-free `memory_control_events` and `skill_revisions` rows as the source for `memory.state_changed`, `memory.reset`, `learning.proposal_decided`. To the Space session: `memory_control::eligible_thread_origin` helper (S1) for the Dreaming containment predicate (overlap row: one migration `(scope_kind, scope_id)` + audience predicate, section 2.6 row 13). To Teach mode (F-t3-081): the proposal contract; the `origin` CHECK (0006) must be extended by whoever adds an origin.
- Consumes WS01 (live waiver/retention-receipt set landed, deploy runbook), WS26 screening, WS06 audit chain, a route kind stamped on the run (WS10/WS11 or F-t3-001, for OD4), Space `space` skill scope extending the new unique index. Hot spots: `SC/src/grpc.rs`, `dreaming.rs`, `learning_events.rs`, `memory_grpc.rs`, `sessions.proto` (WS01 first, then S1; append proto messages at file end); model-gateway `sse.rs`/`tool_loop.rs`/`http_routes.rs`/`grpc.rs` (thin diffs, new logic in `skills.rs`, `memory_prefetch.rs`); `action-registry.ts`/`dispatchers.rs`/`handlers.rs` additive; `WorkspaceSettingsPage.tsx`/`settings-sections.ts` one `Match` and one section id per workstream; `CP/docker-compose.yml:540` credentials JSON is one line (merge by hand); `plane-service-principals.json` scopes alphabetical.

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- Persisted: state, counts and policy only, never content; `GetMemoryControls` for a ZDR caller returns `reason=zdr`, `use_allowed=false`, no write; `SetMemoryControls` refused `FAILED_PRECONDITION`; reads stay suppressed as today; erasure (delete, Reset) stays allowed because pre-ZDR rows may exist. Per DEC-32 a waived run counts as ZDR for the new stores: ZDR and waived runs are never announced to the learning producer (`learning_events.rs:257-265`; waived 158-178 [live-only]); proposals and revisions persist content derived from retained runs only and are erased with owner/org (`gdpr.rs`). Golden: in-module `memory_control::tests::decision_table` over (zdr, org, state), then a `memory_controls` row beside `zdr_features.rs:165-168` plus `mp-contracts/tests/zdr_retention_matrix.rs` once the live matrix lands; `learning_events` ZDR test unchanged.
- Strengths: two loops separate (`inline_tool_allowed_still_refuses_save_memory`, assertion at W 9107 kept); memory provenance and single index (no second store; `memory_prefetch.rs` tests 112-207, `memory_provenance` unchanged); honest outcomes (`recall_memory_reports_paused_not_empty`, typed `skip_reason`); credential isolation / signed gate (one narrow scope, user-bound routes, `cap.memory.index` mapping `capability_policy.rs:808` untouched, `plane-token-scopes.spec.ts`); multi-tenant runtime (`(org_id, user_id)` scoped SQL, `reset_is_tenant_scoped`, `gdpr.rs` tests 624-625 extended); typed ChatEvent taxonomy (no new SSE event; taxonomy CI gate untouched).

**Top risks**
- A memory writer outside the gate (capability-core SQL, future path): source-scan test, S5 gate, `MEMORY_CONTROLS_ENABLED` kill switch with ERROR logging, matrix note.
- Merge pressure on live-modified session-core, model-gateway and settings files plus cross-user skill exposure during the cache/index change: WS01-S0 first, thin edits, new logic in new modules, proto appends; unique-index fix before any user-scoped write, fail-closed ownership for learned skills, the four gateway tests plus the live two-user proof.
- Org policy staleness and review cost/injection persistence (OD4 cannot be enforced: no route kind on the run): explicit poll bound, `ORG_POLICY_FAIL_CLOSED`, mirror watermark and age metric; disabled owner-scoped proposals, per-user/org caps, org opt-in default off, WS26 hook; OD4 stays an owner decision.

### WS08 - Attachments v2
**Resolves:** F-t3-017 | **Owner:** Model Plane (model-gateway, inference-core, execution-core); supporting Frontend Plane (composer, BFF), Ingestion Plane imports-core and Data Plane documents-api-go for the deferred durable phase only | **Wave:** W1 | **Type:** S1 FINISH, S2-S7 BUILD, S8 BUILD (deferred outline) | **Effort:** S1-S6 about 34-44 d (S1 3-4, S2 5-6, S3 7-9, S4 6-8, S5 8-10, S6 5-7), S7 +4-5 d, S8 XL not sized; critical path S2 -> S3 -> S4, S5 parallel after S2, S6 last; a releasable "no silent loss" increment ships after S1+S2 (about 8-10 d, no proto change) | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline) for every slice (`zdr_posture.rs`, `zdr_features.rs`, ZDR tests, `sse.rs`/`http_routes.rs`/`tool_loop.rs`, FE `chat-normalizers.ts`/`DashboardComposer.tsx`/`chat-client.ts`/`use-chat-controller.ts` are live-modified); WS02 (S4/S7 field and `seed_messages`, 27-site `RunAgentRequest` literal conversion, D8 tag allocation) for S6; WS11 `InferRequest` tag 17; WS03 checkpoint scrub for S6; WS10 owns the `models` array of `/v1/models` | **Gating decisions:** S1-S3 need no decision. DEC-39: ratified proto tags (S3 `InferRequest`, S5 `ExecuteStepRequest = 12` verified free, S6 `RunAgentRequest`); merge order only. DEC-37 (WS08 D1/D4/D7 per map): S7 and S8 wait for the OD18 ruling (4 MB for xlsx/pptx/pdf after the H1 zip guard, local Tesseract, 60k chars stay); ship under today's 1 MB with caps server-declared so a ruling is a config flip; ZDR keeps refusing attachments; subscription route refuses with typed `attachment_route_unsupported` (S2). WS08 D2, D5, D6, D8, D9 local. [RECONCILE] with section 2.6 row 1 / DEC-39: the designs propose `InferRequest.image_parts = 20` and `RunAgentRequest.attachments = 20` while WS11 holds `InferRequest` 17, WS02 18/19 and `RunAgentRequest` 18 (WS02), 19 (WS03); WS08 keeps a dedicated field (not `RunContext.attachments = 5`) so attachments are not gated by WS02's flag; the integrator assigns the numbers.

**Verified starting point**
- Browser reads every attachment before the draft clears: 1,000,000-byte gate before the image branch, PDF/DOCX/XLSX/PPTX text from server extraction, 60,000 chars per file / 120,000 per turn (`FE/src/features/chat/lib/prepare-chat-attachments.ts:18-35`). Documents are inlined as `--- VEDLEGG: name ---`; only `image/*` survives as stream attachments `{kind:'image', data_base64, mime_type}` (`chat-normalizers.ts:1147-1202`, live `:1186-1241`); `StreamAttachment` can only be an image (`chat-types.ts:505-509`). Subscription route silently drops every attachment (`FE/src/shared/api/chat-client.ts:1114`, live `:1144`).
- Any image attachment diverts the whole turn to `AnalyzeImage`: `select_image` (first image only) then `vision_stream` (no history, tools, memory, grounding), before the agentic branch, so a Do turn with an image never reaches the agent (`RS/model-gateway/src/sse.rs:1341-1355`, `:1381`, `:5646`; `vision.rs:52-70`). ZDR turns refuse any attachment (`sse.rs:976-977`). A subscription-pinned request carrying an image would be analysed on the platform chain (server has no route check): data-egress gap, fixed in S2.
- Wire has no image parts: `ChatMessage{role, content, name, compaction_summary}` tags 1-4 (`MP/proto/model_plane/v1/inference.proto:198-211`); adapters build `"content": m.content` (`RS/inference-core/src/provider/openai.rs:411-429`, `anthropic.rs:670-709`). Vision flag is per provider not per model (`provider/mod.rs:669-687`; `openai.rs:610-650,1040-1070`; `anthropic.rs:933`; `codex_subscription.rs:612`). The default Verevon modes are not catalogue entries (`chat-client.ts:816-833`), so a client catalogue gate cannot answer for `verevon-balance`.
- Stacked caps: sessionStorage handoff 2,000,000 chars (`pending-chat-launch.ts:7,66-97`), axum default 2 MiB JSON body on BFF and model-gateway (`GW/domains/chat/streams.rs:18-23`, `sse.rs:835-846`; no `DefaultBodyLimit` override), tonic 4 MiB default decode (no `max_decoding_message_size` anywhere, so inference-core `MAX_IMAGE_BYTES` 20 MB is unreachable over gRPC). A 1 MB image is 1.33 MB base64: two images already exceed handoff and body caps (derived, not reproduced).
- Sandbox: `code_interpreter` `files_in` is model-authored `{name, content_b64}` into the writable root, workspace deleted on every path (`RS/execution-core/src/code_interpreter.rs:133-145,179-264,329-345,430-565`); a read-only layering seam exists, `LaunchOptions.extra_ro_binds` (`sandbox.rs:95-112,202-210`; `executor.rs:250-263`) with no caller. Both loops reach the same `code_interpreter` arm (gateway via `ExecuteStep` `tools.rs:163-260`, Do via `execute_step_inner`). `ExecuteStepRequest` highest tag 11, `RunAgentRequest` highest 17 (`execution.proto:268-318,76-209`).
- Audit stale: "phase 1 client-side: read the vision flag and disable image attach" would block images that work today (AnalyzeImage serves them regardless of the picked model); the flag matters only after S4 makes the picked model the reader. OD18 is a recommended default, not a ruling. Durable text route is BFF to documents-api-go (not model-gateway; ZDR 403 at `documents.go:304-305`); imports-core `POST /api/v1/import/extract` is ephemeral, 1,000,000 bytes, rejects scanned files (`app/chat_extract.py:8-50`). `unsentAttachmentNames` (`chat-normalizers.ts:1098`) has no callers. [live-only]: `RS/model-gateway/src/zdr_posture.rs`, `zdr_features.rs` (table over every `InvokeRequest` field, destructured without `..`; `attachments` is `refused_feature`), `tests/zdr_feature_table.rs`, `zdr_posture_matrix.rs`, `zdr_refusal_inventory.rs`, `MP/api/contracts/zdr-refusals.json`.
