**Provides / consumes**
- Provides: to WS19 the `attachments/` read-only mount inside `code_interpreter` (S5/S6), `mp-contracts::attachments` caps and `RunAttachment`; WS19 adds its LibreOffice layer and bundle mount on the same `extra_ro_binds` seam (vector, so a second pair is additive). To WS22/WS24: read-only input mount semantics and reserved name `attachments`; a future `SandboxBackend` must preserve ro binds (`External` policy applies no bwrap: refuse staging). To WS10: sibling `attachment_policy` on `/v1/models` (WS10 owns `models`, `features` keeps `vision`). To WS27: image parts count as input tokens, `usage` frame unchanged. To WS26: attachment text is user-authored and already inlined; sandbox outputs stay `unscreened` provenance (`tool_loop.rs:2570-2590`); `RunAttachment.sha256` is the scanner extension point. To WS12: the named tests and an FE architecture guard (no silent `[]` drop). To WS03/WS02: Do attachments registry lifetime (release on terminal, cancel, park).
- Consumes: WS01 live ZDR wave committed; WS02 tag 18 and `seed_messages`; WS11 `InferRequest` tag 17; WS03 checkpoint scrub; WS05 unchanged `code_interpreter` declaration; integrator for tags (`InferRequest.image_parts` proposal 20, `RunAgentRequest.attachments` proposal 20, `ExecuteStepRequest.attachments = 12`). Hot spots: `model-gateway/src/sse.rs`, `tool_loop.rs`, `http_routes.rs` (WS01 commits first; S2 validator merges before S4); `execution.proto`, `inference.proto` (integrator tags, WS02 first, `buf breaking` in `.github/workflows/buf.yml`); `execution-core/.../agent.rs`, `runtime_loop/mod.rs` (WS08 touches only the `code_interpreter` arm and the seed note); FE `chat-normalizers.ts`, `DashboardComposer.tsx`, `chat-client.ts`, `use-chat-controller.ts` (WS09 transcript refactor edits the controller first; WS08 adds new files). Space session: durable and Space-bound uploads are theirs plus Data/Ingestion; attachments are never passed to Quarry or `browser_agent`.

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR attachment refusal precedes everything (`sse.rs:976-977`; live waived refusal `:971-979`); no attested provider exists yet so the refusal stays (DEC-37; `docs/ZDR.md:97`); staging refuses ZDR and the step cannot carry bytes (`enforce_persistence_free_execution`, `execution-core/src/grpc.rs:1237`); images are request-scope memory only, never in session-core, events, cache, artifacts, checkpoints or audit outbox; bytes never logged (sha256, size, count only); workspace deleted by the Drop guard. Per DEC-32 waived turns refuse attachments (same typed codes). Golden rows: `zdr_feature_table.rs`, `zdr_posture_matrix.rs`, `zdr_waiver_route_tests.rs` (all [live-only]), `(Do, attachment, ZDR)`, `(code_interpreter staging, ZDR)`; `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`.
- Strengths: two loops separate (only a pure contracts crate is shared; `cross_service_loop_contract.rs` extended, mutation-tested); kernel-enforced sandbox (ro bind additive after the writable bind, unsandboxed host refuses staging; `sandbox.rs` argv tests, `verify-sandbox-isolation.sh` live); honest outcomes (never silent drop, invalid base64 errors); typed event taxonomy and byte-verbatim BFF (no new event variants, BFF only gains a body limit; `gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check`); credential isolation (sandbox env cleared, no network; existing `child_env` tests plus new log-redaction test); intent routing with provenance (vision filter is another skip-before-network gate; `fallback.rs` tests); multi-tenant runtime (bytes never cross plane databases).

**Top risks**
- Hot-file merge pressure (`sse.rs`, `tool_loop.rs`, `agent.rs`, both protos) and ambiguous proto tags: WS01-S0 first, new modules and thin diffs, add-only protos with integrator-assigned tags (section 2.6 row 1).
- Stacked limits make cap raises fail in the wrong hop, and per-model vision truth can over-advertise: caps server-declared (S2), limits derived from one number (S7) with a four-hop test; denylist function with a test table and server-side fail-closed `vision_unsupported` regardless of client belief.
- Weakening the sandbox claim and bytes in requests (memory, logs, checkpoints): refuse staging on a passthrough host, live negative-write proof, request-scope RAII-zeroing registry, sha256-only logging, WS03 checkpoint scrub test, ZDR refusal at the boundary.

### WS09 - Transcript performance

**Resolves:** F-t3-021 | **Owner:** Frontend Plane (renderer, benchmark, chunk self-heal, BFF history relay); Model Plane session-core and model-gateway for S8 only (conditional on measurement) | **Wave:** W1 | **Type:** per-slice mix: [FINISH] S2, S3; [BUILD] S1, S4-S10 | **Effort:** committed core S1+S2+S3+S5 = L-, 12-17 d; conditional S4/S6/S7 3-6 d; paging S8+S9 11-16 d; S10 8-12 d; worst case XL ~35-50 d, expected ~15-23 d | **Depends on:** S8 on WS01-S0 (land live baseline) and WS17 (shared `list_conversation`); S2/S3/S5 hunks rebase onto the WS01-S0 FE live-file commit; S12 of WS12 gives the CI home for S5's Playwright project | **Gating decisions:** DEC-29 (S8, S9: cursor/field ownership, D4); DEC-39 (proto tag numbers for S8). Local: D1 (benchmark placement, S1 wording), D2 (nodeKey, S2), D3 (snapshot cadence, S3), D5 (numeric triggers for S4/S6/S7/S8/S10, owner sign), D6 (chunk policy, S5), D7 (no action contract for load-earlier, S9), D8 (Trace collapse, S3). Free to start: S1, S2, S3, S5.

**Verified starting point:**
- Turn list already keyed by turn id (`FP/src/features/chat/components/ChatPage.tsx:827`, [LV 838]); the audit's "un-virtualised list at ChatMessages.tsx:669" actually cites the per-message node list. Real remount cascade: `ChatMessages.tsx:532` `nodeEntries` builds new objects per recompute and `:667` `<For each={nodeEntries()}>` has no `keyed`, so on Solid 2.0.0-rc.0 every node of the streaming message (incl. `AnswerRegion` -> `ChatMarkdown`) remounts per token; `ChatMarkdown` `:1084-1092` reparses the whole message (`parseMarkdownBlocks`, `chat-media-markdown.tsx:751`). Read from `@solidjs/signals@2.0.0-rc.0 dev.js:7805-8060`, not run; S1 pins it.
- Costs the audit missed (magnitude unmeasured): local snapshot rewritten every flush (`use-chat-controller.ts:1742-1767` -> `writeThreadSnapshot :557`, writes localStorage + sessionStorage); per-delta `upsertTaskStep` (`:2609-2616`, `:3809-3818`); one Trace row per token (`appendUiEvent :272-275`, cap 5000); cold open is a sequential waterfall (`loadThread :1004`: listChatThreads, `/transcript`, `/messages`, two identical unpaged session-core reads via BFF `history.rs:595-640` and `json_handlers.rs:239`).
- Read path: `sessions.proto` `ListConversationRequest` fields 1-5, no limit/cursor (next free 6), response `messages = 1` (next free 2); `SC/src/grpc.rs:5216-5328` [LV 5387-5507] has no LIMIT; `sequence` is a global identity so it must not be the cursor; index `idx_messages_thread_id (thread_id, sequence)` (`SC/migrations/0001_init.sql:14-24`).
- No chunk recovery exists: no `vite:preloadError` listener or `location.reload` in `FP/src`; Vite 8.2.2 dispatches the event in production builds only. Composer draft is not persisted (`use-chat-controller.ts:304`).
- [live-only]: `ChatMessages.tsx` (+229), `ChatPage.tsx`, `use-chat-controller.ts`, `chat-client.ts` (+53), `FP/src/index.tsx` (`boot()` wrapper), `FP/src/demo/` (fetch-fixture runtime, the pattern S1 copies), `sessions.proto` (+42), `grpc.rs` (+861), `http_routes.rs` (+237), `zdr_posture_matrix.rs`.
- Audit stale: "No ZDR or Space-authority impact" is wrong (S3 touches the ZDR-gated local persistence funnel, S8 the Space reader branch `audience_ceiling`); "quick win purely Frontend" holds for S1/S2/S3/S5 only; I5 cites `ChatMarkdown :1052-1060`, real `:1084-1092`.

[RECONCILE] WS17 also proposes `include_inactive = 8` on the same request: DEC-39 / section 2.6 row 1 assigns numbers. Edit order: WS09 S2 merges first in `ChatMessages.tsx` (hunks `:667`, `:1084`, registry); small local hunks in `use-chat-controller.ts` (`:1742`, `:2611`, `:1004`); S4 touches only `case 'chunk'`.

**ZDR clause and regression guards:**
- ZDR: S3 is a content-persisting boundary (localStorage, sessionStorage, 500 ms server snapshot); the throttled writer only delays calls to `writeThreadSnapshot` so `isTemporaryThread`, `isForeignOriginThread`, `isLocalRetentionAllowed` still apply. Proof: temporary thread or ZDR posture = zero `setItem` for transcript/history keys across a full stream and every flush trigger; a pending trailing write is dropped (not flushed) if the thread turns temporary/ZDR before the timer. Case added in `snapshot-throttle` test files and referenced from WS01 S0's matrix list. S8: no new store; paged read of a ZDR/temporary thread returns the same refusal as the unpaged read (golden matrix case); cursor is a message id validated inside the thread (no cross-tenant existence oracle). S1 fixtures are synthetic, `setItem` wrapper records counts and lengths only. S2/S4/S5/S6/S7/S10 persist nothing (S5 stores one timestamp).
- Audit section 8 strengths: rendering/a11y/calm workspace (S2 equivalence property test, `ChatMessages.codeblock.test.tsx`, `ChatMessages.chart.test.tsx`, `chat-markdown.test.ts`, `ChatMessages.truth.test.tsx`; S7/S10 screen-reader and find-in-page checks); typed ChatEvent taxonomy and byte-verbatim relay (S4 interleaving test, `.github/workflows/sse-taxonomy.yml`); ZDR by construction (S3 cases, S8 golden row, existing `use-chat-controller.zdr-refusal.test.ts` green); multi-tenant runtime (S8 `_against_real_pg` tests: foreign cursor, other org, audience ceiling); honest outcomes (S4 flush-on-abort, S9 saved-answer tail test); context management (per-turn `load_recent_thread_messages` untouched, default `limit = 0`).

**Top risks:**
- Solid 2.0 rc `<For>` behaviour is read, not run: S1 counter pins it before S2 builds; reference-keyed fallback and `VITE_CHAT_RENDER_LEGACY`.
- Wrong target (the biggest per-token cost may be snapshot/step/Trace, not I5) and incremental-parse divergence: S1 attributes cost per source before S2-S4; D5 triggers are numbers; differential property test at every prefix plus full-parse fallback.
- Hot-file merge pressure (`ChatMessages.tsx`, `use-chat-controller.ts`, `chat-client.ts`, `index.tsx`, `sessions.proto`, `grpc.rs`) and paging changing what the UI can see: WS01-S0 first, small disjoint hunks, add-only proto, message-id cursor inside the thread, explicit "load all", partial-aware merge, default-off flag.

### WS10 - Org and role model entitlements

**Resolves:** F-t3-023 | **Owner:** Control Plane decides (org-core stores/resolves/explains); Model Plane enforces (inference-core is the PEP and the only MP puller; model-gateway asks via new RPC); Frontend Plane admin UI + BFF | **Wave:** W1 | **Type:** BUILD-NEW with one [FINISH] (S1 and the `OrgPolicy` read-back in S4) | **Effort:** XL, 31-44 d (critical path S0, S2, S3, S4, S6, S7 ~26-37 d; ~4-5 elapsed weeks with three engineers plus a one-day shadow soak) | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline) before S1, S3, S4, S6; WS01 S5 (`inference-core/src/{config,auth,grpc}.rs` hunk) before S3; WS01 S6 (`sse.rs`) before S4; WS01 S2 `rebuild-service.sh`, WS01 deploy harness + ZDR-TEST org for S7; WS11 ladder rung ids (interim `quick < standard < deep`); WS12 gates for S6 | **Gating decisions:** DEC-13 (S2-S7; D1-D4, D6; reverses `GATEWAY_DEDUPLICATION_PLAN.md:388`); DEC-36 (D8 effort-cap seam, S4); DEC-39 (proto tags, migration number, ADR number). Local: D5 chat-only, D7 plan/group deferred, D9 role override replaces default, D10 ZDR write guard, D11 typed refusal wire, D12 keep `ALLOWED_MODELS` as operator ceiling, D13 owner+admin write via BFF + org-core check, D14 Convex comment-only. Free to start: S0 (records DEC-13), S1 (needs D11 only); S2 once S0 records D1/D9/D13.

**Verified starting point:**
- `ALLOWED_MODELS` read by `load_allowed_models` (`MG/normalize.rs:84-93`); gate `normalize.rs:363-378` returns untyped 400; `normalize` has only three callers (unary `http_routes.rs:8821`, stream `sse.rs:688/927`) so gRPC `invoke` (`grpc.rs:683`), `ai_chat`, deep research, tool loop and execution-core bypass it; dev override `MP/deploy/docker-compose.override.yml:12` is a no-op.
- `OrgPolicy.allowed_models = 4` (`MP/proto/model_plane/v1/gateway.proto:1343-1355`) is refused by name (`MG/grpc.rs:148-160`, `2220-2226`); `get_policy` (`:2167-2200`) returns it zero-valued.
- `list_models` sends `ListModelsRequest::default()` (`http_routes.rs:7490-7502`); inference-core `ListModels` (`IC/grpc.rs:388-459`) sees real `org_id`/`user_id` (`AuthenticatedPrincipal`, `IC/auth.rs:82-90`) but no claim carries a role. Chain can pick unadmitted models: `resolve_intent` (`provider/fallback.rs:1011-1041`), `tool_ladder` (`:1105-1127`), provider default (`:236-244`, `1261-1317`); response cache `cache.rs:52-70` consulted at `fallback.rs:1160` so admission must run before it.

**ZDR clause and regression guards:**
- ZDR: entitlement metadata is model ids and actor ids only, no content; in-memory cache only; under `enforce` an unresolved entitlement refuses, never falls back, ZDR included; a policy leaving no attested model for a ZDR token yields `model_not_entitled`; BFF write guard 409 `model_policy_leaves_no_zdr_model`; refusal happens before `StartRun` (zero session-core calls, asserted); refusal bodies content-free. Golden rows `r_refusal_is_content_free`, `z_policy_has_no_content`, `z_policy_survives_zdr_toggle`, `z_zdr_token_pinned_denied`, `z_zdr_org_policy_strips_all_attested`, `z_entitlement_unavailable_under_zdr_refuses`; postgres test proves `gdpr_hard_delete_organization` leaves no rows.
- Audit section 8 strengths: ZDR by construction (`model-entitlement-contract-sync`, `mp-contracts/tests/model_entitlement_matrix.rs`; existing `scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh` untouched and green); intent routing with provenance (`provider/intent.rs` tests unchanged, "no policy byte-identical" chain test); honest outcomes (no silent downgrade, `infer-statuses.json`); typed ChatEvent taxonomy (error frame reused: `python3 "apps/Model Plane/scripts/gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py" --check`, HEAD already fails it, live passes); two loops separate (`cross_service_loop_contract.rs` unchanged); credential isolation (`service_auth_test.go`, contract test, `release-contract-test.sh`); multi-tenant runtime (RLS test).

**Top risks:**
- Stale cache lets a revoked model through, and fail-closed outage blocks chat: TTL 30 s, restrictive answers cached longer on purpose (900 s vs 120 s), shadow day first, one-restart `off` rollback, explain shows revision.
- Live ZDR hunks in `grpc.rs`, `auth.rs`, `sse.rs`, `http_routes.rs`: hard precondition WS01-S0, additive local hunks, WS01 S5/S6 edit first.
- Policy escapes via service principals and scheduled runs: D6 stated, `service_exempt` metric, WS20 interface; revisit with an acts-for-user contract; Quarry planner exemption needs writing by the Quarry session.

**ZDR clause and regression guards:**
- ZDR: effort is a request parameter only, already declared Honored under ZDR (`zdr_features.rs:120`); no new store, so no golden-matrix row; no reasoning text under ZDR (`zdr_features.rs:174-175`); summarized thinking streamed ephemerally and never appended to `assistant_output` (`sse.rs:3067-3071`); no new `InvokeRequest` field so `FIELD_TREATMENTS` untouched; Codex runs on the waived/subscription route with broker `store:false` unchanged (`direct.go:497-500`); Do runs under ZDR refuse as today. ZDR evidence is "test-proven only" until WS01 delivers an attested provider.
- Audit section 8 strengths: ZDR by construction (`zdr_features.rs` table test, `zdr_turn_honors_the_effort_level`, `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh` green); typed ChatEvent taxonomy (no new event, `.github/workflows/sse-taxonomy.yml` unchanged); intent routing with provenance (`intent.rs` tests `:773-917` unchanged, `pinned_model_is_not_clamped`, S5 re-resolution test); two loops separate (`autonomy_ladder_contract.rs`, `cross_service_loop_contract.rs` unchanged); honest outcomes (`truncation_budget_includes_the_allowance`, clamped flag logged); proto-first (`buf breaking` additive, `baseline.binpb` not regenerated by WS11; `cargo test -p mp-contracts`); default path unchanged (golden-body tests, `thinking.rs` tests `:61-117` retained).

**Top risks:**
- Provider wire drift (Azure api-version, per-model levels, Foundry `output_config`; claude-api reference cached 2026-10-06): P0 probes, `verified_on` per row, bounded single retry, default-models-have-rows test.
- Half the derivation lives in [live-only] files and pinned legacy tests encode old behaviour (`anthropic.rs:1903-1915`, Codex `:1004-1068`, `source_validation` 4096): WS01-S0 first, S5 hunk-minimal, ZDR parity test, deliberate test changes with the reversal named in PR and ledger.
- Cost increase from Q3 allowances and Deep finally meaning Deep (D1): bounded allowances, owner sign-off, S7 posture clamp, WS27 visibility, flags default off.

**Provides / consumes:**
- Provides: all FE workstreams (WS08, WS09, WS10, WS11, WS13-WS16, WS23, WS27) `spa-ui-gates` (new bare copy or raw colour in chat/agents and in any newly added `src/` file fails), `FE/src/test/locale-leak.ts`, `FE/docs/UI_STATE_CHECKLIST.md`; WS02, WS04, WS14, WS15, WS27 (any ChatEvent change) must update the `payload_contract` sample table and regenerate `MP/api/contracts/sse-event-payloads.json` + vendored copy + `chat-event-payloads.generated.ts` in the same PR (`MP_UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 cargo test -p model-gateway --lib sse_event_payload_golden`, then sync-script digest); WS15 S6 gate extended to orchestration run events and ad-hoc names; WS04, WS09, WS17 a working `buf breaking` and lint ratchet (S2); WS09 a CI home for stack-free Playwright and bundle budget (S12); WS01 a reachable CI to prove the ZDR wave (S1); all Rust workstreams log-content, plane-boundary, size ratchet (ceilings for the three Model Plane hot files).
- Consumes: WS01-S0 (committed ZDR wave, staging stack for S7 evidence); WS09 `chunk-recovery` project and spec; WS08 FE guard test runs in `pnpm test`; WS03 `action-surface-contract.test.mjs`; WS02/WS15/WS27 add samples for the variants they introduce. Edit order: `ChatMessages.tsx` S3 first; `sse_events.rs` WS01-S0 then S7 (test submodule only) then others regenerate; `chat-client.ts` S8 touches `emitCitation` and usage case only; `zdr-contract-sync.test.sh` WS01 first, S7 appends rows only; CI workflow files each add a job, no edits inside existing jobs except S1 trigger filters; `FE/package.json` at most one script line; `FE/playwright.config.ts` WS09 owns projects.
- Cross-session: Space session opts into `features/spaces` by adding `'src/features/spaces/**'` to `UI_GATE_SCOPES` after clearing its leaks (new Space files are covered by the new-file check), must not edit `eslint.config.js` or the suppression file, and must route room events through `chat-client.ts` and the golden; Quarry/browser session owns `BrowserChrome.tsx` (out of scope), and `@quarry/client` is the single documented plane-boundary exception (CI must generate the SDK before any new job installs).

**ZDR clause and regression guards:**
- ZDR: gates persist nothing; fixtures synthetic text. S7 golden carries key names/types of ZDR-bearing frames (`usage.retention_class`, `error` with typed ZDR refusal fields, `retrieval_notice`) so a rename is caught, no content, no new golden-matrix row; S10 is a preventive control on log persistence (no log line carries turn content; complements the runtime test at `agent.rs:8797`); S3 touches ZDR-adjacent [M-live] `ChatMessages.tsx` (existing `*.zdr-refusal.test.ts` must stay green); S7 live probe includes a ZDR turn.
- Audit section 8 strengths: typed ChatEvent taxonomy and byte-verbatim relay (`sse-taxonomy.yml` extended, `payload_contract`, `chat-event-contract.test.ts`, `zdr-contract-sync`; BFF untouched); ZDR by construction (above); multi-tenant runtime and proto-first with breaking-change CI (`buf breaking` red/green proof, S2); rendering/a11y/calm workspace (`chat-accessibility.test.ts` 44 px targets `:26` and live region `:60`, `chat-localization.test.ts`, S5 smoke, no tab-focus change); two loops separate (`cross_service_loop_contract.rs` unchanged); credential isolation (S9/S10 only add checks); honest outcomes (CI_BASELINE records real reds; no gate is "done" without a recorded run).

**Top risks:**
- CI does not run today (3 runs ever, default branch `dev` has no workflows, no PRs) and first runs are red for pre-existing reasons: S1 first, D1(c) owner request, each gate its own job with its own baseline, CI_BASELINE doc with owners, no gate counts as done without a recorded run.
- Hot-file contention with 20+ workstreams (ChatMessages.tsx, `sse_events.rs`, `chat-client.ts`, size-ratchet Rust files): small disjoint hunks, edit-order rules, second lander regenerates golden, ceilings force module extraction.
- Selector gate quality (esquery flags, `:not` with child combinator, false positives, leaks in data not JSX) and golden blind spots (conditional branches, transport events, run events): S4 spike with local-rule fallback, S5 render smoke, baseline absorbs noise, minimal/full sample pair + exhaustiveness guard, S6 covers other emitters; honest limit: gate judges "a word in a string in a UI position", not language.

### WS13 - Approvals v2
**Resolves:** F-t3-006 (Priority, L) | **Owner:** Model Plane: session-core (approval SoR, grants, provenance, audit outbox), execution-core (gate, cause derivation, denial memory, standing release), model-gateway (decide/list projection, scope validation, step-up edge), mp-contracts; Frontend Plane (BFF validation + cards); Control Plane only for the step-up claim (S5) and audit-core wire fixture (S3) | **Wave:** 2 | **Type:** S0/S2/S8 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S1/S3-S7 BUILD-NEW | **Effort:** XL, 37-54 engineer-days (S0 1-2, S1 4-6, S2 4-5, S3 4-6, S4 3-5, S5 7-10, S6 8-12, S7 3-5, S8 3); S1-S4 about 3-4 weeks, independently shippable | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline); WS12 S3 (button locale, edit order) and S4 (copy gate) for S2; WS03 S2 (skip-outbox/consume, soft for S3) and WS03 S3 (release seam, hard for S6); WS05 S1 (declared classes; interim name table until then) for S6, WS05 S5 for S7; WS06 for audit chain | **Gating decisions:** DEC-08 (scope/carrier/eligibility, ADR-0011, gates S6, S7; S1-S5 proceed; WS13-D1..D4, D8), DEC-09 (step-up, gates S5 only; WS13-D5), DEC-10 (auto-posture: S3 labelling ships regardless; WS13-D6), DEC-06 (typed refusal `scope_not_available_in_space`, ships in S6; WS13-D9); local WS13-D7 (denial memory run-scoped by call digest), WS13-D10 (extra `RUN_APPROVAL_DECIDED` envelope: verify capability-core session-review tolerates an unknown `event_type` first; WS16 S3 reports sessionreview, runwatch, Convex, insight-core, taskexec all ignore unknown types). No decision needed to start: S0, S1, S2, S3 (labelling+audit), S4, S8 scaffolding.

**Verified starting point**
- Proto carrier gap: `Approval` fields 1-13 (`MP/proto/model_plane/v1/orchestration.proto:340-381`), `CreateApprovalRequest` 1-11 (`:629-676`) has no context/cause channel, `DecideApprovalRequest` 1-5 (`:738-757`), `ApprovalStateChanged` 1-5 (`:1068-1074`); `ApprovalKind` 0-5 (`:194-207`). Next free at HEAD: Create 12, Approval 14, Decide 6, StateChanged 6, ApprovalKind 6 (WS03 S2 also claims Create 12 and Approval 14/15). The audit's "no proto change first" is false for the carrier (additive proto needed).
- Session-core writes `metadata={reason, step_id}` only (`SC/src/orchestration_grpc.rs:2461-2530`); `decide_approval` (`:2596-2680`) pins `decided_by` to the caller (`:308-315`); `store::decide_approval` (`orchestration_store.rs:755-815`) is one transaction. HTTP `approval_value` (`MG/http_routes.rs:6042-6053`) drops context/cause; SPA `normalizeApproval` (`FE/src/shared/api/orchestration-client.ts:61-74`) reads fields absent on the wire; card headline is the raw `approval.kind` (`FE/src/features/chat/components/ChatMessages.tsx:1887-1930`); BrowserChrome label map is Bokmal-only (`BrowserChrome.tsx:159-170`).
- Five approval creators with hand-written reasons: `pause_for_approval` (`EC/runtime_loop/agent.rs:4469-4504`), `ExecuteStep` pause (`EC/grpc.rs:959`), `resolve_write_approval` (`EC/runtime_loop/mod.rs:1722-1815`), browser gate (`EC/browser_events.rs:238-296`), plus gateway cache builder `MG/approvals.rs:52-80`. `StepOutcome::awaiting_approval()` (`mod.rs:170`) is argument-free, returned from three sites (`:511-515`, `:546-548`, `:557-559`), so the loop cannot tell why it paused.
- Provenance defect not in the audit: under the non-`ask` posture `resolve_write_approval` (`mod.rs:1790-1812`) records `DecideApproval(GRANTED)` with the user's bearer, so a self-approval is indistinguishable from a click. `EVENT_TYPE_APPROVAL_DECIDED = 123` (`MP/proto/model_plane/v1/events.proto:147-148`) is never emitted. No server-side step-up exists (only client-side `PrivacyDataSection.tsx:120-124`); Better Auth `freshAge` 300 s (`CP/auth-core/src/auth/auth.ts:802`), token mint `model-plane-token.controller.ts:126-205` emits no claim.
- Autonomy-rung grant is a different thing (`PlanModeStore.grants`, `MG/coordinator.rs:69-100`); not reused (DEC-08). ZDR: a ZDR run cannot pause (`agent.rs:4551-4560`), decide refused for ZDR callers (`require_non_zdr_durable_mutation`, `http_routes.rs:1494`); so no approval/grant/denial record may exist for ZDR.
- [live-only]: `MG/zdr_posture.rs`, `SC/src/retention_receipt.rs`, `mp-contracts/src/zdr_refusal.rs`, migrations 0044/0045; decide handlers modified live (+249/+237/+207 lines); S3 and S6 edit exactly those paths and must rebase on the landed wave.

**Provides / consumes**
- Provides: WS03 `action_key`/`call_digest`, `ApprovalScope`, `UseApprovalScopeGrant` (standing release for "this run" feeding `ApprovedRelease`), `decision_source`; WS06 flat audit bodies `approval_decided` and `approval_auto_released`; WS14 closed `CauseCode` list (WS14 adds codes through same enum), `approval-copy.ts` scaffolding, `step-up` helper; WS15 enriched `ApprovalStateChanged` and optional `RUN_APPROVAL_DECIDED`; WS16 `cause.code` as urgency input (never text), new approval kinds register in WS16's `kind -> state` map; WS21 `browser_action_risk` + `risk_category` pass-through (per-site consent codes added by WS21 to same enum, DEC-20); WS23/WS24/WS25 `approval_scope_grants` API and `scope_options`; Space session `ApprovalScope`, `decision_source`, `rule_id`+`rule_source` slots (DEC-08, ADR-0011; Space adopts before either lands a proto field; any new `ApprovalKind` for quarantined output takes value 6 via one coordinated proto change).
- Consumes: WS05 `side_effect_class`, `approval_floor`, `policy_source`, `tool_policies`; WS03 `ApprovedRelease`, `ConsumeApproval`, `continuation_mode`, `/continue`, denial closer text; WS12 S3/S4; WS01 live ZDR files and rebuild harness; WS06 chain acceptance; Control Plane step-up endpoint and claim.
- Shared-file rules: `orchestration.proto` append-only one PR per message group; `orchestration_grpc.rs`/`orchestration_store.rs` WS03 S2 before S3/S6, never two authors on `decide_approval`; `agent.rs`/`mod.rs`/`permission/mod.rs` WS05 S1/S3 -> WS03 -> S1/S6 (S1 touches only the three returns and `PendingPause`); `http_routes.rs` WS03 S2 first for `continuation_delivery`; `ChatMessages.tsx`/`orchestration-client.ts` WS12 S3 -> WS03 `continueRun` -> WS13 -> WS14; `action-registry.ts` additive only; audit-core fixtures only (WS06 owns chain).

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR: no approval, cause, grant, denial memory or audit row may exist for a ZDR run (it cannot pause, `agent.rs:4551-4560`; decide/grant/list/revoke refused for ZDR principals with typed `zdr_refusal`); audit body is content-free even for non-ZDR; step-up is identity-only. Waived runs: treated as ZDR for new stores [RECONCILE] DEC-32 (WS13 states only ZDR; apply the DEC-32 default refuse to grants). Gate: golden cases in S8 via `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`.
- Audit section 8 strengths touched: signed fail-closed capability gate (S6 standing release only converts a grantable `Ask` for exact `(tool, action_key)`; `capability_deny_beats_a_grant`, WS03 gate tests, `autonomy_ladder_contract.rs` unchanged); Ask/Do + autonomy ladder (`PlanModeStore`/`coordinator.rs` untouched); ZDR by construction (above); honest outcomes (`the_auto_posture_record_is_labelled_posture_auto`, `unattributed` counter); credential isolation (no password in logs, BFF allowlist); Model proposes/Quarry executes (browser cause pass-through, `..._aborts_the_run_on_denial` unchanged); typed ChatEvent taxonomy (additive fields only); multi-tenant (every query org+user+thread scoped, erasure in `gdpr.rs`); R12 (`a_provider_write_is_never_released_by_a_grant`).

**Top risks**
- Hot-file contention (`orchestration_grpc.rs`, `orchestration_store.rs`, `agent.rs`, `mod.rs`, `ChatMessages.tsx`) with WS02/03/05/12/14/24: edit-first rules, S1 limited to the three return sites, live wave first.
- A standing grant is wider than a click: closed D4 eligibility, tool+operation key, TTL, per-use audit, revoke list, flag default off, Space refusal, never the rung carrier.
- Step-up depends on Control Plane and audit/run-log consumers may reject the new event: S5 isolated behind DEC-09 and `shadow`; `APPROVAL_AUDIT_EVENTS_ENABLED` default off until audit-core fixture accepted; D10 verification before run-log row. Not determined: whether session-core can read verified claims, `MG/auth.rs` claims shape, S7 settings surface, whether `ListApprovals` payload justifies a denied-digest RPC.

---

### WS14 - Finish human-in-the-loop
**Resolves:** F-t3-011 (Priority, Partial, audit effort L) | **Owner:** Model Plane (session-core durable owner of questions, execution-core hosts `ask_user`, model-gateway routes and emits); Frontend Plane (trays, cards, BFF pass-through); Control Plane no change | **Wave:** 2 | **Type:** S1 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S2 FINISH+BUILD; S3-S6 BUILD-NEW; S7 FINISH | **Effort:** XL, about 35-48 engineer-days (S1 4-6, S2 6-8, S3 6-8, S4 8-10, S5 5-7, S6 4-6 optional, S7 2-3) | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline) for S2+ (S1 pure FE layout may start before, needs only live FE hunks); WS04 S4 (durable thread grant, `MODEL_GATEWAY_DURABLE_PLAN_GRANT`, `SetRunModeRequest.approved_plan_id=6`) for S2; WS02 S6 (composer edits to `sse.rs` first); WS03 S3 (stage-1 resume) for S5; WS05 declared classes (stub class acceptable) for S4; WS12 CI gates | **Gating decisions:** DEC-04 (plan approval starts a new Do run; WS14-D1; gates S2), DEC-05 (question store as own `run_questions` table, two delivery values; WS14-D2/D3/D4/D5; gates S3-S5, S6 via D5; SPACE and QRY owners must acknowledge before S4 merges), DEC-32 (waived runs refuse question creation; WS14-D8; S3), DEC-06 (`max_questions=0` for Space-scoped runs), DEC-11 (WS14-D7 approvals owner is session-core, not blocking), WS14-D6 local limits. Free without a decision: S1, S2 (needs DEC-04 recorded), S3 after DEC-05 acknowledged.

**Verified starting point**

- Provides: WS13 tray host (`ComposerTrays` slot for the approval card; fixed priority approval first); WS15 `user_question` control event, `awaiting_input` status, `QuestionAsked`/`QuestionAnswered`/`RunPausedForInput`; WS16 content-free triggers (question pending, plan awaiting approval, with `expires_at`) and `selectDockedTrays` as the single FE source of "what is waiting on me"; WS03 `PendingPause{kind: input}` consumer, `AgentResume.question_id/answer_json`, `ask_budget` in `LoopFoldV1.extensions`; WS04 `BindGrantToRun` appended to `ThreadAutonomy`; WS05 `ask_user` class `interaction`; WS20 `max_questions=0` for unattended runs; WS21/WS25 (F-t3-045) `CreateRunQuestion` + `QuestionCard` primitive; Space session shared `QuestionCard`, `ConversationNode{kind:'question'}`, `run_questions`, `delivery:"next_turn"` as `request_input` (owner-only answers until `authorized_actor`; Space must not add resumable kinds to cold worker; typed `secret` item kind refused today).
- Consumes: WS01 live ZDR wave and compose recipe; WS04 S4 grant columns and flag; WS03 `/continue`, `AgentResume`, fold, parking, flags; WS02 `build_run_agent_request` edits first and `RunAgentRequest` numbering; WS05 classes; WS12 gates.
- Edit-first: `sse.rs`/`http_routes.rs`/`sse_events.rs` live set, WS02, WS04 S4 before S2; `agent.rs` WS03 S6 refactor first; orchestration protos append-only; session-core handlers WS03 S2 first; FE chat files live hunks first, S1 layout owner, WS09 virtualisation keeps trays outside the virtual list.

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR: no tool, question or plan exists under ZDR (agentic refused, ZDR chat has no tool loop); three new routes refuse ZDR callers with existing `agent_run`/`approval_continuation` features; `max_questions=0` for ZDR/waived [RECONCILE] DEC-32 (waived = refuse, WS14-D8); stored content is model-authored prompt and user answer, same class as `plans` and `messages`; erased by `ON DELETE CASCADE`. Gate: `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh` golden cases in S2-S4.
- Audit section 8 strengths: signed fail-closed gate (`trusted_capability_id("ask_user")` must be `Some`, never in `is_risky_call`, no path without `EvaluatePolicy`); Ask/Do + autonomy ladder (rung still via `AutonomyEscalation::request`, `autonomy_ladder_contract.rs` passes, stale-grant test); two loops separate (only `user_question` crate shared, `cross_service_loop_contract.rs`); honest outcomes (expiry is typed `question_expired`, failed execute keeps visible retry); typed ChatEvent taxonomy (`gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check`, control-event rule test); credential isolation (validator rejects `secret`, card warning); calm/accessible (aria-live polite, no focus steal, 375 px checks); multi-tenant (owner-bound RPCs, org filter, real-PG tests).

**Top risks**
- Hot-file collisions (`sse.rs`, `http_routes.rs`, `agent.rs`, `ChatMessages.tsx` touched by 6-8 streams plus uncommitted live work): new files (`ask.rs`, `user_question.rs`, `ComposerTrays.tsx`, `QuestionCard.tsx`), single call-ins, edit-first table.
- Over-asking degrades UX: budget 3, description rules, flag off by default, 20-prompt eval before S7 flips, per-org `max_questions=0` later (WS10/WS25).
- Authority widening via plan continuation and dependency slip: DEC-04 new run, server-side bind with plan-id match, single-use CAS, no client rung; S2 degrades to `next_message` when WS04 flag is off; S5 optional for first release; get Space acknowledgement of one vocabulary before S4 merges.

### WS15 - Typed run lifecycle events and status cues
**Resolves:** F-t3-049 (Recommended, L) | **Owner:** Model Plane (model-gateway emits; execution-core and session-core carry the Do-loop signal) and Frontend Plane (renders; owns thread-status derivation [RECONCILE] WS16-D8 / DEC-11 says WS16 owns the enum and precedence, WS15 consumes one function; see 2.6 row 17) | **Wave:** 2 | **Type:** S2/S4/S7 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S1/S3/S5/S6/S8/S9 BUILD-NEW | **Effort:** L, core S1-S7 about 20-30 engineer-days (S1 3-4, S2 3-5, S3 5-7, S4 1-2, S5 4-6, S6 3-4, S7 1-2); optional S8 2-3, S9 5-8 | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline) for every slice; WS09 S2 (`renderLive`/`nodeKey`) before S5; WS02 first on `sse.rs`; WS03 S8 for S7; WS12 S4/S7 gates; WS16 enum for S6 | **Gating decisions:** DEC-11 (WS15-D3 thread-status vocabulary and precedence; gates S6; precedence order is cheap to change), DEC-17 (WS15-D7 trigger-kind chip deferred until run-level `origin` exists; not scheduled); local: WS15-D1 (reverse the in-code "log line and nothing user-facing" Do-compaction decision at `sse.rs:9862-9873`; new ledger entry, gates S2 Do-run part only), D2 (post-hoc provider wait only), D4 (S8), D5 (S9 hand to WS23), D6, D8. Free without decision: S1, S2 (non-Do part), S3, S4, S5, S6 (apart from the D3 order).

**Verified starting point**
- ChatEvent: LIVE `MG/sse_events.rs` `enum ChatEvent` `:144` (23 variants; WT 21; `RetrievalNotice` `:368` and `ZdrError` `:384` live-only), `family()` `:410` exhaustive, control events `:430-443`, `name()` `:460`, `payload()` `:490`, `RichEventSink` `:767` (`emit(&self)` `:1026`). `done` is a transport frame, not a variant; terminal variants `Stopped`/`Error`; SPA already normalises all three (`FE/src/shared/chat/verevon-ui-events.ts:316-318`), so a `turn_end` event is unnecessary. Taxonomy gate `MP/scripts/gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py` (`VARIANT_ARM` `:81-83`, `NON_CHATEVENT_CONSUMER_CASES` `:43-49`), CI `.github/workflows/sse-taxonomy.yml`; HEAD fails the gate (doc lacks `plan_proposed`, `source_scope_applied`), LIVE passes with 28 rows; `PYTHONUTF8=1` mandatory on Windows. BFF relay `FE/apps/gateway/src/domains/chat/streams.rs:113` is a raw byte-level proxy (the audit's `streams.rs:99-109` cite is wrong); AG-UI adapter `ag_ui.rs` is not verbatim (`map_upstream_frame` `:752`, CUSTOM list `:883-906`, test `:1263`).
- Silent behaviours, all log-only (LIVE): context overflow shed `MG/sse.rs:2931-2984` (`MAX_CONTEXT_LENGTH_RETRIES=3` `:4578`); compaction truncation fallback `sse.rs:5609`, planned/reused `budget_compacted_thread` `:5621`; skill guidance truncated `fetch_skill_context` `:5662` (`:5758-5766`); Do-run `compaction_triggered=5` (`MP/proto/model_plane/v1/execution.proto:237`, read `sse.rs:9874-9880`); tool retry in `EC/runtime_loop/agent.rs:1916-1964` (`retry.rs`, `MAX_TOOL_ATTEMPTS=3`) and `MG/tool_loop.rs:4022-4064` (`dispatch_audited_tool` `:3947`, mirror `MG/tool_retry.rs`); inference retry `inference-core/src/provider/fallback.rs:1526` completes all passes before returning, reported only on the final chunk as `ProviderTiming.attempts/failed_attempts_ms/retry_wait_ms` (`inference.proto:411/415/419`); `MG/turn_timing.rs:248-261` forwards only `attempts`. Audit correction: post-hoc retry visibility needs no inference-core proto change (fields exist, S4); a live signal does (deferred, D2).
- The Do loop reaches the chat stream only through session-core: `RunAgent` is unary (`execution.proto:34`), events flow `RecordOrchestrationEvent` (`orchestration.proto:122`) -> broadcast (`SC/orchestration_grpc.rs:3192`, `event_run_id` `:1819`) -> `StreamRunEvents` -> gateway `follow_agentic_run` closure (`MG/sse.rs:9677-9690`, today only `step_update`); Do-path frames are not resume-buffered.

**Provides / consumes**
- Provides: WS12 four new ChatEvent names (`retry_scheduled`, `compaction`, `context_change`, `run_parked`) with `payload_contract` sample rows and one SPA `case` each; WS09 single `lifecycle-notices` node per turn (no `message.delta` event, WS15-D8); WS16 `deriveThreadStatus` token set and signals (`run_parked`, `run_paused_for_approval`, `awaiting_approval`), local `lastSeenAt` as placeholder for server read-state; WS03 typed home for `run_parked`; WS27 `lifecycle_notice` token module (cost clamp/budget notice = new `context_change.change` token, not a new variant); WS11 effort clamp surface via token `effort_clamped`; WS23 incident vocabulary; Space session `thread-status.ts` and lifecycle renderer via `createConversationNodeRegistry`.
- Consumes: WS01 S0; WS02 `runs.metadata.run_context` states -> `context_change{change:"run_context_skipped", layer, state}` (token defined here, producer with WS02 S6); WS03 `RunParked`/`RunResumed` tags 24/25, raw frame, `RunDetail.resumable/park_reason`; WS04 `queued_input` additive `ids`; WS12 S4/S7/S6; WS09 S2; WS16 server run-state enum.
- Edit order: `sse_events.rs` WS01 S0, WS12 S7, WS02/WS04, then variants in arrival order (WS13/WS14 before WS15); `sse.rs`/`tool_loop.rs` WS02 first; protos append-only; `chat-client.ts`/`ChatMessages.tsx`/`CoreSidebar.tsx` WS09 S2 first, add-only; `agent.rs` WS03 first.

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR: every payload is counts and closed tokens, ephemeral stream frames, no store in S1-S7; Do runs are never ZDR; chat loop under ZDR has no tool loop so no retry frame; error text never leaves `transient_reason`; S8 persistence refused under ZDR (persisted for persistent and `zdr_waived` as counts only [RECONCILE] DEC-32: waived counts as ZDR for NEW durable stores, so S8 should refuse waived unless the owner rules otherwise); S9 persists nothing for ZDR callers. Gate: `tests/lifecycle_event_contract.rs` (payload key allowlist, `Compaction` has no `summary`, ZDR turn builds identical frames) plus S8 `zdr_turn_persists_no_lifecycle_notes`.
- Audit section 8 strengths: typed ChatEvent taxonomy and byte-verbatim relay (taxonomy gate, WS12 S7 golden, `streams.rs` diff empty, BFF `cargo test --manifest-path apps/gateway/Cargo.toml --all-targets`, `plain_path_emits_only_control_events`); honest outcomes (cues report only observed events, `truncated` never shown as summarized, counts are `Option`); two loops separate (`tool_retry_contract.rs` extended to the reason table); context management (`overflow_contract.rs`, `skill_budget_contract.rs`, `compaction_parity_contract.rs`, `context_recovery_contract.rs` green, byte-identical stream flag off); calm accessible workspace (one polite sentence per notice, no focus steal); capability gate/Quarry/credential isolation untouched (retry never changes dispatch, risky calls still never retried).

**Top risks**
- Contention in `sse_events.rs`, `sse.rs`, `chat-client.ts`, `ChatMessages.tsx` with 5-20 workstreams and about 1000 uncommitted live lines: start only after WS01-S0, fenced add-only blocks, logic in NEW `MG/lifecycle_events.rs`, second lander regenerates golden and doc.
- Events ship before the SPA renders them or flood a calm UI: flag default off until S5, one node per turn, cap 20, de-dup, transient retry cue.
- ZDR leak through helpful detail and Windows false "stale" gate: closed-token allowlist test; run gate with `PYTHONUTF8=1`, use `--lib`, diff failing sets against HEAD; Do-loop retry frames are not resume-buffered (accepted, same as `step_update`; Trace remains authoritative).

### WS16 - Attention model for long-running agents
**Resolves:** F-t3-015 (Priority, XL) | **Owner:** Model Plane / session-core (closed state enum, derivation, seen marker, `RUN_ATTENTION` outbox event, live attention hub); Application Plane / notification-core (types, channel matrix, native delivery, Web Push, VAPID sender); Frontend Plane (rail, badge, shell-stream client, PWA files, BFF routes) | **Wave:** 2 | **Type:** S2/S4/S7 FINISH+BUILD mix; S1/S3/S5/S6 BUILD-NEW | **Effort:** XL, about 45-55 engineer-days (S1 6, S2 5.5, S3 4.5, S4 9, S5 9, S6 10.5, S7 3.5); two engineers about 8 weeks | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline); WS01 S2 (outbox publisher fix + migration 0043 deployed; otherwise outbox rows sit undelivered, postmortem 0002); WS03 S8 (terminalization/park changes, parked fold predicate); WS13/WS14 (approval kinds; WS14 supplies kind 6); WS12 S1/S4/S6; WS17 soft | **Gating decisions:** DEC-11 (WS16-D1 session-core owns enum/derivation/typed events, WS16-D8 WS16 owns thread-status precedence; gates S1; matrix edit MASTER L129 recorded in ledger), DEC-38 (WS16-D2 native adapter in notification-core, D3 per-user stream hosted in session-core hub with BFF relay, D4 VAPID in notification-core with Application Plane bootstrap keys, D5 no push for ZDR in v1, D6 waiting states always notify; gates S4, S5, S6; SPACE must confirm D3 and the shared allowlist); local WS16-D7 (server `attention_seen_at` for persistent threads, client-local for ZDR/temporary), D9 (leave dead orchestrator typed publisher, record in ledger), D10 (rail strip, not replacement). Free without decision: S1, S2, S3, S5 (D3 confirm before S5 merge); S4 and S6 need DEC-38.

**Verified starting point**
- Run state is a free string and thinner than the audit assumes: `runs.status` TEXT default `'queued'` (`SC/migrations/0001_init.sql:33`); only writers `terminalization.rs:999`, `grpc.rs:12360`, `run_service_grpc.rs:814`; nothing sets `awaiting_approval`, a paused run stays `running` in Postgres (the audit's `execution-core::RunStatus` seed `EC/state.rs:15-40` is in-memory per replica). Derivation must read `approvals` (`status='requested'`, `0003_orchestration_tables.sql:135-161`) and `plans` (`status='proposed'`, `:29-44`). `ThreadSummary` ends at `persona_enabled=14` (`sessions.proto` ~1017, next free 15); list SQL `SC/src/grpc.rs:5405-5411` ([L] ~5576); relayed in `MG/src/http_routes.rs:7680` (mapper `:7907`, route `/v1/threads` `:144`), `GW/domains/chat/history.rs:64,146-147,407`, `FE/src/shared/api/chat-client.ts:733` (normalizer `2295-2315`).
- FE vocabulary duplicated: `chatRunStatusChip` (`FE/src/features/core/components/CoreSidebar.tsx:867-897`) and `activeStatuses/attentionStatuses` (`FE/src/features/chat/lib/chat-organization.ts:47-48`, filter `:55`); no "Waiting for you / Unseen / Settled" rail sections exist.
- Typed events: session-core emits `ApprovalStateChanged`/`RunPausedForApproval` in-process only (`SC/src/orchestration_grpc.rs:2461`, emit `2555-2585`, `broadcast_event` `:1726`, NATS bridge consumes only). Audit text wrong here: orchestrator-core's typed publisher (`orchestration.New`, `NewRunPausedForApproval`) has no production caller (`cmd/main.go:305` discards it), so fixing `nats.conf` alone yields no typed events. The session-core outbox is the right home: `session_audit_outbox` accepts `mp.v1.run.%.event` (`SC/migrations/0016_...sql:26-34`), `classify_outbox_subject`/`check_outbox_authority` (`audit_publisher.rs:69-123`), publish grant `nats.conf:44`, capability-core subscribe `nats.conf:88-96`, stream `MODEL_PLANE_RUN_EVENTS` (`nats-provisioner/main.go:44-50`); only enqueuer today is `RUN_COMPLETED` (`learning_events.rs:185-293`), withheld for ZDR runs (`:263`), so a ZDR run currently produces no run-watch notification. Unknown `event_type`s ignored by Convex mirror (`AP/convex-core/convex/nats.ts:1007-1010`), insight-core (`agent_subscriber.go:126`), taskexec (`CC/internal/taskexec/completion_consumer.go:95`), sessionreview (`CC/internal/sessionreview/trigger.go:56`), runwatch (`consumer.go:202-205`).
- Notification path: run-watch consumer binds durable `capability-core-run-watch-notify` (`CC/internal/runwatch/consumer.go:64-76,163-174`), maps only `RUN_COMPLETED/RUN_FAILED`, idempotency key `run-watch:{org}:{run}:{user}:{event_type}` (`:253`), sets no `retention_mode`, never reads envelope `zdr` (`go/pkg/envelope/envelope.go:66-96`); allowlist `isNotificationTypeAuthorized` (`NC/internal/http/handlers.go:199-218`); channel rows migration `NC/migrations/013_register_run_watch_notifications.up.sql` (latest is 014). Latent bug: `Service.IsNotificationEnabled` (`NC/internal/preferences/preferences.go:144-155`) is per type not per channel, gating `Accept` (`NC/internal/notification/service.go:324-335,446-460`): email off suppresses in-app. Default stack cannot deliver: `NOTIFICATION_DELIVERY_MODE` `novu|disabled` default `disabled` (`NC/internal/config/config.go:62,84-105`; `AP/docker-compose.yml:914`); whether the running stack uses Novu is not determined (record it in S4 evidence). No native Web Push sender or subscription table; `AccountSettingsPage.tsx:102` stores a dead `pushNotifications` boolean.
- Shell polling is the navbar resource (`FE/src/features/core/components/CoreShell.tsx:35`, 60 s/180 s timer `:59-84`, `GW/domains/navbar.rs:150-195`); no per-user stream. **Provides / consumes**
- Provides: WS15 `AttentionState` enum + `precedence()` (the status half of F-t3-049; WS15 must not define a second vocabulary) and `attention_unseen`; WS14 `NEEDS_INPUT` mapping for reserved `ApprovalKind` 6 and `modelplane.needs_input` (deep link only, never decide from a toast); WS13 any new approval kind appears in Waiting by registering in the one `kind -> state` function, sweeper needs no producer edit; WS20 off-surface approvals via `modelplane.needs_approval`; WS03 reserved `AttentionReason 4 = parked` (WS03 supplies the fold predicate); WS17 post-step enrichment (no SQL edit) and non-paged `/v1/attention`; WS12 stream-frame fixture, `run-attention-envelope.json`, ACL contract test; WS01 no ACL/CHECK change; Space session one enum, one allowlist, one badge counter, one SW (Space signals map `approval_needed`=`needs_approval`, `agent_awaiting`=`needs_input`, `agent_done`=`completed`; `mention`/`reply`/`invite` Space-owned; Space adds no states; Space aggregate joins as a second BFF upstream `source:"space"`); Quarry F-t2-033/038 consent notifications reuse the three types (browser human-assist pauses must be approval rows).
- Consumes: WS01 S0 + S2; WS03 S8; WS04 none blocking; WS13/WS14 approval rows with kinds; WS12 S1/S4/S6; WS17 soft.
- Edit order: `sessions.proto` `ThreadSummary` 15+ WS01 S0 first, append-only, S1 uses new `attention.proto` for everything else; `SC/src/grpc.rs`/`terminalization.rs`/`orchestration_grpc.rs` live set first, S1 one post-step call, S5 pokes last; `CoreSidebar.tsx`/`use-chat-controller.ts`/`chat-client.ts` live set first, small hunks; `NC/internal/http/handlers.go` single allowlist; `nginx.conf`/`index.html`/`public/` WS16 S6 owns PWA files (Space F-t1-035 slice B delivered here); `GW/domains/notifications.rs`/`main.rs`/`domains/mod.rs` additive.

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR: persists `attention_seen_at/run_id` on a persistent thread row (ids + timestamp, erased with the thread; `gdpr.rs:431-432` erases outbox with org); `MarkThreadSeen` refused for ZDR/temporary callers (client-local state); sweeper never enqueues for ZDR runs (read `managed_run_terminalization_outbox.zdr`, `waived` treated as not-persistent, consistent with DEC-32; confirm with Z owners); `RUN_ATTENTION` consumer fails closed on nil/true `zdr`; notification rows carry type, recipient, opaque ids, generic copy only (no title, tool name, args, reason); stream frames ephemeral with no replay buffer or frame-body logging; no push for ZDR threads in v1 (WS16-D5); SW and subscription rows hold no conversation data. Gates: `SC/tests/attention_zdr_matrix.rs` over `zdr-posture-matrix.json`, `TestAttentionNotificationsAreContentFree`, sender refuses `retention_mode` `zdr`.
- Audit section 8 strengths: ZDR by construction (above); honest outcomes (terminal states read never written; derive tests cancelled->idle and expired approval ignored; S3 never touches `terminalization.rs` transactions); typed ChatEvent taxonomy + verbatim relay (stream is a separate SSE, `gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check` unchanged, BFF relay allowlist-free); multi-tenant runtime (cross-tenant tests S1/S5/S6, subscription erasure on org deletion); credential isolation (VAPID private key only in notification-core env via bootstrap, encrypted columns, no key in BFF/SPA); two loops separate and signed gate untouched; calm accessible workspace (a11y assertions, sections hidden when empty, push suppressed when focused).

**Top risks**
- Platform preconditions unmet (live wave uncommitted, outbox publisher fix not deployed, notification delivery `disabled` by default): gate S3/S4 evidence on WS01 S0/S2, record delivery mode, native types (DEC-38) remove the Novu dependency.
- Notification noise and ZDR leakage: only waiting states notify, per-user matrix, coalescing, SW suppression when focused, completed/failed opt-in (WS16-D6); nothing emitted for ZDR, fail-closed consumers, content-free payload tests.
- Hot-file conflicts (`sessions.proto`, `grpc.rs`, `CoreSidebar.tsx`, `use-chat-controller.ts`, `nginx.conf`) and browser/PWA variance: new `attention.proto`, post-step enrichment, sweeper instead of call-site edits, edit-order rules; server-driven capability, production-build e2e, bootstrap-generated keys, rotation documented, Safari/iOS untested unless run.

### WS17 - Conversation history foundation (paging, search, recall, fork, versions)
**Resolves:** F-t3-012 | **Owner:** Model Plane / session-core (single writer: index, tree, fork); model-gateway owns routes and the read-only recall tool; Frontend Plane owns BFF routes, sidebar, action contract, version switcher | **Wave:** W2 (S1/S2/S6/S7 free early) | **Type:** S1, S2 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S3-S9 BUILD-NEW | **Effort:** XL, ~40-58 eng-days serial (S1 4-6, S2 3-5, S3 5-7, S4 3-4, S5 3-5, S6 4-6, S7 3-4, S8 8-12, S9 6-9) | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline); WS09 S8 (paging fields, D6); WS04 (replica-safe run state, S8); WS07 S1 (memory state, S5); WS26 (screening quality, S5) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-29 (OD19 tree/index/ledger: gates S3 ledger+matrix, S8, S9; S1-S7 start without it); DEC-32 (waived content: S3 predicate, S5, S6; ship S3 UI-only first); DEC-30 (S5 enabling only); DEC-11 (unread definition, S7; S1-S6 free); DEC-39 (proto/migration numbers, every slice). Free now: S1, S2, S6, S7 (S3 UI-only first).

**Verified starting point:**
- `messages` has no org/user column (ownership = `messages JOIN threads`); single production insert `append_message_in_tx` `SC/src/grpc.rs:2430` (insert `:2569`); migrations run at boot via `sqlx::migrate!` (`SC/src/store.rs:34`, sqlx 0.8.6). Readers to protect: `list_conversation` `grpc.rs:5216-5330`, `load_thread_messages` `:3905-3925`, `list_threads` laterals `:5391-5404`, slot checks `:2460,:2479`, `SC/src/dreaming.rs:1226-1246`, `memory_control.rs:32`; cross-service via `ListConversation` (`MG/src/sse.rs:5219-5300`, `tool_loop.rs:3225-3283`, `GO/capability-core/internal/sessionreview/transcript.go:15-19`).
- **Audit stale: the 80-thread cap is also the authorization source.** `ListThreads` has no cursor (`sessions.proto:947-974`, max field 7); defaults 80/200 (`grpc.rs:68-69,148-153`); BFF `MAX_THREADS=80` (`BFF/chat/history.rs:21,335-352`); `get_thread_transcript` (`:209`), `save_thread` (`:261`), `delete_thread` (`:299`) `find()` in that capped list, so thread 81+ cannot open; FE fails closed on "not in list" (`FE/features/chat/components/use-chat-controller.ts:1051,1838`). By-id read is part of S1.
- **Audit half-stale:** pin is wired end to end (`history.rs:79-95,415`, `CoreSidebar.tsx:436-447`); archive RPCs + MG routes exist (`MG/src/http_routes.rs:147-152`) but no BFF/FE surface and no archived listing.
- **ZDR stale:** `authorize_operation` refuses any non-`:read` scope for ZDR credentials (`SC/src/auth.rs:198-203`); ZDR turns write no messages (test `grpc.rs:12496`), so `messages` has no ZDR content; live question is `retention_class='zdr_waived'` content (`[live-only]` migration 0044; R17).
- Versions "rejection" is a spec, not a ledger entry: `MP/docs/CHAT_RESUME_AND_VERSIONS_SPEC.md:82-130` (flat-marker design rejected; persisted versions deferred); reversal = amend spec + new ledger entry (DEC-29). Client-only versions `FE/features/chat/lib/chat-versions.ts:1-22`; regenerate today appends a duplicate pair (`use-chat-controller.ts:3549-3550`).
- Tools: `reattach_context` `tool_loop.rs:3225-3283`; `conversation_tool_allowed` positive list `:4214`; trust classes `MG/src/moderation.rs:206-262`; audit category table `MG/src/audit.rs:18,44-45`.
- Live-only to land first: migrations 0044/0045 + `migrations/revert/`, `retention_receipt.rs`, `[M]` `SC/src/grpc.rs` (+861), `auth.rs` (housekeeping gate `[L] :295`), `dreaming.rs`, `sessions.proto` (+42), `MG http_routes.rs/tool_loop.rs/sse.rs`, FE `chat-client.ts`, `ChatSidebarOrganizer.tsx`, `CoreSidebar.tsx`, `ChatMessages.tsx`, `use-chat-controller.ts`, ZDR tests `MG/tests/zdr_posture_matrix.rs`, `zdr_feature_table.rs`, `zdr_refusal_inventory.rs`, `MP/api/contracts/zdr-refusals.json`. Not determined: waived+persistent thread mixing; Quarry artifact id authorization on fork; FE capability read path; local `chat-thread-history` cap; `MG/src/grpc.rs:2314` `ListThreadMessages` second front.

**Common to all slices.** Branch from the WS01-S0 commit. Each migration gets a mirror in `SC/migrations/revert/`. Proto append-only; new messages at file end; `buf generate` (checked-in `MP/go/gen/model_plane/v1/sessions*.pb.go` + python stubs); `buf breaking "apps/Model Plane/proto" --against "apps/Model Plane/proto/baseline.binpb"` clean. Commands: `cd "apps/Model Plane/rust" && cargo test -p session-core --all-targets`; `cargo test -p model-gateway --all-targets`; `cargo fmt --all -- --check`; real-PG `DATABASE_URL=<disposable> cargo test -p session-core --bin session-core <name> -- --ignored --test-threads=1`; BFF `cargo test --manifest-path "apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/apps/gateway/Cargo.toml" --all-targets`; FE `cd "apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3" && pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm vitest run <files>`. Evidence = rebuilt images on a running stack: `bash "apps/Model Plane/scripts/compose.sh" up -d --no-deps --build session-core model-gateway` and `docker compose -f "apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/docker-compose.yml" up -d --no-deps --build gateway` (service key not verified; BFF rebuild restarts the frontend container). Shared operator script: NEW `MP/scripts/tests/history-foundation-proof.sh` (style of WS09 `transcript-paging-proof.sh`), extended per slice.

**Provides / consumes:**
- WS09: consumes its `ListConversation` paging (`limit=6, before_message_id=7`, `has_more=2`); WS17 adds `include_inactive=8` and `SessionMessage` 7-10 after them, never renaming (DEC-29/D6); by-id read removes the BFF 80-list dependency for the transcript route.
- WS16: provides `starred_at/last_assistant_at/last_read_at`, `ThreadSummary.unread/starred/archived_at`, one unread definition (DEC-11); WS16 must not add a second.
- WS18: provides `ForkThread` core, lineage columns, waived/Space refusal rules; Space-target copy is WS18's. WS18 consumes `ListThreads(space_id)`/`getSpaceThreads` for the project list.
- Space session: `SearchMessages` (owner + audience-ceiling SQL), `threads.last_activity_at`, `ListThreads` cursor, rule that Space threads never use `parent_message_id`/`on_active_path`; Space adds BFF per-hit re-auth, room UI and a revised-away-originals clause to `search_sql` (WS17 lands first; first to land owns `append_message_in_tx`; their non-owner append must keep `last_activity_at/last_assistant_at`).
- WS07: consumes `memory_control::resolve` for recall (D5). WS02: provides nothing; WS02's assumed "durable summary store from WS17" is NOT in this stream (section 2.6 row 6: three stores, three lifetimes). WS12: new `chat-history.ts` contract + tests.
- Consumes: WS01-S0; WS09 S8 (if WS17 lands first it defines identical fields); WS04; WS07 S1; WS26.
- Edit order: `sessions.proto`/`grpc.rs` WS01-S0 first then first-to-start, append at file end; `dreaming.rs` WS07 S1 first (two single-clause edits); MG `http_routes/sse/tool_loop/session_flow` thin diffs, new logic in new files; FE hot files WS09 S2 first, additive hunks, new components in new files with one-line mounts. [RECONCILE] WS17 D4 (owner search includes waived threads; recall/fork refuse) vs WS02 D5 (waived = persistent like Ask): settled by DEC-32. [RECONCILE] unread/status vocabulary with WS15: DEC-11 / section 2.6 row 17. [RECONCILE] Space revision entries vs version tree: section 2.6 row 12.

**ZDR clause and regression guards:**
- ZDR turns write no thread; every slice that writes (S6 fork, S8 `SetActiveLeaf`, S9 branch/rewind) is refused for ZDR credentials (MG 412 `zdr_durable_mutation_forbidden`, SC `session:write`); S1/S3/S4 are `:read` and allowed, persist nothing new; S2/S7 housekeeping (archive, star, unread) allowed under every posture, text fields still refused. Waived content: owner search includes, recall excludes, fork refuses (DEC-32). Temporary/ZDR chats keep client-only versions (test). Golden rows in `[live-only]` `zdr_posture_matrix.rs`/`zdr_feature_table.rs`/`zdr_refusal_inventory.rs`; `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`.
- Audit section 8 strengths: ZDR by construction (golden rows above); GDPR erasure/single writer (`search_after_delete_thread_finds_nothing`, `org_erase_removes_hits`, fork independence, `gdpr.rs` order test ~`:680`, `memory_erasure.rs` pin); two loops separate (recall only in gateway read-only loop, `recall_is_not_offered_in_conversation_only_turns`, `cross_service_loop_contract.rs`); context management (`context_recovery_contract.rs`, three `reattach_context_*` tests `tool_loop.rs` ~`:12456-12534`, compaction reads active path); memory provenance (Dreaming skips copied/inactive rows); honest outcomes (typed `history_search_disabled`, recall `unavailable` with reason, typed conflicts); typed ChatEvent taxonomy (no new SSE variant); accessibility (listbox tests, no `innerHTML`).

**Top risks:**
- A history reader missed by the tree silently mixes branches into prompts/learning/Dreaming: flag-independent `on_active_path`, source-scan allowlist test, dark flag, randomized invariant test, capability-core test.
- Search cost across tenants (GIN has no org key) and backfill/`CONCURRENTLY` at boot: owner filter in SQL, 2 s timeout, EXPLAIN gate on 20-tenant corpus, side-table or `btree_gin` fallback before enabling, batch-variant backfill, test on populated disposable DB.
- Prompt injection and waived-content laundering via recall/fork, plus hot-spot collisions (`sessions.proto`, `grpc.rs`, `use-chat-controller.ts`, `ChatMessages.tsx`) and migration numbering: `PriorConversation` trust class with screening, owner-only, current thread excluded, waived/zdr excluded (DEC-32 ruling needed), WS01-S0 first, additive hunks, integrator numbering.

### WS18 - Spaces as the chat project
**Resolves:** F-t3-013 | **Owner:** Frontend Plane (BFF + SPA) with Model Plane (instruction layering, retrieval receipt, Do-run knowledge scope); Application Plane (Convex) storage only; Control stays membership/decision authority (matrix rules 9-10, `apps/master-ownership-matrix.md:165-166`; the card's "Application Plane membership" is wrong) | **Wave:** W2 (S0 and S2 can start in W1) | **Type:** S0, S1, S2 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S3, S4, S5 BUILD-NEW | **Effort:** L, ~24-34 eng-days (S0 1, S1 2-3, S2 3-4, S3 4-5, S4 8-12, S5 6-9) | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (live `sse.rs`, `sse_events.rs`, `zdr_features.rs`, FE live hunks); WS02 S4+S5+S6 (S1, S5); WS03 and WS02 S7 (`agent.rs`, S5); WS12 (new FE files) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-28 (OD19 container, D2 opt-in, D3 eligibility, D4 audience confirm; gates S4 and S2 merge acknowledgement; S1/S2/S3/S5 build without it; also WS02 S6); DEC-06 (D5 sandbox tools refused in room projects; D7 follow-up retrieval Space-scoped fail-closed, needs Space + Control acknowledgement before S2 merge); WS18-D6/D8/D9 local (D8 TTL gates S5 enablement). Free now: S1, S2 (build), S3, S5 (build).

**Verified starting point:**
- Chat sidebar "projects" are localStorage folders: `FE/features/chat/lib/chat-organization.ts:2-34` (cap 100), `FE/shared/actions/chat-organization.ts:1-27` ("browser-only... never grant access"), mounted by `ChatSidebarOrganizer.tsx`. /chat has no Space entry by design: `use-chat-controller.ts:2274-2288`; `scopedThreadRefs` for resume only. Chat list = `origin='chat'` by stored fact (`SC/migrations/0034_thread_origin.sql:12-13,44-47`; `ListThreadsRequest.origin/space_id` `sessions.proto:947-964`, SQL `grpc.rs:5418`). The "reversal" is smaller than the audit implies: a Space-scoped thread is already an ordinary session-core thread.
- Space already has instructions (`FE/features/spaces/components/SpacePage.tsx:626-729`, PATCH gated editor/manager/owner `BFF/spaces.rs:3720`), knowledge panel (:434), multi-thread room (`getSpaceThreads` `FE/shared/api/spaces-client.ts:157`, `getSpaceThreadTranscript :672`, `listSpaces :124`, `createRoom :945`). Space kinds `personal|room|project|case` (`CP/user-core/migrations/017_space_authority.up.sql:20`); BFF refuses `project`/`case` (`spaces.rs:2865-2879`); Convex allows exactly ONE personal Space (`AP/convex-core/convex/spaces.ts:154-170,245`), so multiple projects = rooms.
- Instructions: BFF stamps `org_instructions`/`space_instructions` on every body (`BFF/spaces.rs:4291-4324`; role gate `resolve_space_role :3728-3763`; order in `BFF/chat/streams.rs:55-90`), so Do bodies already carry `space_instructions`; gateway composes only on the inline path (`MG/src/sse.rs:6243-6296`, used `:1747`); agentic branch `:1381-1447` passes `agentic_space_id` but no instruction; `RunAgentRequest` highest tag 17 (`execution.proto:76-211`). WS02 S4/S6 own the fix (`run_context=18`, `AuthoredLayers`, `MODEL_GATEWAY_RUN_CONTEXT`).
- **Three gaps the audit missed.** A: follow-up turns (append branch `BFF/spaces.rs:99-190`) carry `space_append_context` with NO retrieval token; gateway reads the token from `req.space_context` only (`sse.rs:1014,1628,1650,2012`), so every follow-up retrieves org-wide. B: Do `knowledge_search` uses the user bearer only (`EC/src/knowledge_tools.rs:105-155`; call sites `runtime_loop/mod.rs:1048,1221`). C: Do admission in a Space run demands the sandbox bearer (`EC/src/grpc.rs:1224-1235`) but the Space capability decision requires `KindPersonal` (`CP/user-core/internal/spaces/space_capability_decision.go:138` (`validatePersonalAuthority`; `KindPersonal` is defined at `CP/user-core/internal/spaces/authority.go:17`)), so `code_interpreter` refuses in room-kind projects.
- Receipt: `source_scope_applied` exists (`MG/src/sse_events.rs:199,359,406,491`), emitted only when `retrieval_scope != Auto` (`sse.rs:2199-2208`); SPA accepts only `knowledge_only|web_only` (`chat-client.ts:1324-1334`); `retrieval_notice` is live-only and means a reduced answer: do not reuse. Audit line cites are live-tree numbers; `spaces.rs:4281-4330` is `inject_authored_instructions`, not `resolve_space_role`.

- Shared-room audience exposure from /chat and Do in room projects losing sandbox tools (gap C): org room excluded, creator-only rooms default, audience chip + first-send confirm, server re-decision every turn; D5 visible notice, personal Space unaffected, full fix tracked to Space session's Control decision family.
- Do retrieval-decision expiry mid-run and merge conflicts in live-modified hot files (`sse.rs`, `use-chat-controller.ts`, `ChatMessages.tsx`, `agent.rs`): typed degrade never unscoped fallback, TTL measured before enabling S5; WS01-S0 first, new modules, named-line-only diffs.

### WS19 - Verified Office deliverables
**Resolves:** F-t3-014 | **Owner:** Model Plane (execution-core verifier + sandbox mount; model-gateway report relay + `skill_file`; capability-core bundle registry); Frontend Plane supporting (artifact panel registry + previewer) | **Wave:** W2 | **Type:** S1 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S0, S2-S9 BUILD-NEW; S10 deferred BUILD | **Effort:** XL, ~58-70 eng-days serial (S0 3, S1 2.5, S2 7, S3 5.5, S4 10, S5 5.5, S6 9, S7 5.5, S8 9, S9 5.5; S10 unsized); critical path S0 -> S2 -> S4 -> S5 -> S9 ~31 d; two engineers ~7-8 weeks | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline: `tool_loop.rs`, `sse_events.rs`, `sse.rs`, `grpc.rs`, ZDR tests); WS08 S5 (`ro_binds` seam, S8); WS05 (class vocabulary for `skill_file`); WS06 (audit emitter); WS07 (skills cache, bundle revisions); WS12 (i18n/SSE golden gates) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-37 (OD18 fonts/caps: gates S4 font list only; ship on recommended default, caps are WS08 S7); DEC-22 context: WS19 D5 respects the no-trait SandboxBackend ledger deferral; local D2-D9 recommendations stand (D2 gates S3 enforce half; D3 S6/S8; D6 S6); DEC-39 (proto number `ExecuteStepRequest.skill_mounts=13`, migration names). Free now: S0-S3, S7, S9 text.

**Verified starting point:**
- `code_interpreter` runs in bwrap: `WorkspaceWrite` over one per-call root, network disabled, allowlisted env (`PATH, HOME, TMPDIR, MPLCONFIGDIR, MPLBACKEND, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE, LANG`), Drop-guarded workspace (`EC/src/code_interpreter.rs:1-34,305-348,430-452,579-582`); output = top-level regular files only, 8 MiB/file, 16 MiB total (`:79-82,642-715`). bwrap argv `--ro-bind / /`, no `/proc` (`EC/src/sandbox.rs:91-113,156-224`); `LaunchOptions.extra_ro_binds` exists with **no production caller** (`executor.rs:250-263`). Default timeout 120 s (`executor.rs:53`) while the tool text says "~30s" (`MG/src/tool_loop.rs:4307`): fix in S2.
- Image `debian:bookworm-slim`, uid 10001, apt python libs + `python-pptx==1.0.2`; no LibreOffice, poppler or extra fonts (`EC/Dockerfile:41-97`). **ZDR clause and regression guards:**
- ZDR: `ExecuteStep` refuses ZDR callers/requests (`EC/src/grpc.rs:650-661,881`), so `code_interpreter`, verification (S2-S4), previews (S5) and mounts (S8) are unreachable under ZDR; nothing persisted outside the Drop-guarded workspace; reports logged as counts only; `check` is a short text list stored with the turn like the file card (only when `code_interpreter` ran); previews are transient and never persisted; bundles are org config, not conversation content (no ZDR refusal at rest); `skill_file` read-only, follows `fetch_skill_context` ZDR gate (verify in S7). If persistence-free ZDR execution later enables `code_interpreter`, verification inherits it. Golden rows in `zdr_feature_table.rs` [live-only].
- Audit section 8: signed fail-closed capability gate (no new capability; `permission/mod.rs:415` test `code_interpreter_runs_ungated_while_shell_stays_gated` unchanged); two loops separate (`cross_service_loop_contract.rs` extended, shared contracts only); honest outcomes (`render_unavailable_is_warning_not_pass`, FE chip wording tests, never "verified in Office"); typed event taxonomy + byte-verbatim relay (additive optional keys, WS12 taxonomy gate + Rust SSE golden); credential isolation (sandbox env allowlist untouched, `child_env_for`, `verify-sandbox-isolation.sh`); kernel-enforced sandbox (LibreOffice inside same argv, no `/proc`, no widened binds; S0 script, `render_argv_has_unshare_net_and_no_extra_binds`); Office exports in browser untouched (`chat-export/index.test.ts`).

**Top risks:**
- LibreOffice does not run under this bwrap profile, or costs too much (image size, cold start, OOM under concurrency): S0 spike first, fail closed to structural-only, never widen binds, sidecar (D5 b) fallback, separate RUN layer, pre-warmed profile, semaphore 2, 60 s timeout, `structural` rollback.
- Parser CVEs on model-authored files and false `failed` read as an Office-fidelity guarantee: separate verify workspace, no network, timeout, macros/link updates off, scrubbed untrusted report, rebuild cadence owned by WS01; advisory default (D2), corpus-measured rate before enforce, explicit wording tests.
- Tenant bundle scripts as supply-chain/injection vector, and merge pressure on `tool_loop.rs`, `sse*.rs`, `executor.rs`, `ChatArtifactPanel.tsx`, `execution.proto`: admin-only zip upload, audit, caps, no import (D6), sandbox-only execution, read-only mount, digest-verified blobs; new modules, thin diffs, S1 first, edit-order rules above.

### WS20 - Scheduled runs as governed, tool-using Do runs
**Resolves:** F-t3-018 | **Owner:** Model Plane (orchestrator-core supervision, execution-core run, session-core run record + receipts, capability-core schedules/fires/notify producer); Control Plane user-core signs the consent contract binding; Application Plane notification-core delivers off-surface; Frontend Plane consent card, "See results", origin chip | **Wave:** W2 | **Type:** S0, S1, S3 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S2, S4-S8 BUILD-NEW; S9 BUILD gated (design only, not scheduled) | **Effort:** L-XL, ~40-58 eng-days (S0 1-2, S1 5-7, S2 2-3, S3 4-6, S4 8-10, S5 5-7, S6 8-12, S7 4-6, S8 3-5; audit "L" understates) | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline: `zdr_refusal.rs`, exec-core/session-core wave) and S2 (outbox publisher); WS02 S4/S7; WS03 S6; WS05 S1/S3/S4; WS16 S3/S4 (soft); WS13 cause vocabulary (soft) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-16 (stored-consent contract, ceiling `read`, expiry, draft meaning, loop branch, D8 instruction snapshot: gates S4-S8; S0-S3 free; S0 writes the ADR); DEC-02 (OD8 decoupled: gates S9 only); DEC-38 (notification type `modelplane.scheduled_run_outcome`: gates S7); DEC-17 (run origin enum: S2, no owner needed beyond MPO + SPACE/QRY ack); DEC-13 (D11 service exemption, no gate); DEC-28 (composer `compose_authored_instructions` for D8). Free now: S0, S1, S2, S3.

**Verified starting point:**
- Pipeline today: capability-core sweeper inserts task + `cron_fires` row status `'pending'` never updated (`GO/capability-core/internal/cron/sweeper.go:226-262`); dispatcher re-authorizes with Control (`internal/taskexec/workflow_dispatcher.go:215-236`); orchestrator `buildScheduledRunInput` re-hashes template, derives only `{goal, policy}` (`GO/orchestrator-core/internal/orchestration/workflowreg.go:249-330`); `ScheduledRunSupervision` (`cmd/workflows/interactive_run.go:188-259`) loops `executeScheduledStepLoop` (`step_loop.go:97-136`, up to 10 single-inference steps), fails the run on `NeedsApproval` (`:242-243`), completes with a count-only summary (`:134`).
- **Audit missed: the step output is never persisted.** `EC/src/grpc.rs:238-451`: claim receipt, read goal, one `Infer` (`scheduled_inference_request 487-520`: `verevon-balance`, `max_tokens 1024`, `tool_choice none`, 60 s), receipt with **output digest only** (`:407-418`); no `AppendMessage` in the scheduled path. "See results" has nothing to show even if enabled; plan rows D5/D6 are real prerequisites.
- Control: create/fire/run/step decisions are Ed25519 tokens, all refuse ZDR (`CP/user-core/internal/spaces/schedule_create_decision.go:76-97`, step `151-174`); create payload binds only `template_digest` (`:45-71`); no tool/approval field in `controlDecision` (`control_authorizer.go:147-170`). Verifier `EC/src/scheduled_step_decision.rs:36-60`: `Claims` is `deny_unknown_fields`, `DECISION_VERSION "v2"`: **new claims break an old exec-core; deploy order matters** (S5).
- Reusable seams: `run_agent_with_tools` (`EC/src/runtime_loop/agent.rs:743-800`, private today); purpose-lock allowlist from the tool Vec (`:850`); per-call rung refusal fed back to the model (`:1664-1700`); `pause_for_approval` (`:2086-2100`) must never fire for a system-owned run; `finalize` + `ManagedRunTokenProvider` (`session_terminal_auth.rs:84-100`); `RunDeadline::wall_clock_after` (`run_budget.rs:124`); `ScheduledInferenceTokenProvider` scope `inference:invoke` (`scheduled_inference_auth.rs:19-21`). The real `RunAgent` RPC is impossible unattended (four user-delegated bearers, `EC/src/grpc.rs:1207-1232`).
- Run record: `persist_scheduled_run_bindings` writes immutable `runs.metadata{source:"scheduled_run", schedule_id, fire_key, space_id, subject_id, template_digest, policy_digest, idempotency_key}` (`SC/src/grpc.rs:1378-1446`); run owner `service:orchestrator-core`; thread origin resolved server-side (`:2314-2342`). FE: "See results" unconditionally `disabled` (`FE/features/chat/components/ScheduledRunsPanel.tsx:135-142`); no fires listing endpoint exists.
- Audit/sweep stale: card ":610 D3-D11 unbuilt" (D9, D12 built); plan D4 "bounded read-only tool loop in the scheduled step" conflicts with audit "same RunAgent path" (resolved by D3); `docs/system-run-context.md` task-runner principal is the shortcut the audit rejects (implementation status not determined); "ADR unattended authority" does not exist (draft-only ruling only at plan `:68`, OD9 `:457`); `MP/go/go.work` is tracked (memory note "no go.work" stale). Live-only to land first: `mp-contracts/src/zdr_refusal.rs` (live `EC/src/grpc.rs` ZDR refusal calls it), `EC/src/grpc.rs`, `agent.rs`, `auth.rs`, `SC/src/grpc.rs`, `terminalization.rs`, `sessions.proto` (+42), migrations 0044/0045, `ScheduleDialog.tsx` (+24), `ScheduleDialog.test.tsx`, `zdr-posture-matrix.json`.

- Consumes: WS01 S0 and S2; WS02 S4/S7; WS03 S6; WS05 S1/S3/S4; WS16 S3/S4; WS13 cause vocabulary (soft); Space session's shared-Space decision family (overlap-space D8) for shared-room schedules (out of scope).

- Cross-session: Space session owns shared-room schedules, `turn_origin` mapping and the Work tab (S3 only links to the transcript; scheduled results appended with non-`conversation` scope; Space audience predicate must treat `origin=schedule` threads as non-extractable); Quarry/browser: browser tools never delegable in v1.
- [RECONCILE] unattended authority vs "draft-only": DEC-16 (amended not reversed). [RECONCILE] notification vocabulary vs WS16 `modelplane.needs_approval`: DEC-38 / section 2.6 row 21. [RECONCILE] origin vocabulary: DEC-17 / section 2.6 row 10. **ZDR clause and regression guards:**
- ZDR: all four Control decisions refuse ZDR (`schedule_create_decision.go:86`), orchestrator `DeniesZDR` (`workflowreg.go:117`), exec-core live refusal; `RecordScheduledRunResult` returns `failed_precondition` for ZDR/waived runs; `run_scheduled_agent` refuses `zdr || waived` before claim (R17: waiver clause "never scheduled or unattended" restated, DEC-32). Persisted (non-ZDR only): the assistant message with withheld-effect args in the scheduled thread; receipts numeric/digest; `cron_fires` numeric columns; contract = tool names + dates, no content; notification payloads content-free (no goal, answer, tool or argument text); no content in Temporal history or logs. Golden: `zdr-posture-matrix.json` rows `scheduled_run` (S1) and `scheduled_do_run` (S6) via `contract-goldens.yml` and `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`; `scheduled-step-config-contract-test.sh`.
- Audit section 8: signed fail-closed capability gate (unattended run still calls `EvaluatePolicy`, unknown tool fails closed, `autonomy_ladder_contract.rs`); Ask/Do switch + autonomy ladder (posture `READ_ONLY` + `plan_mode`, test that no path raises the rung for `origin=schedule`); two loops separate (no change under `model-gateway/src/tool_loop.rs`; `cross_service_loop_contract.rs`); honest outcomes (`TestScheduledRun_UnknownOutcomeStopsWithoutAnotherStep` `interactive_run_test.go:179`, new `..._AgentLane_UnknownOutcome...`, withheld effects shown); credential isolation (no user bearer in the lane; `TestScheduledRunInputRejectsDetachedTemplateAndForgedExecutionFields` `startworkflow_test.go:452`; Temporal history has no tokens/output); multi-tenant/GDPR (new `cron_fires` columns and message in session-core erasure scope; extend `gdpr.rs` order test `:724-729`); typed event taxonomy (no ChatEvent added).

**Top risks:**
- Hot-file conflicts in execution-core `agent.rs`/`grpc.rs` and session-core `grpc.rs` (live M, five other workstreams) and decision skew (`deny_unknown_fields`, Temporal replay): land WS01-S0 first, S6 limited to three hunks, new logic in `scheduled_agent.rs`/`scheduled_contract.rs`; fixed rollout order in S5, `#[serde(default)]`, `GetVersion` branch, additive proto fields, replay test.
- Consent goes stale or is never understood, and prompt injection through web reads in an unattended run: mandatory expiry (D5), re-verification at fire and step, auto-pause (S8), plain-language card, ceiling `read` so zero effects possible in v1, withheld-effect block visible, `ExternalWeb` provenance; adversarial security review before enabling the flag in production.
- Credential window / long runs and ownership ambiguity for off-surface delivery: D2 decouple (service credentials only, 240 s wall clock, heartbeat + 6 min activity timeout, anything longer is S9); matrix amendment recording capability-core as notification-core caller (S7), content-free payloads.
