### WS21 - Agent browser on real broker grants
**Resolves:** F-t3-008 (XL; audit split: grant + Do tool L, site governance L) | **Owner:** Model Plane (execution-core + model-gateway + browser-broker) for grant derivation, Do tools, consent gate; Control Plane org-core (later user-core) for site policy of record; Frontend Plane for BFF routes, consent cards, settings; Ingestion Quarry v2 stays executor and final enforcer (QRY session) | **Wave:** 2 | **Type:** S0/S1/S7/S10 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S2-S6, S8, S9 BUILD-NEW | **Effort:** XL, 50-70 engineer-days (S0 1-2, S1 1-2, S2 3-4, S3 6-8, S4 5-7, S5a 7-10, S5b 6-9, S6 7-10, S7 2-3, S8 3-5, S9 6-9, S10 3-4) | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline); QRY session (Grant v2 proto note, Quarry-to-broker identity, issuance endpoint, takeover/redaction); WS03 (continue/bearer mint, park), WS05 S1/S3, WS10 (org-core wiring first), WS13 S1 (cause carrier), WS12 (UI gates), WS14 (login hand-off); Space session (a) for S8 | **Gating decisions:** DEC-19 (layered policy, gates S3, S4, S5b, S8), DEC-20 (server-bound tools, consent cause, grant store; gates S4, S5a, S5b, S6), DEC-02 (credential window, gates S5a), DEC-06 (Space floor S8), DEC-21 (interim login S9). No decision needed: S0, S1, S2; S3 needs DEC-19 D6/D7 (low risk, recommended); S4/S5 need DEC-19/20 and QRY's identity+issuance.

**Verified starting point**
- The Do loop offers `browser.observe`/`browser.act` unconditionally (`EC/runtime_loop/agent.rs:4196-4205`, fn `offered_tool_defs` 4109); `execute_step_with_subagent` passes `browser_grant=None` (`runtime_loop/mod.rs:447`), so the arm at `mod.rs:589-594` -> `tool_bridge::execute_quarry_browser_mcp` (`tool_bridge/mod.rs:237-249`) always returns "requires a broker-validated grant". Only unary `ExecuteStep` builds a grant (`EC/grpc.rs:184-215, 886-948`). Both tools fail on every Do run today.
- Beyond the audit text: nothing creates a Quarry run for Do (`tool_bridge/mod.rs:229-232`); model supplies `grant_id/quarry_run_id/lease_id` (`agent.rs:4199`); `RunAgent` accepts no browser bearer (`grpc.rs:1203-1271`) though model-gateway already verifies `VerifiedBrowserBearer` (`MG/auth.rs:1078,1212,1271-1273`) and never forwards it (`MG/sse.rs:8296-8346`); no production `AcquireGrant` caller under `apps/`; broker store is in-memory, 15 min TTL (`BB/internal/grant/grant.go:62-70`, `server/server.go:22`); Control has no site table; four unpinned domain canonicalizers (Go, Quarry, exec-core, gateway).
- Capability gate: all browser tools map to `cap.browser.open` (`capability_policy.rs:793`), seeded `high` (`capability-core/migrations/0008...:12`), never attested (`UNATTESTED_BASELINE`, `agent.rs:3954`); `browser.act` is in the risky list (`permission/mod.rs:266-271`, no retry `retry.rs:149-151`).
- Browser workspace "AI run" is dead in prod: `GW/domains/browser.rs:1235` sends `grant_id: "session:{id}"` and no browser bearer while `MG/browser_run.rs:304` requires one.
- Audit wording "the gateway mints" is wrong: authority is minted in Model Plane under the user's bearer; the BFF only forwards. Plan drift: closure plan says delegated `aud=quarry` bearer; code uses exec-core's own service token (`quarry_agent.rs:591`); WS21 keeps the service token and binds the user through the grant owner.
- [live-only] files that S2+ edit (land first): `MG/{browser_run,sse,tool_loop,http_routes,auth,state}.rs`, `EC/runtime_loop/{agent,mod}.rs`, `EC/{grpc,auth}.rs`, `MP/proto/model_plane/v1/sessions.proto`, IQ `quarry-edge/src/{state,config,main}.rs`, FP `chat-client.ts`, `ChatMessages.tsx`, `use-chat-controller.ts`; untracked `mp-contracts/src/zdr_refusal.rs`, `api/contracts/zdr-refusals.json`, `MG/zdr_posture.rs`. Browser-broker, `tool_bridge/mod.rs`, `browser_agent*`, `capability_policy.rs`, gateway `browser.rs`, `audience_tokens.rs`, Quarry `agent_routes.rs` are identical in both trees.

**Provides / consumes**
- Provides: WS05 tool names `browser.open|observe|act|close` and ids `cap.browser.observe` (read) / `cap.browser.act` (external_write) (resolves WS05 D9 row split); WS13 causes `browser_site_consent{host,max_level,options}` and pass-through `browser_action_risk`; WS03 serializable `BrowserSession` (ids only; opaque `tool_state` key `browser` in `LoopState`; on resume revalidate grant with fresh bearer, else re-mint and reopen); WS15 events `browser_session_opened`, `browser_site_decision{host,effective,source}` (content-free); WS20 rule: scheduled runs never get a browser bearer (test belongs to WS20); WS25 org-core site `revision`; WS26 untrusted-observation hook on `execute_browser_do_tool`; WS27 step count/`max_cost_usd` from grant constraints; WS06 audit events (host + level only); WS22 browser grant + consent vocabulary before F-t3-026; Space session `browser_entitled` + `space_id` through broker.
- Consumes: WS01 ZDR wave; WS02 `RunAgentRequest` numbering (no new tag here); WS03 pause/continue/credential-margin park; WS10 org-core wiring and principal pattern; WS12 UI gates; WS13 cause carrier + `approval-copy.ts`; QRY Grant v2 note, issuance endpoint, Quarry-to-broker identity, durable store, takeover/redaction, budgets (overlap-quarry C1-C5, DEC-19/20, section 2.6 row 15). [RECONCILE] store location (Redis per matrix L73 vs Postgres per T2) and policy delivery (signed claims vs service endpoint) are QRY's call (DEC-20; 2.6 rows 7, 15); WS21 works on in-memory and org-core pull.

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR (DEC-32 rule): browser runs are refused today (`MG/browser_run.rs:307`); Do browser tools gain a typed refusal (`browser_tool`, `browser_login`); `x-browser-authorization` is never minted or forwarded under ZDR; a ZDR run cannot pause for approval (`agent.rs:4551-4560`). Persisted (non-ZDR): grant (broker), Quarry run (QRY matrix), run events; site policy tables hold hostnames, levels, actor ids only, no URLs/paths/page content, erased with org; persistent profiles refused under ZDR.
- Audit s8 strengths: "model proposes, Quarry executes" (tool grammar has no URL/CSS/JS/coords/paths except `browser.open{url}`, `deny_unknown_fields`; `agent_routes.rs:326-336` untouched); two loops separate (`inline_tool_allowed` still refuses `browser_agent`/`browser.*`, asserts near `MG/tool_loop.rs` 9107-9110); credential isolation (S2 tests, grep that no JWT/grant id appears in model-visible text, `scrub_checkpoint_test.rs:68` extended); ZDR propagation (matrix rows + `browser-workspace-zdr.spec.ts`); browser risk classes (S5b parity test); fail-closed (S4 org-core-down = refuse); cross-service constants (S0 golden + `browser_do_contract.rs`).

**Top risks**
- Bearer lifetime (DEC-02): ~300 s bearer vs long runs and 15 min grants. Mitigation: lazy mint, typed `browser_credential_expired`, park via WS03, grant TTL up to 30 min, OD8 tracked.
- Conflict storm in `agent.rs`/`mod.rs`/`grpc.rs` (six streams, large live diffs) and QRY dependency (without Quarry identity S4/S5 stall; broker restart loses grants). Mitigation: new modules + one arm + one field, strict edit order, S0-S3 and S1/S2 independent, S5 behind flag, in-memory limitation documented.
- Prompt injection via page text reaching a model that can click, and policy drift (four canonicalizers, Control dependency). Mitigation: act-level per-site consent, risk classifier, Quarry fence, untrusted wrapper + WS26 screening before default-on, fail closed, shadow mode, golden fixtures.

---

### WS22 - Per-thread sandbox computer
**Resolves:** F-t3-009 (XL) | **Owner:** Model Plane: sandbox-manager (Go: lease, thread-workspace rows, lifecycle), execution-core (Rust: bwrap spawn, per-step workspace sync, future trait); model-gateway and BFF only carry a bearer, an id and a typed event; Control owns entitlements/signed decision later; cost-core metering later | **Wave:** 2 | **Type:** S0/S4 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S1-S3, S5-S8 BUILD-NEW (S7 gated DEC-22, S8 gated DEC-22/23) | **Effort:** XL, about 43-60 engineer-days without the microVM epic (S0 2-3, S1 7-10, S2 8-10, S3 4-6, S4 3-4, S5 8-12, S6 5-7, S7 6-8) plus S8 spike 5-8 d | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline); WS15 S1 (S6), WS17 thread-delete hook (S5), WS08 (reserved `attachments/`, `extra_ro_binds`), WS21 browser grant + consent vocabulary before computer track (S8) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-22 (SandboxBackend reversal, microVM; gates S7, S8), DEC-23 (authority departs from the card: delegated user bearer for S1-S5 flags default off; Control decision `model.thread.sandbox_workspace` required before default-on for an org and before billed/persistent compute; thread-only scope; retention; exclusions; ZDR refuse; gates S3 default flip and S8). S0-S6 need no decision beyond recording; S3 default flip also blocked until S5 ships.

**Verified starting point**
- sandbox-manager already accepts `scope_type "thread"` (`SM/internal/server/server.go:96`; insert `lease.go:163-166`) but every `AcquireLease` mints a new lease (`lease.go:142-171`); a non-Space lease has `backend_id=""` and no workspace (`GetWorkspaceManifest` empty when `space_id==""` `server.go:295-297`; `PromoteWorkspace` refuses `:329-331`); workspace rows are keyed `(org_id, space_id, run_id, path)` (`SM/migrations/0001_workspace_manifest.up.sql`), no thread key anywhere.
- Only `AcquireLease` caller passes `scope_id: run_id, scope_type: "agent"` and only for Space (`EC/sandbox_lease.rs:143-144`); `execute_step` demands the sandbox bearer only for Space-scoped `code_interpreter` (`EC/grpc.rs:864-874`), BFF mints it only for Space turns (`GW/domains/chat/shared.rs:211-238`). Inline plain-chat `code_interpreter` goes `MG/tool_loop.rs:2555` -> `MG/tools.rs:163-267` -> direct `ExecuteStep` with `thread_id=""` into `execute_step_inner` (`grpc.rs:936-938`): memory tools fail closed there and this must not change; `ExecuteStepRequest` fields 1-11 (`space_id=11`, `MP/proto/model_plane/v1/execution.proto:268-318`).
- Edit loss is narrower than the card: a non-Space call runs in a per-call temp dir removed by `Drop` (`EC/code_interpreter.rs:305-348, 514-517`); a Space inline call hydrates and caches by `run_id` with no run-end signal so the upload at `sandbox_lease.rs:436-488` never fires (module doc `17-29`). New finding: nothing calls `PromoteWorkspace`, so Space "durable" edits do not persist across runs even for governed `RunAgent`.
- `diff_and_upload` uploads every regular file incl. staged program file, `.tmp`, `.mplconfig` and cannot see deletions (`EC/workspace_hydrate.rs:176-198`); header 1-14 "not wired" is stale. CAS keys are global `cas/sha256:<hex>` (`workspace_cas.rs:39,28`), no delete/GC and no Go-side S3 client. ZDR is stronger than the card: `execute_step`/`run_agent` refuse all ZDR up front (`grpc.rs:881, 1237`).
- No trait exists (`MpSandboxPolicy::External` only forwards a network policy `EC/policy.rs:55-69`); no lease sweeper (lazy expiry `lease.go:194-196`); no failure-cause/heartbeat, no sandbox SSE event, no FE action contract; no CI workflow runs sandbox-manager Go tests.
- [live-only] (WS01-S0 lands): `mp-contracts/src/zdr_refusal.rs` (`Refusal::feature_not_supported`, `FEATURES` live 215-340), `execution-core/tests/zdr_refusal_inventory.rs` (`NAMED_SITES` live 15-25), `EC/{grpc,auth}.rs`, `EC/runtime_loop/{mod,agent}.rs` (live +12 to +14 in `mod.rs`), `MG/{auth,sse,tool_loop,sse_events}.rs`, `FP chat-client.ts`, `MP/docs/sse-event-taxonomy.md`. Everything in `SM/`, `sandboxes.proto`, `execution.proto`, `sandbox_lease.rs`, `code_interpreter.rs`, `workspace_*.rs`, `shared.rs`, `streams.rs` is clean in live.

**Slices**
Common flags (vocabulary `enabled`, as `EXECUTION_CORE_PROCESS_HOST` `EC/sandbox.rs:327-338`; no KEY/TOKEN/SECRET so `release-artifact.sh:732-753` needs no audit): `SANDBOX_MANAGER_THREAD_WORKSPACE`, `EXECUTION_CORE_THREAD_WORKSPACE`, BFF `SANDBOX_THREAD_WORKSPACE_ENABLED` (strict true/false, default false). Compose: `MP/deploy/docker-compose.yml` execution-core block 1005-1280 (near 1184), sandbox-manager 1575-1611. Rollback = set off (rows stay, unreachable). Rebuild recipe: `bash "apps/Model Plane/scripts/compose.sh" up -d --no-deps --build sandbox-manager execution-core model-gateway`, drive the flow through the BFF.

**Provides / consumes**
- Provides: WS24 file tools (thread workspace RPCs `GetThreadWorkspace`/`ApplyThreadWorkspaceChanges`, `WorkspaceScope::Thread`, `workspace_sync::{open,commit}`; file tools must go through them, never a raw directory) [RECONCILE] WS24 designs file tools only for Space-scoped hydrated workspaces (`space_scoped`, own lock `HydratedWorkspace.lock`, `content_version`); thread-scope reuse is a later hunk, settled by DEC-24 (no promote caller) and 2.6 row 23 (naming); WS03 (per-step write-through: restart or pause loses no committed thread edit; `WorkspaceStateLost` applies only to Space overlay); WS15 closed tokens + `sandbox_state`; WS27 counts only (`file_count`, `total_bytes`, later VM-seconds); WS19 files listable; WS05 `code_interpreter` stays `sandbox_compute` (WS22-D7); WS17/WS07 `DELETE /v1/threads/{id}/workspace` erasure hook; F-t3-024/035 later: trait + `SandboxFailureCause`.
- Consumes: WS01-S0; WS08 `attachments/` + `extra_ro_binds`; WS15 S1 `lifecycle_notice.rs` + fenced block; WS17 thread-delete; WS10/WS25 org entitlement/`org_agent_policy` key for DEC-23 option b (env flags until then); WS04 (thread path stateless per step, replica-safe); WS20 (scheduled runs mint no sandbox bearer = ephemeral path). Space session (a): shared-Space decision family (today `KindPersonal` only, `CP/user-core/internal/spaces/space_capability_decision.go:138` (`validatePersonalAuthority`; `KindPersonal` is defined at `CP/user-core/internal/spaces/authority.go:17`)), promote caller + ACL ruling, caps/egress as signed-decision claims; no per-agent home here (a new `scope_type` on the same lease model if F-t1-028 needs one; thread and Space rows never share a table or RPC).

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR (DEC-23 D8, DEC-32): refuse. `execute_step`/`run_agent` already refuse ZDR; new guard `sandbox_workspace` survives a later un-refuse (then ephemeral per-call path, no lease/CAS/manifest); `AcquireLease` thread scope with `zdr=true` refused; nothing hydrated, uploaded or written on refusal. Persisted (non-ZDR): thread path, hash, size, owner; blobs under `cas/thread/<org>/<thread>/`; erased by S5 (rows and blobs, tested).
- Audit s8 strengths: kernel-enforced sandbox and Space CAS workspaces never bypassed (S1 alias tests, bwrap argv tests, `verify-sandbox-isolation.sh`, `space_path_flag_off_is_byte_identical`); ZDR by construction (`thread_workspace_refused_under_zdr`, `zdr_refusal_inventory.rs`, `zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`); two loops separate (`direct_execute_step_still_passes_empty_thread_id_to_memory_tools`); signed capability gate (`code_interpreter` still passes `evaluate_with_evidence` `mod.rs:488-539`; no tool added S1-S6); credential isolation (child env cleared, scrub on upload `workspace_hydrate.rs:146-151`); honest outcomes (`ephemeral_result_declares_no_persistence`); typed ChatEvent taxonomy (`--check`); multi-tenant GDPR erasure (S5 delete tests).

**Top risks**
- Persistent user data without an erasure path and authority departing from the card (DEC-23). Mitigation: default-on blocked on S5; blobs under per-thread prefix; thread-delete hook with WS17; owner-bound bearer, structural no-alias, flags off; owner ruling before default-on; Control decision before any billed compute.
- Per-step hydrate cost, per-turn token mint, hot-file conflicts (`grpc.rs`, `mod.rs`, `agent.rs`, `sse_events.rs`). Mitigation: 256 files / 64 MiB cap, optional blob cache, lazy bearer; WS01-S0 first, hunks are call-ins to `workspace_sync.rs`, append-only proto (integrator assigns `AcquireLeaseRequest`=8, `ExecuteStepRequest`=12).
- One-implementation trait, unfunded microVM, silent assumption of persistence in Space. Mitigation: S7 gated with a real executor stub, S8 ends in an ADR, S4 states the limit in ledger and `workspace.scope`.

### WS23 - Connected apps that work in chat
**Resolves:** F-t3-010 (Priority, L); plan rows F2-F6, F8, F9, F10, F12, G7-G10 (writes), OD10/OD12 | **Owner:** Model Plane (execution-core Do loop, model-gateway Ask loop, mp-contracts shared tables) with Ingestion Plane (integration-corev2: catalogue truth, provider reads, token custody) and Frontend Plane (composer, BFF, action contracts); Control Plane two small edits (service-principal scope; connection ownership stays in integration-corev2) | **Wave:** 2 | **Type:** S0/S1/S2/S7/S8 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S3-S6 BUILD-NEW; S9 BUILD gated, not scheduled | **Effort:** L by audit; design total 52-66 engineer-days (S0 2, S1 5, S2 5, S3 9, S4 6, S5 9, S6 7, S7 5 plus owner wait, S8 9; S9 excluded) | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline, rebuilt stack, token-exchange registry row verified); WS05 S1 (class row; interim name mapping works); WS03 (`waived` signature lands first); WS02 (tool-list ordering, field number); WS13 (typed cause); WS26 (screening policy); WS12; WS08 (binary extraction) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-26 (OD10: Do reads default-on, Ask reads narrow reversal, `@` trigger; gates S3 default-on, S5, S6 mention, S7), DEC-27 (OD12 dedicated accounts and OAuth consent, `integration:write` scope; gates S7, S8), DEC-06 (Space refusal). No decision needed: S0, S1, S2 (tightening, no owner needed), S3 build, S6 chips. Local D1 (tool shape), D9, D10, D11 local.

**Verified starting point**
- integration-corev2 implements reads: Microsoft `profile`, `calendar.events`, `mail.messages`, `mail.messages.get`, `drive.files`, `drive.file.content`, `sharepoint.sites`, `sharepoint.drives`, `mail.send`; Google `profile`, `gmail.messages`, `gmail.messages.get`, `gmail.send`, `calendar.events`, `drive.files`, `drive.file.content` (`IC/internal/actions/service.go:203-234, 276-307`). execution-core CATALOG lacks `.get`, content, sharepoint, profile and its summaries still say "No server-side query/search filter is implemented yet" (`EC/integration_tools.rs:98-115`, stale text 102, 111) although `IC/internal/actions/mail_read.go:18-50` has query/from/since/until/folder/unread/pageToken/headers.
- Gating is partial: catalogued reads (`mail.messages`, `gmail.messages`, `drive.files`, `calendar.events`) are `Some(false)` and NOT gated under ask; only uncatalogued ops return `None` and are gated (`integration_tools.rs:747-761`, `permission/mod.rs:195-218`, tests `456-486`). `read_provider_action` and `PROVIDER_READ_TOOL_ENABLED` exist nowhere in code. `read_action_json` already mints an `integration:read`-only token (`integration_tools.rs:494-540`).
- Connector content is classified `OrgInternal` (`EC/provenance.rs:35-67, 80-96`): no UNTRUSTED framing, no injection screen (same hole the ledger fixed for `web.read`, `MP/docs/decisions/ledger.md:2311`; contract test `execution-core/tests/compaction_parity_contract.rs:542`). `data_category()` has no provider arm so connector results are audited `public_non_personal` (`agent.rs:314-327`, `MG/audit.rs:8-23`); non-ZDR `record_tool_step` persists `truncate(output, 2000)` of every tool result (`agent.rs:309, 4361-4431`).
- Approved writes cannot mint a token: `execute_action` mints `integration:write` with the plain org credential; `model-execution` principal has only `integration:read` and `shipping:read` on the ingestion audience (`integration_tools.rs:421-435`, `CP/config/plane-service-principals.json:263-290`, `IC/internal/api/server.go:3357-3363`; live registry not inspected). Approval reason is generic "tool 'execute_provider_action' requires approval" (`agent.rs:4459-4467`).
- Owner ruling 5 enforced server-side (`IC/internal/api/connection_access.go:73-114`, `server.go:3064-3079`); model-gateway already holds a verified `aud=ingestion` USER bearer (`MG/auth.rs:109-124`, `tool_loop.rs:2086-2128, 2406-2457`). Do-mode runs refused under ZDR (`EC/grpc.rs:650-661, 1237`); Ask-mode ZDR chat allowed so Ask reads need their own refusal.
- Audit text stale: "calls are approval-gated" true only for uncatalogued ops; `ORG_CLASS_TOOLS` lives in `MG/tool_loop.rs:4228-4237` not `source_validation.rs:11-23` and its doc says connector tools are in neither bucket; citation origin is `web`/`knowledge` only and the frontend DROPS other origins (`MG/sse_events.rs:182-190`, `FP/src/features/chat/components/chat-normalizers.ts:1326`, `chat-types.ts:137`). integration-corev2 has no CI workflow.
- [live-only] to land first: `MG/zdr_posture.rs`, `mp-contracts/src/{zdr_refusal.rs,retention.rs}`, `zdr-refusals.json(+.sha256)`, `MG/tests/zdr_posture_matrix.rs`, `FP/src/shared/api/zdr-refusals.ts`, session-core 0044/0045; live-modified files this stream edits: `agent.rs` (+207), `mod.rs` (+18, `waived` param), `grpc.rs` (+98), `tool_loop.rs` (+425), `sse_events.rs` (+203), `sse.rs` (+883), `DashboardComposer.tsx` (+7), `ChatMessages.tsx` (+229), `chat-normalizers.ts` (+39), `chat-types.ts` (+7), `chat-client.ts` (+53). No connector hunk found in live diffs (conflicts positional only).

**Provides / consumes**
- Provides: WS05/WS24 `read_provider_action` as `cap.tool.provider.read`, class `read`, floor `none` (`operation_is_write` is the `call_refinement=provider_operation` source for `execute_provider_action`; parity golden proves the catalogue); WS02 `ConnectorScope` as a `RunAgentRequest` field (or `RunContext` member if WS02 prefers) with the tool appended last for cache stability; WS03 scope ids in run metadata, read tool idempotent, writes keep the approval-worker path; WS13 `provider_effect_summary` + `integration:write` mint requirement; WS20 scope ids + flag for owner-bound scheduled reads (a scheduled run reads only connections named in its contract); WS26 `TrustClass::ConnectorContent` in both copies + parity test (WS26 owns scanner policy, may later screen `inbox_*`); WS12 UI copy through locale helpers, contracts before UI; WS15 typed `reconnect_required`/`capability_missing` (incident ring handed to WS15 S9, WS23-D11 local); WS06 `data_category=personal` rows (audit-core wire fixtures need a case); F-t3-056 write confirmation cards build on S8.
- Consumes: WS01 (live ZDR set, rebuilt stack, token-exchange row); WS05 S1; WS13; WS03; WS02; WS08 (binary text extraction, WS23 returns metadata + `unsupported_content`); WS12; WS26; WS27 (prompt-size cost); Control (DEC-27 scope). Cross-session: Space session (a): connector tools refused whenever `space_id` set in both loops and chip row omitted in Space rooms (DEC-06), no `@` in global composer until Space agrees (2.6 row 16), shared inbox `inbox_*` stays org-wide (OD11 owner-only fix belongs to Ingestion/Conversation Core), no new run origin; Quarry (b): connector content never goes to Quarry, no shared grant, redaction pattern set owned by `scrub.rs`.

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR (DEC-26, DEC-32): refuse in both loops via feature key `connector_read` before any model call and again at dispatch; nothing persisted; chips hidden and request field omitted. Non-ZDR persistence is counts-only (`[data_category=personal ... content_redacted=true]`), citation snippet is metadata only (never body text, as it is persisted in thread history); personal connector tools never offered when `space_id` set.
- Audit s8 strengths: signed fail-closed capability gate (binding test for every `offered_tool_defs` entry, unknown operation stays `None`); autonomy ladder / Ask-Do switch (`tests/autonomy_ladder_contract.rs` extended; read tool rung `read_only`); two loops separate (Ask gains a READ tool only: `inline_loop_denies_mcp_until_governed_agentic_dispatch_exists` ~9430, new `inline_loop_has_no_write_operation`, `provider_read_contract.rs`); ZDR by construction (S1 golden rows + `zdr-contract-sync.test.sh` + `record_tool_step_never_persists_connector_text`); honest outcomes (typed statuses, `omitted` counts, verified write read-back); typed ChatEvent taxonomy (no new variant, `gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check`, `chat-normalizers` test); credential isolation (tokens stay in integration-corev2, grep test that no tool result contains `access_token`, `browse.rs` allowlist unchanged); research honesty (`tool_allowed_for_retrieval_scope` tests for all three scopes).

**Top risks**
- Prompt injection through mail/file/event text steering a later write, and personal data persistence/leak. Mitigation: S1 before any widening (framing, screening, `personal` audit, counts-only persistence); writes always-ask and attested; schema has no `body`; scope enforced server-side; Space/ZDR refusals in both loops; plan F8b (approval for egress tools after a Flagged read) recorded as follow-up decision before any customer org enables the flag.
- Delegated-token path not live (org fallback gets `personal_connection_no_delegated_user`) or write scope missing. Mitigation: S0 verifies live registry; S7/S8 prove with captured evidence before any "done"; honest typed errors meanwhile.
- Catalogue drift (no Go CI, two catalogues) and provider behaviour differing from unverified helpers. Mitigation: vendored golden with sha pin, new Go CI workflow, Rust parity tests; S7 one-line scoped fixes each with a live-response fixture.

### WS24 - Do-mode action admission and workspace file tools
**Resolves:** F-t3-025 (Recommended, M, typed action registry for the agent) and F-t3-030 (Recommended, M, workspace file tools) | **Owner:** Model Plane (capability-core Go = admission table; execution-core Rust = Do-loop tools and edit engine; model-gateway = Ask-loop read arms, verify only) with Frontend Plane (classification map + CI ratchet) and Control Plane user-core (signed run decision/view generalisation); no Data, Ingestion, Application or Quarry code changes | **Wave:** 2 | **Type:** S0/S1/S5/S9 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S2-S4, S6-S8 BUILD-NEW | **Effort:** L by sum, about 38-53 engineer-days (audit says M+M; the audit excludes the unexecutable-allowlist cleanup, four-plane contract plumbing and honest durability): Track A (file tools) S1-S4 17-23 d, Track B (admission) S5-S8 18-25 d, S0+S9 3-5 d; two engineers in parallel about 4-5 weeks. Per slice: S0 1-2, S1 2-3, S2 5-7, S3 5-6, S4 5-7, S5 4-6, S6 6-8, S7 5-7, S8 3-4, S9 2-3 | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline: Z-18 own-connection ZDR waiver set across `agent.rs` +207, `mod.rs` +18 `waived`, `grpc.rs` +98, `auth.rs`, `agent_route.rs` +285, `knowledge_tools.rs`, `workspace_promote.rs`) before S3/S4; WS03 `waived` signature; WS05 S1 (class vocabulary/rows) before S3/S6; WS12 (CI); WS13 (approval floor); WS08 (shared `code_interpreter` arm, reserved `attachments`) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-24 (workspace edit visibility D1 and rung D2; gates S4 exposure `WORKSPACE_TOOLS=all` on the product route; blocking for product value, not for building), DEC-25 (typed side map, 7 unexecutable ids, hash basis, read authority; gates S5-S8), DEC-10 (OD2 Do on product route; flag flips only; WS24 D9). No decision needed: S0-S5, S8 option (a); local D3 (tool names) and D8 (glob engine: `globset` needs Cargo.lock review, fallback hand-rolled ~80 lines) proceed.

**Verified starting point**
- Registry has 168 ids (84 low / 61 medium / 23 high; 28 `requiresApproval:true`; 31 irreversible; owner planes 82 application / 37 model / 28 control / 17 ingestion / 4 data) and is almost all writes: only `brreg.lookup_organization` and `shipping.get_quotes` are read-shaped (`FP/src/shared/actions/action-registry.ts:1452, 1463`). `ActionDescriptor` has no model-access field (`FP/src/shared/actions/types.ts:15-25`). Eligibility is a hand-edited 8-id `Set` (`model-eligibility.ts:126-139`) feeding `catalog-manifest.ts:52-55` and `agent-tools.ts:112-137`; nothing outside the frontend consumes the manifest or its SHA-256, so the ratchet has no server teeth.
- Only 1 of the 8 eligible ids has a Model Plane executor (`tickets.create`); the 7 others (`inbox.follow_conversation`, `inbox.set_csat_preference`, `chat.save_thread_snapshot`, `inbox.review_ai_action`, `org.mark_exported`, `org.acknowledge_deletion`, `chat.submit_feedback`) fail closed (`EC/capability_policy.rs:838` `_ => return None`; gateway `unknown tool` `MG/tool_loop.rs:3785`). Four unconnected admission lists exist: frontend 8-id set (L1), Ask-loop 9 Verevon reads outside the registry (`MG/tool_loop.rs:3544-3660`, `MG/verevon_actions.rs:1-30,179-250,879`) (L2), exec-core `offered_tool_defs` + `trusted_capability_id` + capability-core seeds (L3), and the `tickets.create`-only owner-effect path hard-coded in `CP/user-core/internal/spaces/run_action_decision.go:12-17,63-72,340-347`, `model_action_view.go:16-31`, `capability-core/internal/api/model_action_view.go:14-24,107-175,180-232`, `EC/ticket_tools.rs:31-50`, `agent.rs:701-725` (L4).
- No file tool exists in either catalogue (Do catalogue = `offered_tool_defs` from `EC/runtime_loop/agent.rs:4109` + `process_tool_defs` 4079-4107 + MCP + client tools + one server-resolved owner action). Hydrated workspace is the whole Space file set under `temp_dir/verevon-space-workspaces/<space>/<lease>` (`EC/sandbox_lease.rs:183-250`, `EC/state.rs:94-98,240-250`, `EC/workspace_hydrate.rs:68-88`); `diff_and_upload` redacts text with `scrub_string` and hashes the redacted bytes (`workspace_hydrate.rs:102-132,146-155`); an upload or snapshot failure is only a `warn!` (`sandbox_lease.rs:526-546`).
- Overlay is per run; `PromoteWorkspace` has no production caller (ledger `MP/docs/decisions/ledger.md:3437-3442`; `SM/internal/workspace/store.go:67-100,158-217`); `EC/workspace_promote.rs` is the Data Plane v2 document client (name collision). `scrub_string` idempotence is by inspection only and not proven (`EC/scrub.rs:26,62-108,148-157`). Tool calls in a round run concurrently (`agent.rs:1870`).
- Permission: names containing `write`, `create`, `patch`, `update`, `remove`, `post` are risky by substring (`EC/permission/mod.rs:100-108,181-183,229-284`); precedent `process_stdin` (`process_tools.rs:482-505`, ledger.md:3609). Invariant tests to extend: `every_offered_tool_has_a_capability_binding`, `every_offered_tool_capability_has_an_attestor` (`agent.rs:3800-3880`). Onboarding precedent `cap.process.background` (`capability-core/migrations/0015_...up.sql`, `health_attest.rs:58,73,734-775`, `availability.go:55,97`).
- Audit text: "admits 8 of 168" understates (7 of 8 are unexecutable; the reads that work are not in the registry); "add a class and exempt reason to ActionDescriptor" replaced by a typed side map (DEC-25). `workspace_hydrate.rs:6-14` header is stale ("not wired"). Execution-core deps: `regex`, `sha2`, `dashmap`; no `globset`, `walkdir`, `similar` (`EC/../Cargo.toml:14-38`).
- [live-only]: Z-18 change set (above); `MP/api/contracts/zdr-refusals.json(+.sha256)`, `mp-contracts/src/{retention.rs,zdr_refusal.rs}`, `execution-core/tests/zdr_refusal_inventory.rs`, `FP/src/shared/api/contracts/zdr-refusals.json`, `FP/src/shared/actions/chat-work-period.ts`. **Provides / consumes**
- Provides: WS05 the "new tool needs row + class + attestor" contract exercised by `cap.workspace.read/.write` (classes `read`, `context_write`; transitional `is_workspace_write` removed when WS05 S4 lands); WS13 no approval for workspace tools (DEC-24 D2a) + `OWNER_ACTION_PINS.requires_durable_approval` for `write_approval` rows + the answer to the "auto posture self-approval" question for workspace tools only (provider writes under `auto` remain a WS13/DEC-10 product call); WS25 ordinary tool names so hooks (`PreToolUse`, `mod.rs:544`) and tool policy apply, `owner_actions` rows policy-addressable by `action_id`, `workspace_grep` output capped; WS20 unattended-allowlist classes (reads `read`, writes `context_write` excluded by default); WS22/F-t3-076 `HydratedWorkspace.lock`, `content_version`, `WorkspaceUploadOutcome`, overlay rows with `base_hash` == version token, no-promote-caller boundary, verify item that `HydratedWorkspace.baseline` for a run with an overlay row is the layered hash not the Space hash (`workspace_hydrate.rs:44-51`, `store.go:67-100`); WS19 no file delivery path added; WS21/WS23 `agent-action-admission.json` + `model-action-view-v2` (a connector action is admitted by adding a row); WS12 exhaustiveness and digest checks as CI-gate candidates.
- Consumes: WS05 class vocabulary/rows; WS13 approval floors; WS03 `waived` and durable resume; WS02 tool ordering/prompt cache (deterministic defs); WS08 reserved `attachments` and shared `code_interpreter` arm; WS01 Z-18 landed + deployed stack; WS12; DEC-10 (OD2). Cross-session: Space session (a) owns Space authority, `agent_action_entitled`, Space files UI and promote UX (ledger.md:3437 needs product input); Control user-core files are edited additively (one owner per PR) and `spaces.*` actions (F-t1-019) add rows to the same table, not code; Quarry (b): none, browser actions stay `none` in the contract (overlap C6, 2.6 row 14: Model consumes only capability-core rows).

**ZDR clause and regression guards**
- ZDR (DEC-24, DEC-25, DEC-32): workspace tools are not offered and not executed under ZDR or a waived run (hydrate writes plaintext Space content to host disk and acquires a durable lease row); `RunAgent` already refuses ZDR; admission view `Validate` rejects `ZeroDataRetention`; every contract row carries `zdr_allowed:false`; no new persistence (overlay rows already exist; S1 adds counters only); typed key `workspace_tools` [live-only] once Z-18 lands.
- Audit s8 strengths: signed fail-closed capability gate (advertised -> bound -> seeded -> attested tests extended `agent.rs:3805-3880`, `an_unpinned_server_action_is_dropped`, `TestModelActionViewFailsClosedWithoutControlVerifier`); Ask/Do switch, plan gate, autonomy ladder (`autonomy_ladder_contract.rs` unchanged, `plan_mode_refuses_workspace_edit_but_allows_workspace_read`, `leaf_subagent_may_grep_but_not_edit`); two loops separate (no write or workspace tool in model-gateway; `inline_loop_rejects_side_effects_that_require_governed_agentic_approval` `tool_loop.rs:9105` unchanged; contract test read-only over arms); honest outcomes (`durability: "run_overlay"`, `workspace_upload_failed_total`, tool result never says "saved to Space"); credential isolation (no new service credential, no service-asserted identity from exec-core: DEC-25 refuses it); kernel-enforced sandbox / Space CAS (file tools are pure file ops under canonical root, no symlinks, no network; `diff_and_upload` + Promote remain the only durable path); typed envelopes / action contracts first (typed side map, hashed contract file).

**Top risks**
- Edits are scratch without a promote caller (DEC-24 D1). Mitigation: truthful `durability` text, no promote claim, S4 behind `all`; product owner picks explicit accept with F-t3-076 before the product route exposes writes.
- Hot-spot conflicts in `agent.rs`/`mod.rs`/`grpc.rs` with Z-18, WS03, WS05, and admission table widening authority by accident. Mitigation: land-first order, new files (`workspace_edit.rs`, `workspace_tools.rs`, `owner_actions.rs`, `owner_reads.rs`), three small hunks; default `tickets_only`, exec-core pins (name, hash, schema, adapter) so a compromised catalog cannot add or reshape a tool, byte-identical `tickets.create` proof, service-asserted identity refused, ZDR rejection kept.
- Hash-domain/redaction surprises and classification drift (the unexecutable seven). Mitigation: S1 corpus test first, `secret_pattern` names the class not the secret, never write redacted text silently; exhaustive `Record<ActionId,...>`, vendored contract with digest pin, contract tests in four planes, demotion recorded explicitly per id, never silent.

### WS25 - Org agent policy registry, loop guard, durable hooks, tool-output handles
**Resolves:** F-t3-032 (Org agent policy registry, XL, Recommended), F-t3-063 (loop guard, durable org hooks, universal tool-output handles, L, Recommended) | **Owner:** Model Plane: capability-core (registry of record, signed gate), execution-core (hook firing, Do loop, run-scoped handles), model-gateway (mode resolution, policy cache, inline loop, admin proxy), mp-contracts (pure helpers); Frontend Plane (BFF routes, action contracts, Customize hub, mode picker); Control Plane supplies identity facts only (later audit sink via WS06) | **Wave:** 2 | **Type:** S0-S5 and S12 FINISH (S1-S4 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK: guard/handles exist as private helpers, hook engine exists but is unwired); S6-S11 BUILD-NEW | **Effort:** XL, about 70-95 engineer-days (S0 2-3, S1 5-7, S2 4-6, S3 5-7, S4 5-7, S5 8-10, S6 5-7, S7 8-10, S8 5-7, S9 8-12, S10 4-6, S11 8-12, S12 3-4); critical path S0 -> S3, S6, S7, S8, S9, S10, S11, S12 about 45-55 days with two engineers | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline); WS03 (`waived` signature rebase, S2/S3/S5); WS05 S1 (declared tool class for D7 and `result_query` row), S2-S5 (`pinned_digest`, `tool_policies`, admin-scope Go twin; S9); WS04 (replica-safe registry hydration, S8); WS02 (RunAgentRequest tag reservation, params struct); WS10 (`revision` reference, settings-page pattern); WS12 (UI gates, S10/S11); WS26 (normaliser before S4) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-14 (WS25-D1, capability-core as system of record; gates S7-S12, must be recorded in S0), DEC-15 (WS25-D3 strictest-wins, D4 fail-closed, D7 post-tool withhold; gates S3, S6), DEC-13 (WS25-D8 fail-closed fetch shape; S9). Local: WS25-D2 (no webhook hooks, S8), D5 (4 mode ids, labels non-blocking, S6), D6 (locks are ceilings, S7), D9 (`loop_detected` terminal code, S2), D10 (JSON `agent_mode` carrier, S6), D11 (plugins key `reserved`, S7). Slices S0-S6 start with no decision.

**Verified starting point:**
- Hook engine is real but unwired for Do: `EC/hook/mod.rs` `evaluate` 109-135 (first match wins 127-131, malformed context allows 117-121, unknown token allows 103-107); fires in `EC/runtime_loop/mod.rs` `execute_step_inner` pre 544-550, post 782-787. The Do loop passes `""`: `EC/runtime_loop/agent.rs:1919-1923` (live 1923-1927); `RunAgentRequest` has no hook field (highest tag 17, `MP/proto/model_plane/v1/execution.proto`; `ExecuteStepRequest.hook_context = 6` at 285). Gateway paths already forward rules (`MG/tools.rs:207-211`, `MG/browser_run.rs:417-427`, both `permission_mode "auto"`).
- Gateway hook registry is in-memory and order-nondeterministic: `MG/runtime_registries.rs:2002-2011` (`DashMap<(org, hook_id), Hook>`), `hook_context_json` 2030-2060; `Hook` (gateway.proto:1262-1270) fields 1-7, no priority. gRPC `register_hook`/`list_hooks` (`MG/grpc.rs:2043-2060`) check only `gateway:write/read`, no org-admin check; no HTTP/BFF/UI. Durable table `hook_configs` (`SC/migrations/0004_tasks_cron_hooks_skills_memory.sql:164-185`) is unused (only `SC/src/gdpr.rs:376`).
- Modes: `EC/permission/mod.rs:5-20` `from_wire` unknown becomes Auto; `check_autonomy_rung` 124-141 treats UNSPECIFIED as Ok while `mp_contracts::autonomy::rank` (autonomy.rs:45-52) ranks it narrowest (so an org rung cap must always send an explicit rung). Do loop `resolve_mode` (`agent.rs:3396-3403`) is already fail-closed (audit stale). Posture seams: `MG/sse.rs:1128-1131`, rung at `:8658-8665`; `MG/profile.rs` (`from_wire` 38-43, `floor_from_scopes` 95, `resolve_posture` 119); invoke request `profile` at `MG/http_routes.rs:6668`, `plan_mode` 6709.
- Existing org surface the card omits: gateway.proto:161-166 RPCs, `OrgPolicy` 1343-1350, `set_policy` (`MG/grpc.rs:2202-2262`, `unowned_policy_fields` 154-161 refuses by name what nothing enforces; precedent to copy).
- Loops/handles: `duplicate_call_signature` private at `MG/tool_loop.rs:2304-2322` (used 7085-7092, error at 7113-7121); `retrieval_signature` private at `EC/runtime_loop/agent.rs:3449-3466` (audit wrong that they are in mp-contracts). `RoundsOutcome` 434-458, `OutOfTime` is the typed-stop precedent. Handles: `MG/tool_result_handles.rs` (JSON only; TTL 900 s line 53, `MAX_LIVE_HANDLES` 512), `handle_or_inline_output` `MG/tool_loop.rs:3798-3832`, `fetch_url` arm 2996-3060 cuts to `MAX_FETCH_CHARS = 4_000` (126); `knowledge_search` 3347 and `web_search` 2848 not parked. Do loop `MAX_TOOL_CONTEXT_CHARS = 8000` (`agent.rs:279`).
- [live-only] ZDR set must land first (`MG/zdr_posture.rs`, `zdr_features.rs`, `SC/src/retention_receipt.rs`, migrations 0044/0045, `mp-contracts/src/retention.rs`, `zdr_refusal.rs`, `EC/tests/zdr_refusal_inventory.rs`, `MP/api/contracts/zdr-refusals.json`); live-modified hot files: `agent.rs` +207, `runtime_loop/mod.rs` +18, `MG/tool_loop.rs` +425, `grpc.rs` +47, `http_routes.rs` +237. None touch hook/loop/handle/rung logic, only signatures. Audit stale: plan "B4" has no definition (it is S3 here); Appendix D F-t3-032 text truncated.

**Provides / consumes:**
- WS03: `RunAgentRequest.org_policy` (hook_context, revision, digest); a continuation MUST re-resolve policy at resume, never replay stored rules; LoopGuard and handle stores are per-process, excluded from the fold (fold replaces handle envelopes with an expiry note). WS05: strictest-wins evaluator reading class for D7; rule "hooks and org policy only narrow". WS13: typed `HookAsk{rule_id}` for cause `org_hook` (WS13 owns the cause enum). WS15: `failure_code = "loop_detected"`, `ToolRounds.loop_stop` (terminal-code widening is WS15's call, section 2.6 row 22). WS20: unattended runs carry `org_policy` + resolved mode. WS21-WS24: policy-schema registry (a stream adds a key with a fail-closed value + golden row), `ext.mcp_servers`, hooks by tool name for new tools. WS26: handles park only after `ToolProvenance::assess` on the full body, stored verdict replayed. WS06: content-free `org_agent_policy_changed` / `org_hook_changed` audit rows. Space session: `resolve` takes extra ceilings (tighten-only `space` source, keyed by `SpaceRef`; Space adopts `AgentMode` ids and shares hub components; deviation D1 vs "Control decides" recorded for owner ruling). Quarry session: org hooks may deny `browser_agent` by name, lockdown sets `do_runs=false`; per-site/web policy stays in org-core/Quarry (DEC-19).
- Consumes: WS01 (live ZDR set, deploy/proof harness), WS05 (class vocabulary, `capability_tools`, `pinned_digest`, `tool_policies`, admin-scope Go twin, migration numbering), WS03 (`waived`, resume), WS02 (tag reservation, `build_run_agent_request` params struct), WS10 (`revision`, settings pattern), WS12 (UI/copy gates), WS04 (replica-safe hydration). [RECONCILE] policy placement (org-core in WS10/WS21 vs capability-core here): section 2.6 row 7 (split by nature) and DEC-14.
- Edit-first order: WS05 class seam, then WS03 `waived`, then WS25 S2/S3/S5 in `agent.rs`/`runtime_loop/mod.rs`; WS05 first in `permission/mod.rs`, `hook/mod.rs` has one author (WS25); WS02 params struct first in `sse.rs`/`http_routes.rs`/`grpc.rs`; WS04 first in `runtime_registries.rs`; WS26 normaliser before S4; integrator reserves one `RunAgentRequest` tag, `Hook` 8-10 are WS25; WS05 first in `CC/migrations`, `server.go`, `engine.go`.

**ZDR clause and regression guards:**
- ZDR: loop-guard state is per-turn in-memory signature hashes, never persisted, audit detail hash-only (matrix row `loop_guard_state_never_persisted_under_zdr`); hook rules and org policy are org config, no content, apply to ZDR runs unchanged since they only narrow (`hook_context_applies_to_zdr_run`, `lockdown_blocks_do_run_for_zdr_org`); policy/hook writes refused for ZDR principals, reads allowed (`org_agent_policy_write_under_zdr_refused`, `org_hook_write_under_zdr_refused`); handles never park under ZDR and live only in process memory/run scope (`external_tool_result_not_parked_under_zdr`, `do_run_result_not_parked_under_zdr`); modes resolve identically (`agent_mode_resolves_identically_under_zdr`). Gates: live-only `MG/tests/zdr_posture_matrix.rs`, `EC/tests/zdr_refusal_inventory.rs`, `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`. Waived runs (DEC-32) count as ZDR for new durable stores, which here means none beyond the ones above.
- Audit section 8 strengths: signed fail-closed gate (`hook_allow_never_bypasses_the_signed_gate`, Go `engine_org_policy_test.go`, `autonomy_ladder_contract`); Ask/Do switch and per-call ladder (agent-modes golden, "no choice is byte-identical", no `full_access` mode); two loops separate (`cross_service_loop_contract.rs`, postmortem 0001); typed ChatEvent taxonomy (`gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check`, HEAD fails it per WS10, live passes, do not regress); honest outcomes (typed `loop_detected`, withheld-result notice states the action ran); research honesty (S4 grounding test); credential isolation (BFF no-forged-header tests); multi-tenant runtime (org-isolation tests per handler).

**Top risks:**
- Hot-file collisions (`agent.rs`, `runtime_loop/mod.rs`, `sse.rs`, `permission/mod.rs`): edit-first order, small additive hunks, rebase on WS03 `waived`.
- Fail-closed policy reads break Do availability, and the evaluator change (first-match to strictest-wins, fail-open to fail-closed) surprises existing rules: typed 503 with D8 stale bounds, no-row = defaults, shadow modes log `would_have_denied`, kill switch `ORG_AGENT_POLICY_MODE=off`, ledger entries; in-memory rules have no consumer today.
- Untrusted content parked then re-read unscreened, and ownership dispute over D1 after building: stored screening verdict + label on every slice + ZDR never parks; single `OrgAgentPolicy` read contract in mp-contracts keeps the producer swappable, D1 recorded in S0 before any store code.
NOT DETERMINED (design): Control-side minting of `admin`/`org:admin` scope names; whether capability-core tables use RLS; state access inside `execute_step_inner` for the `result_query` arm; plan-B4 definition; Appendix D F-t3-032 tail.

### WS26 - Content-safety hardening
**Resolves:** F-t3-033 (Recommended, L, W2) | **Owner:** Model Plane: model-gateway (screening enforcement, `ScreenContent` RPC), execution-core (Do seam), session-core (memory and skill write policy), inference-core (one new classifier operation), capability-core (policy row convention only); Frontend Plane (chat link rendering only) | **Wave:** 2 | **Type:** S1, S3, S9 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S2, S4-S8 BUILD-NEW | **Effort:** L, about 26-35 engineer-days (S1 1, S2 5-7, S3 1-2, S4 3-4, S5 2-3, S6 6-8, S7 4-5, S8 3-4, S9 0.5); critical path WS01-S0 -> S2 -> S6 -> S8 thresholds -> S7 -> default flip | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline) for any slice that edits a live-modified file (S4, S5, S6 tool_loop part, S7 runtime_loop part, S9); WS07 S1 (memory gate and source-scan test) and S7 (`skip_reason`, proposals) for S4/S5; WS05 (MCP through the signed gate; until then `ThirdPartyMcp` is framed, never withheld) for S7; WS06 (durable refusal rows); WS12 (FE test wiring) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-31 (WS26-D1 shared crate gates S2; D2 `tighten` default + opt-in `enforce` gates S6, S7; D3 fail-open gates S6; D4 mode in `config_json` gates S6; D5 model-origin org-scope refusal gates S4 scope rule; D6 skills gates S5; D7 spend gates S6 default-on). Local: WS26-D8 (S9, agreed, one line), D9 (report-only retro scan, no gate). S1, S3, S9 (once WS01-S0 lands) and the S4 content check need no decision; S2 corpus skeleton rides with S8.

**Verified starting point:**
- Two hand-synced scanner copies, no normalisation: `MG/moderation.rs:61-81` (16 lower-case phrases), `scan_injection` `:183-186`; `EC/provenance.rs:163-187` identical. Two `TrustClass` copies (`moderation.rs:205-239`, `provenance.rs:31-67`) pinned by name list in `EC/tests/compaction_parity_contract.rs:542` (text-parses both sources). Nothing handles NFKC, zero-width, bidi, U+E0000 tag block, homoglyphs or forged envelope text.
- Posture only changes framing: `ScreeningPosture` `moderation.rs:284-306`; `requires_read_only` `:308` has no production caller; a Flagged result still reaches the model with a NOTE (`tool_loop.rs:4502-4527`, `provenance.rs:265-300`). `screen_tool_payload` `moderation.rs:437-485` (4 MiB, 500 ms `SCREENING_DEADLINE` `:411-412`), policy read per call `:493-552`.
- AUDIT GAP: the semantic pass is not an injection classifier. `semantic_screening.rs:263` calls `AnalyzeLanguage` `operation:"content_safety"`; the prompt scores hate/harassment/violence/self_harm/sexual (`IC/provider/language.rs:638-652`); "ignore previous instructions" scores 0. It embeds page text in the classifier prompt (itself injectable), shares the 500 ms deadline (mostly `Timeout`), and is skipped under ZDR (`semantic_screening.rs:347`; `AnalyzeLanguageRequest` `inference.proto:1063-1090` has no zdr field).
- Shadow telemetry cannot form an evaluation set: `MP/deploy/nats.conf:17-25` lacks the `mp.v1.security.*` publish grant (`publishers_registry_test.go:150-152` records the gap; publish at `tool_loop.rs:7360-7369` logs and drops); events are metadata + BLAKE3 hashes only (`security_events.rs:1-18`), no consumer exists. Eval set must be a curated corpus + counters (S8).
- Chat-only wiring: `assess_external_payload` (`tool_loop.rs:2358-2403`) is the sole semantic caller (JoinHandle discarded `:2386`); Do mode (`EC/runtime_loop/mod.rs:797-801`, browser `:244-254`) runs only the local scan; execution-core has no policy or inference client (has a `ModelGatewayClient` channel, `EC/mcp_gateway.rs:16,51,82`).
- Memory/skill write holes: `IndexMemory` (`SC/memory_grpc.rs:441-513`) checks only non-empty + thread ownership; four callers (execution-core `execute_save_memory` `EC/runtime_loop/mod.rs:2154-2215`, delegation `agent.rs:3366`, gateway correct-memory `FE/apps/gateway/src/domains/memory.rs:97`, orchestrator `activities.go:812`); topic `POLICY`/`AGENT`/`WORKSPACE` maps to ORG scope (`SC/dreaming.rs:1536-1542`), so a poisoned page can persist an org-wide instruction. `upsert_agent_skill` (`SC/grpc.rs:4904-4977`) unchecked; learned skills inject as trusted text. Links: `FE/src/features/chat/components/chat-media-markdown.tsx:1269-1279` passes `[text](target)` straight to `href`; allowlist only in export (`ooxml.ts:115-120`). PII: `sse.rs:963` computes `pii_redaction_required`, agentic return `:1423` passes `req.content.clone()`, Ask redacts at `:1477`.
- Live-modified: `tool_loop.rs` (+425; fn `[L]2382`), `EC/runtime_loop/mod.rs` (seam `[L]811-815`, save_memory `[L]2172-2225`), `SC/memory_grpc.rs`, `SC/src/grpc.rs` (+861; upsert `[L]5075`), `dreaming.rs`, `sse.rs` (+883), `sessions.proto` (+42), `ChatMessages.tsx` (+229). [live-only]: `zdr_posture.rs`, `zdr_features.rs`, `zdr_precheck.rs`, `mp-contracts/tests/zdr_retention_matrix.rs`, migrations 0044/0045. Clean (start today): `moderation.rs`, `semantic_screening.rs`, `security_events.rs`, `provenance.rs`, `compaction_parity_contract.rs`, `memory.proto`, `inference.proto`, `gateway.proto`, `language.rs`, `nats.conf`, `chat-media-markdown.tsx`, `ooxml.ts`. Audit text stale twice on "evaluation set from shadow telemetry".

**Provides / consumes:**
- Provides: crate `mp-content-safety` (`scan`, `sanitize_for_model`, `TrustClass` incl. `ConnectorContent`, `PriorConversation`, `RoomMember`) to WS05, WS21, WS23, WS17, Space; rule: classify a new tool by adding one line to `trust.rs` + one case to the contract test, never a second classifier. `StepOutcome.screening_taint` and `TOOL_RESULT_SEMANTIC_ENFORCED` to WS13/WS05 (WS13 decides taint-to-ask; WS26 does not touch capability decisions). `write_policy::check`/`check_skill`, typed codes `memory_policy:*`/`skill_policy:*`, `IndexMemoryRequest.origin` to WS07 (its `learning.Screen` stays a no-op; its `skip_reason` carries ours). Content-free event schema and refusal log lines to WS06. `scan` for attachment text, bundle files, recall snippets to WS08/WS19/WS17. `injection_defense.config_json.mode` to WS25 as a candidate registry key (WS25 extends it, no second store). Ruling D8 + S9 to WS02. `isSafeLinkHref` + test as candidate FE gate rule to WS12.
- Consumes: WS01-S0; WS07 S1/S7; WS05; WS06; WS12; WS21/Quarry for browser tool names and optional `hidden_text`/`offscreen_text` flags. Quarry keeps capture-time masking (overlap-quarry #11), Model owns the persist/prompt layer; Space defines where `RoomMember` is applied and writes memory only via `IndexMemory` with `origin` set. [RECONCILE] loop separation vs shared crate: section 2.6 row 24 (share types and crates only, never dispatch code).
- Edit order: `MG/tool_loop.rs` small hunks around 2358-2403 and 4470-4527 after WS01-S0; `EC/runtime_loop/mod.rs` S7 touches only the seam and `execute_save_memory`; `SC/memory_grpc.rs`/`dreaming.rs`/`grpc.rs`: WS07 S1 gate first, then S4, then WS07 S7/S5; proto edits append at message ends, `skip_reason = 4` shared by name; `chat-media-markdown.tsx` S3 before WS09; `nats.conf` S1 one-line add, integrator merges lists.

**ZDR clause and regression guards:**
- ZDR: the deterministic local scan is pure CPU and always runs; page text is NEVER sent to inference-core on a ZDR turn (`AnalyzeLanguageRequest` has no zdr field, so the gateway must not call; semantic outcome `skipped_zdr`; ZDR chat drops web tools and Do mode is refused anyway); ZDR threads persist no memory or skills so write policies have nothing to see; refusal logs and events carry hash, counts and rule ids only, never text; envelopes suppressed by natsx under ZDR. Golden rows: `content_screening_semantic` (chat and Do variants) and `content_write_policy` in live-only `zdr_retention_matrix.rs` (until it lands, in-module tests that a ZDR thread id never reaches `check`).
- Audit section 8 strengths: two loops kept separate (S7 RPC is screening only, `dispatch_tool` untouched, `no_dispatch_tool_symbol_in_screening_client`, re-pointed `compaction_parity_contract`); signed capability gate (taint only informs; `autonomy_ladder_contract` green); ZDR by construction (per-slice skip tests); memory provenance (`dreaming.rs` tests `a_stated_memory_always_beats_an_inferred_one_on_the_same_key` 2176 and `only_inferred_candidates_carry_the_extractor_marker` 2220 unchanged); honest outcomes (typed refusals, partial coverage never Clean, unavailable semantic pass is a counter not "screened"); model proposes Quarry executes (no browser action path touched); rendering/accessibility (blocked link keeps text and title; `chat-markdown.test.ts`, `ChatMessages.codeblock.test.tsx` unchanged); credential isolation (classifier uses delegated inference bearer, policy read the capability bearer, no token in events or logs).

**Top risks:**
- Audit premise wrong (semantic pass is a toxicity classifier; shadow events cannot be replayed): new `prompt_injection` operation (S6), real corpus (S8), counters (S1); no default flips until S8 thresholds are archived.
- Latency, spend and false positives (inline LLM per external result; homoglyph/multilingual markers; pages that discuss attacks): deterministic-Clean-only trigger, own 2.5 s deadline, per-run and size caps, default off, fail-open, zero benign deterministic hits in CI, `report` before `enforce` for memory/skills, withhold is org opt-in.
- Classifier manipulation and hot-file/proto contention with WS07/WS17/WS05: tighten-only merge (a manipulated "clean" cannot loosen) + random delimiter + `classifier_manipulation_cannot_loosen_a_verdict`; edit-order table, small hunks, one shared gate function, append-only proto edits, start only after WS01-S0 for live-modified files.

### WS27 - Live run-cost meter and pre-run estimate
**Resolves:** F-t3-028 (Partial, L, W2) | **Owner:** Model Plane: model-gateway (meter, frames), inference-core (routing note), execution-core (Do-round events), cost-core (window); Control Plane quota semantics (org-core, billing-core read-only); Frontend Plane (SPA meter, BFF action contract). One meter, one ledger: meter in model-gateway, cost-core stays ledger of record (`MP/docs/capability-ownership-matrix.md` row 74; `apps/master-ownership-matrix.md` line 88) | **Wave:** 2 | **Type:** S0, S1, S2, S5, S8 FINISH-BUILT-BUT-DARK; S3, S4, S6, S7, S9, S10, S11 BUILD-NEW | **Effort:** L, about 43-62 engineer-days (S0 1-2, S1 3-4, S2 5-7, S3 3-5, S4 4-6, S5 2-3, S6 4-6, S7 4-6, S8 4-6, S9 6-8, S10 4-6, S11 3-4); critical path WS01-S0 -> S0 -> S1 -> S2 -> S3 -> S4, then S6 and S9 (S9 also needs WS02 S3); S5, S7, S8 parallel once S1 and decisions land | **Depends on:** WS01-S0 (land live baseline: `sse.rs`, `sse_events.rs`, `zdr_features.rs`, FE chat files); WS02 S1 (Usage cache keys, soft) and S3 (`RunAgentResponse.usage = 9`, `UsageTally`, billing decision); WS03 (resume keeps `run_id`); WS11 S5 (shared `RouteNote`); WS12 S7 (golden, soft); WS09 S2 (soft, FE first); WS13/WS14 (plan card ownership, S11); WS10 (S11) | **Gating decisions:** DEC-18 (WS27-D1 per-run vs lifetime gates S6 enforcement and S8; D2 gates S8; D4 ZDR in-stream cost gates S5; D5 billing effect gates S2 enablement; D6 stop-at-100% gates S6 enforce). DEC-39 (WS27-D8 tag reservation gates S7, S9). Local: WS27-D3 (null when unknown, gates S1), D7 (cumulative final frame, S2), D9 (member metric name, S10). No decision needed for S0-S4 build, S6 warn-only, S7 build/S9 build (tags reserved by integrator).

**Verified starting point:**
- Tool-decision rounds are never metered (audit says only "fires once at turn end"; it is worse): `run_tool_rounds_for_model` calls `client.infer` per round (`MG/tool_loop.rs:6677`), reads `resp` (6699-6709), only `tracing::debug!`s tokens (6823-6831); `ToolRounds` has no usage field (4565-4613); Usage envelope/frame built once from the final streamed chunk (`MG/sse.rs:3177-3198`, `3987-4001`, `4032-4041`, `4112-4120`). Other un-metered provider calls (to classify): `MG/sse.rs:7551`, `:7782`, `:7811`, `:7898`, `MG/result_validation.rs:62,66`, forced web search `MG/tool_loop.rs:5157`.
- `ChatEvent::Usage` has no cache fields (`MG/sse_events.rs:228-244`, `361-363`, `515-540`); SPA parses `cache_read_tokens`/`cache_write_tokens` but `onUsage` drops them (`FE/src/shared/api/chat-client.ts:323-339`, `1355-1367`; `use-chat-controller.ts:3187-3206`); "Prompt cache" metric hidden (not zero) when both 0 (`ChatPanels.tsx:908-941`). `build_usage_envelope` carries no `run_id` (`MG/sse.rs:9792-9847`) though cost-core decodes it (`CC/cmd/main.go:90`), so chat rows have empty `run_id` and `GET /api/v1/cost/run` (`CC/internal/server/server.go:369-407`) is empty for chat.
- Pricing: `PricingCache::cost_usd_for` `Some(0.0)` for subscription, `None` when cold; but `lookup` falls back to the catalogue `default` row for any unknown model (`MG/pricing.rs:213-262`, `337-359`), so unknown models get a confident wrong cost (Gemini turns too; ledger records "default ~25x too high for embeddings", `MP/docs/decisions/ledger.md:824-829`). Cache legs correct (`cost_from_rate` 314-333).
- Budget semantics mismatch (audit silent): ceiling keys `max_cost_per_run_usd_micros`, `max_tokens_per_run` (`MG/org_quota.rs:23-26`), but cost-core `handleBudgetCheck` compares the cap to ACCUMULATED totals with no time or run filter (`CC/internal/server/server.go:585-614`; `CC/internal/postgres/store.go:195-218`); `org_quota.rs` reads only `key`+`limit` (59-63) so `reset_period` (`apps/Control Plane/org-core/internal/org/types.go:33-41`) is never honoured; `AggregateFilter` already has `Since`/`Until` (`CC/internal/ledger/ledger.go:239-246`). Gateway gate: `MG/sse.rs:1085-1118`, `MG/budget.rs:48-80`, `MG/org_quota.rs:112-157`.
- ZDR branch returns before the budget gate (`MG/sse.rs:976`), emits no envelope and states no cost (`zdr_answer_stream` [L] `MG/sse.rs:8521-8538`, `DirectAnswer` 7670-7681; `MP/docs/ZDR.md:102`; cost-core rejects ZDR envelopes `CC/cmd/main.go:492-493`; audit's "envelopes suppressed" is imprecise: none exists). Do path emits Usage with tokens 0, cost null and a stale comment (`MG/sse.rs:9705-9737`).
- Silent downgrade: `BudgetPosture::Constrained` at >= 0.8 x cap routes one tier cheaper, `Exhausted` forces `cheap_fallback`; posture only logged (`IC/provider/intent.rs:176-218`, `236-269`, `211-216`; `fallback.rs:1011-1041`; 0.8 constant `routing_policy.rs:126-134`); two budget authorities with different failure semantics (gateway fail-closed, inference-core `BudgetClient` 400 ms falls back to Unknown).
- Do: per-round usage only logged (`EC/runtime_loop/agent.rs:1450-1463`); `RunAgent` unary (`execution.proto:34`); precedent for live Do events `publish_plan_transition` (`agent.rs:4909-4949`) -> `RecordOrchestrationEvent` (`session-core/src/orchestration_grpc.rs:3192-3232`) -> `follow_agentic_run` (`MG/sse.rs:9020-9046`, `9066-9100`). Member quota route exists unused: BFF `GET /api/v1/billing/quotas/{metric}` (`FE/apps/gateway/src/domains/billing.rs:55-65`, `120-135`) returns `QuotaStatus{limit,used,remaining,is_exceeded,utilization}`. SPA says cost is "never estimated" (`ChatMessages.tsx:1726` per sweep).
- [live-only]: `MG/sse_events.rs` (M, `Usage.retention_class` [L] 283-285), `MG/sse.rs` (+781; Usage sites [L] 4242, 6983, 8525, 10077), `MG/zdr_features.rs` `usage` row "No cost is stated" ([L] 169-172), `zdr_posture.rs`, `tests/zdr_posture_matrix.rs`, FE `chat-client.ts` (+52), `ChatMessages.tsx` (+181), `use-chat-controller.ts` (+4), `AgentRunConsole.tsx`, `MP/docs/sse-event-taxonomy.md` (M; at HEAD `gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check` fails). Clean in live: cost-core, billing-core, org-core, `budget.rs`, `org_quota.rs`, `pricing.rs`, `intent.rs`. "Plan I16" (card 760) not found in audit `final/`/`cons/`: design built from card text and code. Audit stale: "fires once" omits answer-call-only scope; "per-run aggregate in ledger" empty for chat; "quotas with reset period" display only; "Prompt cache stays at zero" is hidden not zero; downgrade is in `intent.rs:211-216`.

**Provides / consumes:**
- Provides: WS09 `usage_progress` frame, `Message.meter`, cumulative `usage` (`calls`, `cost_by_category`); WS15 closed `warning`/`routing` tokens (counts only; WS27 does not use `context_change`, WS15 may mirror later); WS11 single `RouteNote.applied_effort` + posture clamp visibility; WS20 `RunCeiling` resolution reusable for a scheduled run's cap, run usage keyed by `run_id`; WS02 extends its `RunAgentResponse.usage` and Usage cache keys with progress and pricing, chat envelopes gain `run_id`; WS21/Quarry cost-core ledger key `run_id` and ceiling value to be min-ed with grant claims (Quarry enforces browser step cost; overlap row 13: `max_cost_usd` enforced nowhere today; Quarry expected `ValidateGrantResponse` v2 `budget{max_cost_usd,max_runtime_s}`); WS10 priced-model set = entitled set, honest-null rule; Space session `run_id` join key, extensible payload (WS27 adds no space/agent dims; Space adds no second usage frame, overlap row 16).
- Consumes: WS01-S0 (live ZDR wave); WS02 S1/S3 (cache keys, `RunAgentResponse.usage = 9`, `UsageTally`, D1 billing = WS27-D5 same family); WS03 (per-segment usage under one `run_id`, `extensions.metering` placeholder); WS11 (`effort::allowance`, posture clamp, shared `RouteNote`); WS12 (taxonomy gate, payload golden, `spa-ui-gates`); WS13/WS14 (plan card); Control (org-core quota rows with `reset_period`, billing-core `QuotaStatus`; org-core is the one quota source, overlap row 15).
- [RECONCILE] proto tags: `InferChunk` 16, `InferResponse` 15, `OrchestrationEvent` 27 vs WS03 24/25, WS15 26, WS11 applied-effort: section 2.6 row 1 (integrator reserves, one shared `RouteNote`). Billing effect of metering tool rounds vs WS02 D1: DEC-18.
- Edit order: `MG/sse_events.rs`: WS01 S0, WS02 S1 (cache fields), then fenced `usage progress (WS27)` block, second lander regenerates golden + taxonomy; `MG/sse.rs`/`tool_loop.rs`: WS01 lands live set, WS02 first, WS27 hunks are calls into `usage_meter.rs`/`run_ceiling.rs`; FE `chat-client.ts`/`use-chat-controller.ts`/`ChatMessages.tsx`/`ChatPanels.tsx`: WS09 S2 first, add-only hunks; `inference.proto`/`fallback.rs`/`provider/mod.rs`: integrator reserves, one `RouteNote`; `orchestration.proto`/`orchestration_grpc.rs`: append-only, tag 27 proposed; `agent.rs`: WS03 first; compose passthrough lines only.

**ZDR clause and regression guards:**
- ZDR: no tool loop runs on ZDR turns, so no extra envelopes and no `usage_progress` exist there (golden `usage_envelopes_are_not_published_for_a_zdr_turn`, ZDR emits no `usage_progress` in `zdr_feature_table.rs`); the only ZDR frame is the single `usage`, which under S5 states an ephemeral cost computed from counts + model id (numbers only, nothing stored, no envelope, cost-core would reject one; persistence stays DEF-Z-06b; reverses "no cost is stated", DEC-18); ZDR still bypasses the budget gate and ledger so ceilings and admin dashboards do not cover ZDR spend (state in ZDR.md); Do runs are refused under ZDR, `RunUsageProgress` is counts only and not persisted; route note carries posture + booleans only. Gates: `zdr_feature_table.rs`, `zdr_posture_matrix.rs` case `zdr_usage_states_cost_but_publishes_no_envelope`, `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`.
- Audit section 8 strengths: honest outcomes (cost null when unknown, basis marks subscription: S1 tests plus existing `usage_cost_basis_explains_a_subscription_cost_and_is_otherwise_optional` `sse_events.rs:1631`, `ChatMessages.metrics.test.tsx`, `CostDashboardPage.test.tsx:45`); typed ChatEvent taxonomy + byte-verbatim relay (taxonomy `--check`, WS12 payload golden, `streams.rs` untouched, `ag_ui.rs` test); two loops separate (meter in gateway loop, Do progress via events, `cross_service_loop_contract.rs` unchanged, execution-core only publishes counts); intent routing with provenance (S7 reads, never replaces; `intent.rs` tests `constrained_budget_downgrades_one_tier`, `exhausted_budget_forces_cheap_fallback` unchanged); calm workspace/live region (S4 announces on warning change only); credential isolation (S10 uses BFF delegated path, action contract test).

**Top risks:**
- Ledger and billing jump when tool rounds become metered (S2) and lifetime-vs-per-run budget confusion (D1/D2, S6 warnings can disagree with the 402 gate until S8): flag default off, Control confirms D5 attribution first, frame fix ships independently, evidence compares row counts flag off vs on; ADR in S0, wording says "this run", S8 before enforcement default flips.
- Hot-file conflicts in `sse.rs`, `sse_events.rs`, `chat-client.ts`, `inference.proto` with six other streams plus uncommitted live hunks: land the live wave first, new modules with thin call-ins, fenced blocks, one `RouteNote`, integrator-reserved tags.
- Honesty regressions (progress frame summing an unpriced call, `$0` for a subscription, an upper bound shown as cost) and the standing ZDR gap (ZDR turns bypass budget gate and ledger): pure `UsageMeter` rules + contract tests in S1/S3/S11 + SPA tests in S4; S5 only displays cost, ZDR.md states the gap, `DEF-Z-06b` is an owner decision outside this stream.

### 3.3 Waves 3-5 as epics (audit W3, W4, W5)

Source: audit sections 9.1 (W3, W4, W5 tables), 9.3, 9.4, 10.1 at `C:/dev/verevon-audit-2026-10-14/03-model-plane-chat-and-agents-audit.md`; the cards of the W3/W4 items; `common/overlap-space.md` and `common/overlap-quarry.md`; the decision register in section 2 (`DEC-nn`). Prerequisites are named as `WSnn` (the 27 designs in section 3, waves W1-W2) and `DEC-nn`.

#### 3.3.0 What this section is, and is not

- W3 (23 items) and W4 (12 items) are NOT file-level designed. Nothing below has the evidence level of a `WSnn` design: no slice list verified against the tree, no migration numbers, no proto tags. Each epic states what must be designed before the first line of code. Paths quoted are the audit cards' own citations, marked as the audit gave them; they were not re-opened for this compile. Re-verify before design.
- Effort band = the audit's verified bands of the member items, aggregated by judgement. Audit risk 10.1 #7: 30 of 40 revised estimates went up, mostly because items bundle separable deliverables. Plan every XL as the split given here, never as one unit. The aggregate is not a new estimate.
- W3/W4 items are sequenced by the audit's judgement [INF]. Four trunks gate almost everything: the durable loop (`WS03`, F-t3-002), declared tool classes (`WS05`, F-t3-003), the sandbox computer (`WS22`, F-t3-009) and the history foundation (`WS17`, F-t3-012). A slip in any of them moves the epics hanging on it (audit 9.4 #1, #4, #5; 10.1 #6).
- The 35 W3/W4 items form 14 epics; every item sits in exactly one.
- DEC-40 ("Parked") lists topics no W1-W2 design covers: share model, LLM reviewer, Channel Plane, Gemini/Vertex EU, wide-research gate, BYO metering. Epics EP-4, EP-11, EP-12 and EP-13 each depend on a DEC-40 topic that has no builder yet.
- ZDR: every epic adds new stores or egress. Each carries an explicit ZDR clause and a case in the resolver's golden matrix (audit 10.1 #2; DEC-32 for waived runs). Where the audit says an item is refused under ZDR, that refusal is a requirement, not an option.

#### 3.3.1 Epic index

#### 3.3.2 Epics

### EP-1 Computer track: takeover, live panel, replay, desktop
- Items: F-t3-026 (takeover and hand-back, M), F-t3-035 (live computer panel with typed viewers and PTY exec, L), F-t3-050 (scrubbable browser-run replay, M), F-t3-024 (computer use in the sandbox desktop, XL).
- Owner: Model Plane (pause and resume, tool surface, lease-bound guest executor), Ingestion Plane (Quarry owns control mode, enforcement and epoch), Frontend Plane (`ChatLiveRunPanel`, takeover card), Endpoint Plane only if user-desktop control is ever unparked (DEC-22 parks it; do not plan it).
- Prerequisites: `WS21` (agent browser on real grants; S5a/S5b Do tools, S6 consent card and live-panel binding, S9 interim human login hand-off), `WS22` (S6 typed lifecycle and `sandbox_state`; S7 SandboxBackend trait and `SandboxFailureCause`, which WS22 lists as the seam for F-t3-024/035), `WS03` (takeover reuses `/continue` and the single-use approval consume), `WS14` (`QuestionCard` primitive; browser `human_assist` uses approvals, not questions), `WS16` (human-assist modelled as approval rows so it surfaces in the attention rail), DEC-19 (site policy ownership), DEC-20 (browser Do tools server-bound), DEC-21 (secret custody), DEC-22 (sandbox backend reversal, computer-use scope), DEC-23 (thread workspace). Audit 9.4 #4: the order is strict: F-t3-008 then 026; F-t3-009 then 035 then 024. Nearly all XL cost of 024 sits in F-t3-009 (`WS22`) and must not be counted twice.
- Cross-document: overlap-quarry C4: Quarry's own takeover enforcement (initiator from principal, `USER_TOOK_OVER`, control epoch; F-t2-013 phase 1) must land before 026, because the audit's premise "Quarry already covers it" is false until then. Quarry plan owns enforcement and epoch; Model plan owns the request/resume card; attach/delegation is a Model Plane grant child (overlap-quarry A4). Overlap-quarry A3: split `ChatLiveRunPanel`/`ChatPage` (this epic) from `BrowserChrome` (Quarry plan); both consume one `BrowserSessionViewModel`.
- First slice (S-M): wire the existing manual takeover toggle. Per the card, `ChatLiveRunPanel` mounts `BrowserChrome` with pause and resume but passes no `onBrowserControlMode`, so the toggle is disabled; the BFF already returns 409 to agent actions while a human holds control (audit citations `apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/apps/gateway/src/domains/browser.rs:690-715`, Quarry `step_receipts.rs:55-95`). Then 026 proper: `human_assist` approval kind from `ChallengeSignal`, resume with the user's note and a fresh snapshot; human-typed input never reaches the model.
- Slice order: (1) 026 takeover; (2) 050 slider and per-action brief over existing events (`browser_events.rs` dispatched/decided/observation with `screenshot_ref`); (3) 035 viewer registry keyed on ToolIntent (browser, process, file kinds) plus the missing human output read path for the read-only terminal; (4) 024 step 1 (typed connector, screen hierarchy into the Do prompt and capability policy); desktop surface only after 009 and 035; coordinate fallback stays off until an ADR.
- Top risks: (1) authority creep and prompt injection: on-screen text in a guest desktop, login carry-over after takeover (needs an expiry), `capability_policy` fails closed on an unmapped tool id so computer use needs a new capability id; (2) ZDR: terminal output, recordings and the process registry become new stores; no clip exists under ZDR by construction (050); retention and redaction policy must precede 050; (3) vision cost and a strictly ordered chain: a slip in `WS22` or `WS21` moves all four items.
- Pull forward: 050's scrubber alone is S (events and screenshots exist; audit quick win); it can follow `WS21` S6. Read-only terminal viewer can follow `WS22` S6 once the process-output read path exists. The takeover toggle wiring (above) is S and belongs to `WS21` S6.
- Must be designed before starting: the 026 `human_assist` kind (one approval-kind registry with `WS13`/`WS14`/`WS16`); the viewer-registry contract and redacted-output read path (035); the executor protocol and guest-agent lifecycle for 024, which needs the DEC-22 ADR and the S8 microVM spike. 024 is not startable until DEC-22 closes.

### EP-2 Research and citations
- Items: F-t3-054 (web reading upgrade, M), F-t3-027 (deep research v2, XL), F-t3-053 (claim-level citations, M), F-t3-036 (wide research, L).
- Owner: Model Plane (model-gateway `deep_research.rs`/`tool_loop.rs`, execution-core `subagent/research_fan_out.rs`), Data Plane (knowledge sources, passage viewer through the Data Plane read API), Frontend Plane (plan card, passage viewer, `ChatWideResearchPanel`). Quarry owns the scrape contract and SSRF vetting; Model owns the tools and research runs (overlap-quarry lesser overlaps).
- Prerequisites: `WS23` (connector reads; F-t3-027 needs F-t3-010), `WS26` (content safety; every fetched chunk is untrusted and screened; F-t3-033 should land before F-t3-054, audit 10.1 #4), `WS27` (cost meter and estimate; F-t3-036 depends on F-t3-028), `WS03`/`WS04` (durable loop and replica-safe control for run-based fan-out; 036 depends on F-t3-002), `WS02` (cache and context), DEC-02 (the 300 s delegated bearer bounds fan-out scale until a renewable bearer), DEC-26 and DEC-32 (connector reads, ZDR on connector/web reads), DEC-40 (OD7 wide-research spend gate: recommendation is a separate child budget and no confirm gate in v1), DEC-18 (cost semantics).
- First slice: 054 (M, no audit dependencies): outline plus first chunk plus reference id instead of the 4,000-character cut (`MAX_FETCH_CHARS` at `tool_loop.rs:124-126`), `read_page` and `find_in_page` over the Quarry artifact id the gateway already resolves, batched `web_search` with stable reference ids. Overlap-quarry D10: Quarry's inline return (F-t2-005) precedes the ZDR buffer path; the non-ZDR artifact path works today.
- Slice order: 054; 053 chip binding (the client drops the server's `number`, `chat-client.ts:953-969`; this half is client-only); 027 in three slices (editable plan turn then run turn re-clamped server-side; in-process second pass driven by the coverage shortfall inside the wall clock; knowledge and connector sources under viewer-ACL retrieval); 036 entry point, estimate card, spend check, per-item retry, replay after reload. 027 mid-run steering is demoted: queued input lands only at tool-round boundaries.
- Top risks: (1) the pipeline dies with its SSE stream, so no plan or spend gate can wait inside it; moving research onto a durable run (the `WS03` fold) is a decision, not a refactor; (2) ZDR: deep research is refused under ZDR, so no claim ledger persists there; connector and web reads in non-ZDR runs persist truncated tool output into step records, so F8 redaction (`WS23` S1) must land before connector sources (audit 9.3 #10); (3) cost: two-pass research, N delegated children and unmetered fan-out (cap 16 today); the org cap must clamp client-requested counts server-side.
- Pull forward: 054 immediately after `WS26` (it has no dependencies). 053 chip-binding half needs no research epic. 036's composer entry point and estimate card can follow `WS27` S3/S4 without waiting for 027.
- Must be designed before starting: where research state lives (run record vs session-core fold vs in-memory); the claim-to-source ledger schema and its erase contract; `knowledge_only` scope is rejected with deep research today (`sse.rs:945-985` per the card), so source-scope semantics need a design; the OD7 spend gate.

Proposed owner in overlap-space row 13: artifact revisions are Model Plane; Data owns authored docs and collaborative documents. Settle in design, not by default.

Then user editing in the Output panel, selection comments and selection-scoped AI edits; annotation; collaborative documents last (own design).

- Pull forward: drafts per chat thread (S, FE-only, top frustration) can ship with `WS17` S2. Revision store can start once `WS17` S3 lands.

Selection-to-context and the attributed image strip are separate later pieces.

The dedicated user-content origin, viewer-bound connectors and key-value store are deferred.

They are different records; keep them separate and settle the join in DEC-14. A second definition store in capability-core is a named risk.

Run now must pass the same Control fire authorisation as a cron fire and never call `StartWorkflow` directly. Fleet console: proxy behind a Quarry-audience BFF or remove; the default in overlap-quarry A12 is hide until Quarry has a fleet model.

Phase 3: persistent named agents with scoped memory, after EP-8 and `WS20`.
