- Pull forward: phase 1 as soon as `WS20` S3 and S5 are in; it adds almost no new authority.
- Must be designed before starting: the named-agent authority model; the definition schema and version semantics; the Fleet keep/remove decision.

### EP-7 Unattended autonomy: goals and event triggers
- Items: F-t3-037 (persistent goals with a token budget, XL), F-t3-047 (event-triggered automations, XL; trimmed to trigger, consumer, wake gate and budgeted run it is L).
- Owner: Model Plane (session-core Goal record and ledger, capability-core cron and Watch, orchestrator-core workflow), Ingestion Plane (normalised connector events), Control Plane (signed per-continuation and per-fire decisions).
- Prerequisites: `WS20` (S4-S8 consent contract, fire/step re-verification, guards), `WS03` (S8 parking: credential window and crash interruption), `WS27` (cost ledger and ceilings: goal budget comes from cost-core run usage), `WS02` (F-t3-001), DEC-02 (OD8 renewable bearer, a hidden prerequisite), DEC-16 (stored consent), DEC-17, DEC-18, DEC-10 (OD2 Do on the product route).
- First slice (L): the audit's attended slice for 037: a Goal record and ledger in session-core (non-ZDR only), plus a goal bar in the Agent Run Console that auto-continues while the session still holds delegated authority. Judge next (prefer the proof bundle and postcondition results, rubric fallback through inference-core), then an unattended driver modelled on `ScheduledRunSupervision` that takes fresh Control authorisation per continuation. 047 first slice: trigger record bound to one connection, Space and member implementing `FireAuthorizer`, durable JetStream consumer of `webhook_received` (new nats-provisioner binding), dedupe on `webhookEventId`, payload fetched through the integration-corev2 API and classified untrusted, run started through the scheduled-run path under budget and org quota.
- [RECONCILE] The Watch source-kind design is written twice (Space F-t1-036/047 and F-t3-047); overlap-space row 15 gives capability-core (cron plus Watch) as the single owner with Control's shared-room decision family. The current Watch model is one resource, one Space, one member and the Control validators accept personal threads only for schedule/watch decisions (overlap-space row 15): shared-room triggers wait on that Control epic.
- Top risks: (1) authority: never auto-continue across approval-required effects; a stored record is not authority; the Watch primitive deliberately refuses org-wide subscriptions; (2) the 300 s bearer: runs that pause for hours or start unattended need the OD8 renewable credential, with no service-principal shortcut; (3) event storms and cost; ZDR must come from policy because there is no user turn; goal rows are a new store.
- Pull forward: nothing before `WS20` S5. The attended goal bar can be prototyped after `WS03` S7 if the judge is stubbed.
- Must be designed before starting: the Goal state machine and budget semantics on the Subscription route (a no-op there per the card); the trigger schema and which event types exist (only three in code, no mail-received event [INF], so mail triggers need an Ingestion change first); wake-gate predicate language.

Exclude Space-thread and `agent_run` threads from user-scope extraction (the card verifies Dreaming loads pending messages across all threads with no Space predicate, `dreaming.rs:1263-1285` as cited). Overlap-space D3: this one fix is both F-t1-012 slice 1 and F-t3-060 step 1 and must land before any personal agent, room agentic run or Spaces as project collaboration. Then the migration adding `(scope_kind, scope_id)` plus an isolation flag; overlap-space row 6 proposes one session-core migration carrying the audience predicate as well.

### EP-9 Policy, egress and compliance
- Items: F-t3-043 (egress tiers and credential-injecting proxy, XL), F-t3-055 (web access governance and provider transparency, L), F-t3-062 (enterprise compliance posture, XL), F-t3-068 (policy and data-flow transparency, L).
- Owner: Control Plane (org-core policy records, signed decisions, retention and hold, audit), Model Plane (enforcement at the loops and the sandbox network boundary), Ingestion Plane (Quarry re-check), Support Plane (signed releases per the audit's plane list), Frontend Plane (admin views).
- Prerequisites: `WS25` (F-t3-032 registry; S9 enforcement; policy only tightens vs the server floor; webhook hooks deferred to F-t3-059), `WS22` (F-t3-009 for 043), `WS06` (F-t3-004; the `retentionFor(org)` seam for retention and legal hold), `WS01` (F-t3-005 ZDR attested, F-t3-039 signed release), `WS10`, `WS13`, DEC-14, DEC-15, DEC-19 (web/site policy ADR: Control org-core as record; schema owned by Quarry's F-t2-022 thin slice; overlap-quarry A6), DEC-21 (custody), DEC-33, DEC-34, DEC-35.
- [RECONCILE] Credential custody: F-t3-043 part (c) names a Control secret store; DEC-21 puts provider tokens and BYO keys in integration-corev2 custody and site passwords in the Quarry vault. One custody ADR before 043(c) and F-t3-057.
- [RECONCILE] Policy store: F-t3-055 stores web policy in org-core (read through a fail-closed endpoint, not a bearer claim) while `WS25` D1 stores the agent policy in capability-core. Both stay; the schema registry (`WS25`) takes web policy keys later.
- First slice (suggested order, separable): (1) 055 org web policy record plus loop enforcement in both web paths (inline loop and execution-core) plus the "Searched via X" line; (2) 068 effective-policy origin view for approval posture, ZDR, privacy tier, routing and budgets, and the egress catalogue with CI check; (3) 043 closed tier enum (unknown means Disabled), per-call enforcement report in tool cards, then a real network boundary and the injecting proxy; (4) 062 retention windows and legal hold sweeper (dry-run, audited, propagating to Data Plane indexes and derived memory).
- Verified gaps to close (audit cards): bwrap unshares the network only for `Disabled`, so a proxy variable is advisory; Control grants `space:egress` to any non-default claim with no org entitlement (`user-core/.../space_capability_decision.go`); `IssueSpaceCapabilityDecision` requires a personal thread, so shared-room sandbox/egress decisions cannot be issued (overlap-space row 11, Control epic "shared-Space decision family").
- Top risks: (1) a loose allow-list turns the egress proxy into an exfiltration path; any tier above off is refused for ZDR and high-risk runs; (2) hold versus GDPR erasure precedence needs a Control decision; ZDR runs hold no content, so a typed refusal; do not claim signed releases before a candidate with rollback exists; (3) admin visibility must not leak admin-only or other-tenant policy; the disclosure table is a public claim and must be generated.
- Pull forward: the `space:egress` entitlement gap is a Control-only fix [verify live]; the egress catalogue and CI check (068) fits `WS12`'s guardrail lane.
- Must be designed before starting: the web-policy ADR (DEC-19); the retention schema in org-core and the sweeper's deletion contract with `WS06`/GDPR; the egress boundary mechanism (needs `WS22`).

### EP-10 Connectors: suggestion, write confirmation, custom
- Items: F-t3-056 (suggestion cards and editable write confirmation, L), F-t3-057 (custom connectors, L).
- Owner: Model Plane (execution-core `integration_tools.rs`, model-gateway MCP registry), Ingestion Plane (integration-corev2: custody, REST executor), Frontend Plane (cards, add flow).
- Prerequisites: `WS23` (S8 approved writes; `WS23` names F-t3-056 as building on S8 and the scope; S1 F8 redaction), `WS05` (declared classes), `WS13` (approvals v2), DEC-21 (custody), DEC-26 (connector reads), DEC-27 (accounts and write authority); 057 REST kind also needs EP-9 slice 3 (egress).
- First slice 056 (M of L): editable preview for mail drafts and calendar events with per-tool policy levels; an edited payload must produce a new frozen descriptor and fingerprint (today the card offers only approve or reject; the descriptor is frozen and fingerprint-bound in the Do loop, per the card). Then the suggestion tool and resumable connect intent. 057 first slice: probe-and-test over `http_list_tools` returning tools and auth requirement without saving, and a tool picker; static bearer servers cannot register today because `validate_mcp_server` rejects any raw token, so the secret-reference step is a separate, custody-bound piece.
- Top risks: (1) writes: default always-ask; approval continuations are refused under ZDR; (2) 057 registration is refused under ZDR and secrets must never reach logs or model context; (3) plane boundary erosion: the REST executor and vault tempt a Model Plane-local secret store (audit 10.1 #1).
- Pull forward: the 057 probe and picker (no secrets, no egress) can run after `WS05`.
- Must be designed before starting: the secret-reference contract with integration-corev2; the editable-descriptor model with `WS13`.

### EP-11 Authored behaviours and plugins
- Items: F-t3-058 (one versioned lifecycle for skills, slash templates, presets, XL), F-t3-059 (plugin manifest loader with supply-chain pins, L).
- Owner: Model Plane (session-core `agent_skills`, capability-core `plugin_packages`/`skill_packages`), Control Plane (admin consent, kill list), Frontend Plane.
- Prerequisites: `WS19` (skill bundle store S6, `skill_file` S7, first-party bundles S9), `WS07` (F-t3-007 proposal path and learning ledger; `WS07` names Teach mode and F-t3-058/059 as consumers), `WS25` (extension allow-lists, D2: hook webhook deferred to F-t3-059 until an egress allow-list exists), `WS05`, DEC-30 (three tiers, promotion gate), DEC-40 (plugin supply chain).
- First slice 058 (M of XL): immutable versions with digests pinned on runs and a linter on every upsert path. Today `UpsertAgentSkill` overwrites in place and the learning loop persists candidates enabled with no proposal step (audit citations); a versioned `skill_packages` schema exists unused. Then draft/publish, Space and org libraries (a schema change), import, authored presets, `/learn` and Save-as-skill through the proposal path.
- 059 first slice: org-curated catalogue only. A loader that expands a plugin into child capabilities (skills, remote HTTP MCP servers, slash commands first), every source pinned to a commit SHA or archive digest, a kill list, every component listed at install; stdio and binary components are refused and reported. Catalogue and loader stay in capability-core; install is admin-only.
- Top risks: (1) supply chain: bundle scripts run only inside the sandbox bound to the low-risk `cap.command.sandbox` (audit 10.1 #10); (2) the gateway skill cache pulls once per org per TTL; user-scoped skills need a service-wide view; (3) a skill edit silently changing every matching run until versions land.
- Pull forward: version pinning is the part of 058 that `WS07` should not leave open: if the learning loop is enabled for real orgs before the staging gate, pin versions as part of that gate.
- Must be designed before starting: version and digest semantics across the three tiers (DEC-30); plugin manifest schema, kill-list distribution and the Control consent record.

### EP-12 Sharing, export and the public Agent API
- Items: F-t3-046 (sharing, replay, export and import, XL), F-t3-064 (public, versioned Agent API with origin-tagged runs, L).
- Owner: Model Plane (session-core snapshots, model-gateway routes, OpenAPI), Control Plane (grants in the resource taxonomy, API-key to bearer exchange, audit), Channel Plane docs-only (not a build target, DEC-40).
- Prerequisites: `WS17` (F-t3-012 for 046), `WS04` (F-t3-016), `WS03` (F-t3-002), `WS12` (F-t3-067 contract guardrails for 064), `WS15` (D7: F-t3-064 owns the run envelope origin), `WS20` S2 (run-level origin stamp), DEC-17 (closed run-origin enum), DEC-40 (share model: org-only default, viewer-bound connector calls, ZDR threads refuse sharing, replay and export; MCP Apps on the existing frame before any new origin).
- First slice 064: origin and parent id on every run (the `WS20` S2 stamp widened to all surfaces), then API-key to audience-bound bearer exchange in Control (auth-core's API-key plugin has no consumer today), then the public lifecycle surface over the existing model-gateway run routes (`http_routes.rs:132-236` as cited) with the OpenAPI spec and generated SDKs (`api/generate-sdk.sh`). First slice 046: owner-only whole-conversation export and a redacted replay view; shared snapshot links need a new classified type in Control's resource taxonomy (`resource_taxonomy.json` lists `document` as the only ownable type; Go embed, Rust mirror and lint script kept in parity) and are the part that waits on the ADR.
- Top risks: (1) ZDR threads have no durable transcript; sharing, replay and export must refuse them; (2) authority: unattended API runs need always-ask or pre-approved plans; API-started browser runs still need broker grants; grants belong in Control's taxonomy, never a Model Plane-local ACL; (3) imported conversations may carry prompt injections; the importer is a new untrusted-input boundary.
- Pull forward: origin tagging (DEC-17, `WS20` S2) is W2 work; 046's export slice needs only `WS17` S1-S3.
- Must be designed before starting: the share model ADR (DEC-40; no builder in W1-W2); the API-key exchange contract with Control; which surfaces become origin values.

### EP-13 Model record, providers, Gemini and BYO
- Items: F-t3-042 (model record v2 and role table, L), F-t3-041 (Gemini provider family on Vertex EU, L), F-t3-065 (declarative provider registry and bring-your-own connections, XL).
- Owner: Model Plane (inference-core capability record, session-core RoutingPolicy, model-gateway constants), Control Plane (pricing join via cost-core, entitlement), Integration Core (BYO custody), Frontend Plane (picker).
- Prerequisites: `WS10` (F-t3-023 entitlements: Control decides, Model enforces at admission; 042 depends on it), `WS11` (the per-model effort support table seeds the effort ladder; the Gemini thinking-level wire is a fourth `Wire` variant), `WS27` (pricing primitives, cost ledger), DEC-13 (entitlement ownership, ADR-0005), DEC-21 (BYO keys and custody), DEC-33 (ZDR attestation lifecycle), DEC-40 (Vertex EU residency and ZDR bar; BYO metering; neither has a builder in W1-W2).
- First slice 042 (M of L): extend `ListModels` (`inference.proto:481-525` as cited) with context window, max output and effort ladder; join price from cost-core by id and never copy across planes; picker search. Then the role dimension on RoutingPolicy replacing the hard-coded side-task constants, with a purpose tag on every call (title, follow-ups, judge calls pin `gpt-4o-mini` constants today, per the card).
- 041: attestation first, adapter second. The seeded Gemini row was soft-deleted because no adapter serves it; Gemini turns would be unmetered without pricing (audit 10.1 #5), so pricing precedes enabling. Adapter scope: tool-schema sanitiser, thought-signature replay, Gemini prompt profile (today prompt branching is a claude-prefix check).
- 065 first slice: providers as data (read-only admin overlay on `from_env` configuration) before any BYO connection. BYO connections are held by Integration Core and referenced by opaque connection id; a customer-billed route needs cost-core attribution and the served-by line must name the connection.
- Top risks: (1) an admin form must never be able to assert ZDR or residency: attestation is evidence-bound; (2) Vertex EU may not meet the residency and ZDR bar: the open question 10.2 Q13; (3) plane ownership: inference-core owns capability, cost-core pricing, session-core policy; join by id.
- Pull forward: the purpose tag on every inference call can ride `WS27` S2 (which already counts overhead calls) [suggestion, design check needed]. 042 slice 1 is the cheapest unblocker for EP-14's 040 and EP-5's image tool.
- Must be designed before starting: the record schema split by owner plane; the BYO custody and metering design (DEC-40 parked); the Vertex attestation path (commercial).

### EP-14 Run legibility, trajectory ledger and release gates
- Items: F-t3-034 (trajectory ledger with request capture and timing waterfall, XL), F-t3-048 (readable long runs, M), F-t3-040 (eval and replay release gates, L).
- Owner: Model Plane (model-gateway request snapshots at the call site, eval-lab), Frontend Plane (ledger view, transcript density).
- Prerequisites: `WS15` (F-t3-049 typed lifecycle events; 034 depends on it), `WS09` (transcript performance), `WS12` (F-t3-066 UI quality gates and F-t3-067 guardrails), `WS01` (release safety, F-t3-039), EP-13 slice 1 (F-t3-042 for 040), the ZDR resolver.
- First slice 040: make eval-lab a required stage when prompts or tool schemas change. Today `.github/workflows/eval-lab.yml` runs only the credential-free selftest on eval-lab changes; 14 YAML cases exist and no comparison or promotion code exists despite the README claim (audit citations). Replay of recorded sessions in CI follows; the RoutingPolicy edit becomes a candidate, evaluate, promote flow. 034 first slice: request snapshots (exact system prompt, tool catalogue, parameters) written at the model call site, owner-only; the ledger view and waterfall after. 048 first slice (M): per-user transcript density levels and "Worked for X" folding of settled turns, then the docked "Step N of M" strip and tool narration; nothing in the SPA reads the persisted `compactMode` today.
- Top risks: (1) recorded sessions must be synthetic or scrubbed and never ZDR turns; request capture is a new content store (audit 10.1 #2); (2) which platform prompt text is shown to the user is a product decision; access owner-only, no ACL widening; (3) experiment ids and user-path budgets inside 040 are separate S/M items: do not let them stretch the epic.
- Pull forward: 048 and the 040 CI stage are FE/CI-only and can start as soon as `WS09` and `WS12` are green; 048 is independent of the other two. Any recap model call for narration must not run on ZDR turns.
- Must be designed before starting: the request-snapshot schema and retention; the eval case format and the gate thresholds.

#### 3.3.5 What must exist before any W3/W4 epic starts

- A named designer per epic and a file-level design in the `WSnn` format (scope, slices with effort, verified paths, ZDR clause, proof). This section is the input to that, not a replacement.
- The decisions named per epic closed or explicitly deferred: DEC-14, DEC-19, DEC-21, DEC-22 (blocking EP-1/9/10/11), DEC-28/29 (EP-3, EP-8), DEC-40 items (EP-4, EP-12, EP-13).
- The W1 trunk items (`WS03`, `WS05`, `WS17`, `WS22`) verified live, not only designed: the audit lists several "built" states it could not prove from code (audit 10.3): outbox fix deployment, signed release candidate, Redis in every deployment.

## 4. Sequencing and parallelism

Abbreviations: MP `apps/Model Plane`; CP `apps/Control Plane`; FE `apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3`; SC/MG/EC/IC = `MP/rust/services/{session-core,model-gateway,execution-core,inference-core}`; CC = `MP/go/services/capability-core`. DEC ids and "2.6 row n" = `plan-parts/02-decisions.md`. `[live-only]` = exists only in the `C:/dev/CoresSystem` working tree, not at HEAD afb83928. Integration branch = `fix/ui-premium-restore` (WS12 section 2.3). Audit = `C:/dev/verevon-audit-2026-10-14/03-model-plane-chat-and-agents-audit.md`. Numbers in 4.3 and 4.4 are proposals for the integrator to reserve (DEC-39), compiled from the designs.

### 4.1 Phase 0: land the live baseline (WS01 S0)

**Why first.** Every other design names WS01 S0 as a precondition for at least one slice (WS06 via WS01 S2). The HEAD taxonomy gate is red: `gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check` exits 1 at HEAD (rows `plan_proposed`, `source_scope_applied` missing) and 0 live.

**What the 324 entries are** (202 modified/deleted, 122 untracked): Model Plane 78; FE 106 (61 chat/core/dashboard/shared, 45 product-demo and scene); Ingestion/Quarry/integration-corev2 66; Data Plane v2 57; docs/compliance 6; CI/hooks 6; scripts 3; Control 2.

**Designs depend on (all [live-only]):**
- mp-contracts `retention.rs`, `zdr_refusal.rs`, `tests/zdr_retention_matrix.rs`; `api/contracts/zdr-refusals.json` (+`.sha256`); `scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh` (+95).
- SC: migrations `0044_run_retention_receipt.sql`, `0045_zdr_waived_runs_org_index.sql`, `migrations/revert/`; `retention_receipt.rs`; `grpc.rs` (+817 to +861 as WS04 and WS09 count it), `terminalization.rs`, `auth.rs`, `orchestration_grpc.rs`, `memory_grpc.rs`; `sessions.proto` (+42: `SessionRetentionService`, `CountWaivedRuns`).
- MG: `zdr_posture.rs`, `zdr_features.rs`, `zdr_precheck.rs`; `sse_events.rs` (+~200: `RetrievalNotice`, `ZdrError`); `sse.rs` (+883); `http_routes.rs` (+237); `tool_loop.rs`.
- EC (WS03, WS24): `agent.rs` (+207), `runtime_loop/mod.rs` (+18, `waived`), `grpc.rs` (+98), `agent_route.rs` (+285), `session_core_rules_double.rs` (+537). IC: `auth.rs`, `grpc.rs`.
- FE: `src/shared/api/zdr-refusals.ts`, `src/shared/api/contracts/`, `chat-client.ts` (+53), `ChatMessages.tsx` (+229), `use-chat-controller.ts`. CI: `model-plane-rust-ci.yml` (+50, job `session-core-receipts`).

**Not preconditions** (other sessions land them): Data Plane v2, Quarry/integration-corev2, FE product-demo/scene files and the `demo-product-build` job, the `DashboardComposer.tsx` hunk. MG `relevance*.rs`, `calibration/`, `quarry.rs`, `finetune_routes.rs`, `browser_run.rs` are not named by WS01 S0: classify per hunk before staging.

**How to land** (hunk-level staging in the owning session via `git diff`/`git apply --cached` patches, since interactive `git add -p` is unsupported here; never reset, clean or checkout the live tree: root CLAUDE.md). One commit per group, in compile order:

| Commit | Group | Gate before the commit |
|---|---|---|
| 0A | mp-contracts, `api/contracts`, `scripts/tests/zdr-contract-*` | `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh` (+`--self-test`); `node --test scripts/tests/zdr-contract-schema.test.mjs`; `cargo test -p mp-contracts` |
| 0B | session-core + migrations 0044/0045 + `revert/` + `sessions.proto` | `cargo test -q -p session-core --all-targets` (Windows host: `--lib`); ignored DB test `cargo test -p session-core --bin session-core zdr_receipt -- --ignored --test-threads=1` (>= 8 pass) |
| 0C | inference-core | `cargo test -q -p inference-core --all-targets` |
| 0D | model-gateway | `cargo test -q -p model-gateway --all-targets` |
| 0E | execution-core [RECONCILE: WS01 S0 omits EC; WS03 and WS24 list these EC files as live-only; added here] | `cargo test -q -p execution-core --all-targets` (incl. `cross_service_loop_contract`, `zdr_refusal_inventory`) |
| 0F | FE ZDR files | `pnpm typecheck && pnpm vitest run src/shared/api/zdr-refusals.test.ts` |
| 0G | docs (`ZDR.md`, `sse-event-taxonomy.md`, `core-research/model-gateway.md`, `BUILD_PASS_*`), `docs/compliance/evidence/*` | `python3 "apps/Model Plane/scripts/gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py" --check` at PR tip (0D and 0G ship in one PR) |
| 0H | `model-plane-rust-ci.yml` (`.gitleaks.toml` only with singular `[allowlist]`: CI pins gitleaks 8.24.3) | CI run |

At the PR tip also run `buf lint` / `buf breaking` on the +42 `sessions.proto` lines (outcome not determined; the baseline is 0 bytes until WS12 S2). Effort M (2-4 d).

**Rule.** Every stream branches from the tagged landed commit (`mp-baseline-0`, proposed name), never from the live tree. DoD: `git status --porcelain -- "apps/Model Plane" scripts .github` lists only entries named in the out-of-scope list (Data Plane, Ingestion/Quarry/integration-corev2, Control, `.githooks`, `.gitleaks.toml`, FE product-demo/home/Space/work-period files, and the six Model Plane paths owned by other sessions: `relevance*.rs`, `calibration/`, `quarry.rs`, `finetune_routes.rs`, `browser_run.rs`, which are classified per hunk and staged as a ninth commit 0I only if ZDR-related); HEAD contains 0044, 0045, `revert/` and `SessionRetentionService`; taxonomy check exits 0. WS12 S1 may start in parallel; its buf baseline waits for 0B.

**Cycle breakers.** WS03.S5 / WS05.S1: WS03 ships a stub class table in `effect.rs`, WS05 replaces it. WS02.S6 / WS07.S1: S6 stamps `SKIPPED_BY_POLICY(memory_off)` once WS07 returns a typed skip reason, else `UNAVAILABLE`. WS09.S8 / WS17.S1: first to start lands, the other rebases. **[RECONCILE]** order of the `waived: bool` signature versus the class seam in EC: WS05 and WS03 put WS03 first; WS13/WS23/WS25 put WS05 S1/S3 first. This plan: WS03.S6 (LoopState refactor plus `waived`) merges first as one early PR, then WS05.S3/S4; WS05.S1/S2 touch Go and gateway files only, so they go earlier.

**Critical paths** (engineer-days from the designs).
1. Spine: WS01.S0 (2-4) then WS02.S4-S7 (17-25 d, overlaps) then WS03 S1-S8 (about 63 d; two engineers, stage 1 in about 3 weeks, all in 8-9) then WS13.S6 (8-12), WS14.S5, WS16.S3-S6, WS20.S5-S8, WS21.S5.
2. Tool governance: WS05 S0-S1-S3-S4-S6 (about 25 d with S2/S5 on a second engineer) then WS13.S7, WS23.S3, WS24.S6, WS25.S9.
3. Ops: WS01 S0-S6-S7-S12-S13 (20-25 d to a signed candidate; S13 elapsed time set by the external ZDR contract).
4. Audit: WS01.S1-S2 (about 5 d) then WS06 S1-S6 (24-32 d). 5. Unattended: WS20 S4-S8 (28-40 d), S6 behind WS03.S6 and DEC-16.

### 4.3 Proto field reservation table

Rules: add-only; never renumber a wire field (`execution.proto:139-142`); none of these messages has a `reserved` range. Record this table as one append-only entry in `MP/docs/decisions/ledger.md` at T0 and put the owner tag in a doc comment at each claimed field. "Final" = integrator-assigned number (planned merge order, frozen when the PR enters its train). `buf breaking` is not a real gate until WS12.S2 (0-byte baseline), so this table is the only collision control until then.

### 4.4 Migration number plan

Provisional numbers, not monotonic with train order (`ignore_missing` from WS01.S9 makes that safe); the integrator may renumber before a PR enters its train and freezes the number then. Never take session-core 0044/0045. Every session-core migration ships `revert/NNNN_*.down.sql` (WS01.S9 policy, the live 0044/0045 convention) and is additive. Most Go services (not audit-core, which has a versioned slice applied once) re-run every `*.up.sql` at every boot with no ledger: write idempotent SQL. No third duplicate prefix (capability-core 0013, user-core 008 and 021 already are).

| Service (next free at HEAD / live) | Planned numbers (WS slice, purpose) |
|---|---|
| **session-core** (0044 / **0046**) | 0046 WS03.S2 `approval_consume_and_mode`; 0047 WS17.S1 `thread_activity_cursor`; 0048 WS03.S4 `run_loop_checkpoints`; 0049 WS03.S5 `tool_action_effect_ledger`; 0050 WS04.S4 `thread_autonomy_grant`; 0051 WS07.S1 `user_memory_state`; 0052 WS04.S6 `message_client_id`; 0053 WS16.S1 `attention_state`; 0054 WS14.S3 `run_questions`; 0055 WS17.S3 `message_search_index` (first line `-- no-transaction`, `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`); 0056 WS20.S1 `cron_fire_run_link`; 0057 WS07.S4 `org_learning_policy_mirror` (renamed: it is not the capability-core `org_agent_policy` of WS25.S7); 0058 WS07.S7 `agent_skills_proposals`; 0059 WS17.S6 `thread_fork_lineage`; 0060 WS17.S7 `thread_star_unread`; 0061 WS17.S8 `message_versions`; 0062 WS13.S6 `approval_scope_grants` (the WS13.S7 rule link folds in if planned with S6, else next free); 0063 WS14.S5 `run_question_consume`; 0064 WS07.S8 `skill_revisions` (trigger); 0065 WS20.S4 `cron_schedule_contract`; 0066 WS25.S12 `drop_hook_configs` (only after one release with `HOOKS_DURABLE=on`); WS07.S6 `thread_memory_mode` (optional) takes the next free number if scheduled |

### 4.6 Parallel lanes and merge trains

**Lanes** (one worktree per branch under `C:/dev/CoresSystem/.claude/worktrees/`; branches `feat/mp-wsNN-<slice-slug>`, `feat/cp-wsNN-...`, `feat/fe-wsNN-...`, `feat/ci-wsNN-...`; each starts at `mp-baseline-0` and rebases on the integration branch before its train).

| Lane | Workstreams and slices | Conflicts with | Serial constraint |
|---|---|---|---|

### 4.7 Team and time shape

Assumptions: net 4.0 engineer-days per engineer-week (review, Rust image rebuilds over 10 minutes, deploy evidence); 15 percent coordination overhead; effort = the designs' day ranges (low = committed slices, high = plus optional ones; "not scheduled" slices excluded); owner rulings, the external ZDR contract (WS01.S13) and the QRY and Space sessions are not counted. W1 about 400-520 engineer-days (WS01 45, WS02 34-50, WS03 63, WS04 30-41, WS05 31-44, WS06 38-52, WS07 36-47, WS08 34-49, WS09 15-23, WS10 31-44, WS11 17-24, WS12 27-35). W2 about 620-850 (WS13 37-54 through WS27 43-62; sum of the per-design totals).

Elapsed = max(spine, capacity). Spine: W1 about 10-11 weeks (WS01.S0 3 d, WS02.S4 3-5 d, WS03 8-9 weeks with two engineers); W2 spine ends near weeks 18-22 (WS20.S4-S8, 28-40 d, behind WS03.S6).

| Engineers | Lane mapping | W1 elapsed | W2 elapsed | Whole program (W2 overlaps W1) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 3 | pair on lane C; one engineer rotates A, D, E1, G (order WS01, WS12, WS04, WS09) | 38-50 weeks | 60-81 weeks | about 100-130 weeks; W1 only, then W2 by value |
| 5 | C pair; D one; A and B one; E and G one | 23-30 weeks | 36-49 weeks | about 60-80 weeks |
| 8 | C pair; D one; A and B one; E one; F and G one; one floater | 14-19 weeks (capacity-bound; spine 10-11) | 22-30 weeks | about 37-49 weeks |

Headcount-independent milestones: CI and buf gate real in week 1; audit delivery proven (WS01.S1-S2) in weeks 1-2; run-context contract in week 2; Stage 1 `/continue` (WS03.S3) near weeks 5-6; signed candidate and fail-closed ZDR proof (WS01.S12) near weeks 5-7. Capacity figures treat every design as in scope; an owner scope cut shortens W2 more than extra engineers do.

## 5. Cross-session interfaces

Two siblings share Model Plane code with this program: (a) Verevon Space (`01-verevon-space-audit.md`, F-t1) and (b) Quarry v2 and browser (`02-quarry-and-browser-audit.md`, F-t2). Ownership follows CLAUDE.md: Model owns sessions/runs, capabilities, sandboxes, browser grants and cost; Application owns collaborative projections and notifications; Control owns identity, audit, quotas, entitlements; Data owns documents, wiki and memory documents; Ingestion captures and Quarry executes; Frontend owns UI and the BFF. "Owner" below means the plan that carries the work item once; the sibling lists it as a dependency. Facts were verified in the LIVE tree by the overlap authors; the worktree lacks `zdr_refusal.rs`, `retention.rs`, `zdr_features.rs`, `zdr_posture.rs`, migrations 0044/0045 and `sessions.proto` +42 [live-only].

Interim `ExtractStructured` RPC (`model-gateway/src/grpc.rs:2516-2655`) | Before F-t2-019/037/050/030-ph2/062 | YES: owner and date unassigned; WS10 D6 exempts service principals |
| ZDR propagation | 003, 004, 016/018 / 005 (WS01) | Model: attestation + typed catalogue. Browser stays refused under ZDR (rule R17; `browser_run.rs:307`) | Quarry adopts `zdr_feature_not_supported` + feature from the catalogue (copy under a conformance test, no shared crate); F-t2-003 ph1 (gateway cache hole) independent, do first | none | NO |
| Secret redaction / scrub | 021 / 033, 010 (WS26) | One pattern set owned by Model `scrub.rs`; Quarry capture-time masking copy + conformance test | Defence in depth at three sites | before default-on planner | NO |

### 5.3 Contradictions between the documents and recommended resolution

| ID | Contradiction | Recommended resolution |
|---|---|---|
| S-C1 | Resume: Space "widen resumable kinds in the dispatcher" vs Model "re-enter the loop at a checkpoint" (OD6 open) | Both hold at different levels: approvals inside a running loop = same run, new segment; cold worker only for ExecuteStep approvals and owner actions (DEC-03). Space must not add kinds to the cold worker |
| S-C2 | Input request: end-turn + new run (Space) vs pause-in-place (Model) | One `run_questions` table, two delivery values (DEC-05); ZDR: tool not offered |
| S-C3 | Message versions: append-only revision entries vs version tree | Tree for personal threads only; Space keeps revision entries; WS17 lands first (DEC-29) |
| S-C4 | Space as project: M546 puts Space threads in /chat; migration `0034_thread_origin.sql:44-47` forbids it | Opt-in Projects section through the Space route; no migration change (DEC-28) |
| S-C5 | Memory key: scope kind/id vs audience hash/revision vs third capability-core enum | One migration, both columns (DEC-06/30, 2.6 row 13) |
| S-C6 | Action schema source: Frontend catalog hash vs "Model must not consume the Frontend registry" | Frontend owns classification; capability-core owns admission rows; Model consumes only the latter (DEC-25) |
| S-C7 / Q-C5 | Credential custody: IC broker vs Model proxy + Control secret store vs Quarry vault (F-t3-043 vs F-t3-057/ADR8) | One ADR-0008: provider tokens and BYO in integration-corev2; site passwords via Quarry vault substitution; Model never sees them (DEC-21) |
| S-C8 | Retention store: `space_effect_policies` vs org-core `DataRetentionDays`; hold vs erasure open | Policy value signed by Control, one sweeper in session-core; OWNER to rule precedence (7a) |
| S-C9 / Q-C7 | Origin vocabulary: `turn_origin`, run origin, Quarry enum, six-value thread CHECK | One closed `runs.metadata.origin {chat, schedule, trigger, api}`; do not widen the CHECK; qualify other uses (DEC-17) |
| S-C10 | Steering: owner-bound, model classifies, no editable queue vs member steer and editable chips | Owner-bound now; client-only "Stop and send"; editable queue behind WS17 `withdrawn` marker; member steer via Control decision later (DEC-07) |
| S-C11 | Instruction order: S1450 "platform, org, Space, user, agent" vs M344 "org > Space > persona > user" vs ADR-0003; WS02 D3 text looks like a copy slip | ADR-0003 stands, no fifth layer; one function `compose_authored_instructions`; user-preference position settled at the OD19 sign-off (DEC-28) |
| S-C12 | Naming: "quarantine" (continuation placeholder vs tainted-output approval), "project", "origin" | Rename in wire/docs; bare "origin" = thread origin only (2.6 row 10) |
| Q-C1 | Who mints grants: T3 "gateway mints" vs T2 "gateway must never mint" | Model-gateway endpoint mints under the user's delegated bearer; fix T3 wording (DEC-20) |
| Q-C2 | Grant store: Postgres (T2) vs Redis (Model docs) | QRY decides; Dragonfly is cache-mode (256 MB), so authority state must not assume Redis durability (WS04 note); WS21 proceeds in-memory |
| Q-C3 / Q-C4 | Policy delivery (signed claims vs service endpoint + revision) and per-site persistence (Control settings vs capability-core rules vs per-user table vs session scope) | Layered, ADR-0007: org-core record, broker mint, Quarry final; pulled by revision, never a bearer claim (DEC-19, 2.6 row 7) |
| Q-C6 | `@` trigger: `@tab` vs `@connector` vs "use non-`@`" | Chips first; `@` only after SPACE agrees (DEC-26) |
| Q-C8 | Reviewer-model screening: T2 layer 2 before fetch vs no LLM on the permission path | Tighten-only, shadow, never grants, after an ADR (DEC-40); WS26 semantic pass is tighten-only |
| Q-C9 | Takeover authority: T3 says Quarry "already covers" it | Code confirms T2: Quarry does not enforce yet; ph1 first |
| Q-C10 | Quarry cannot authenticate to the broker in prod | Registry + manifest + dev-bypass removal in W1 (table b row 2) |
| Q-C11 | Wave mismatch: F-t2-001 W1 vs F-t3-008 W2; F-t2-013-ph2 W2 vs F-t3-026 W3; F-t2-022 W2 vs F-t3-055 W3; F-t2-014 W2 vs F-t3-050 W4 | Ordering of Q-D1/D2 governs; pull grant v2 identity into W1 |
| X-1 | Waived-run rule inconsistent across WS02/03/14/17 | One rule, DEC-32 |
| X-2 | Proto tags and migration numbers claimed more than once | Integrator reserves serially; session-core next free 0046; never 0044/0045 (DEC-39, 2.6 rows 1-2) |

## 6. Verification and regression plan

### 6.1 Verification pyramid (what each wave must show)

| Layer | Wave 1 | Wave 2 |
|---|---|---|
| Unit | Rust `cargo test -q -p <crate> --all-targets` (per crate `--lib` on Windows); Go `go test ./...` per module; vitest per file. New pure modules first: `LoopFoldV1` golden, `UsageMeter`, effort ladder table, `RunControl` loopback | Authority/approval tables (`Authority::narrows`, scope eligibility), `HookEvaluator` strictest-wins, closer text table, cause-code mapping |
| Integration | Real-Postgres `#[ignore]` tests with `DATABASE_URL` (receipts, consume CAS, `run_loop_checkpoints`, audit chain); NATS-backed tests with `NATS_URL`; two-replica test harness over one `LoopbackHub`; promtool rule tests | Cross-service contract tests (`cross_service_loop_contract.rs`, `durable_loop_contract.rs`), BFF `cargo test --manifest-path apps/gateway/Cargo.toml --all-targets`, Convex `npm run test` for Application slices |
| E2E (browser) | Playwright `chat` project on a second dev server (`pnpm dev --port 5174 --strictPort`, `LOCAL_E2E_BASE_URL=http://localhost:5174`); perf spec `chat-transcript-performance.spec.ts` | Plan-approve-execute, ask_user tray, Waiting section, project picker, agent-browser consent card, Office previewer, locale-toggled screenshots |
| Chaos / crash | WS03: `docker restart execution-core session-core` between pause and approve; `docker kill execution-core` between reserve and finalize; WS04: `kill -9` owner, NATS stopped, Redis outage fake; WS01: publisher requeue replay; rollback rehearsal on disposable Postgres | WS03 S8: kill past the 15 min deadline => `parked(crash_interrupted)`; WS21: broker restart mid-run => `browser_session_lost`; WS20: consent expiry mid-schedule |
| Live proofs (rebuilt images, running stack) | Section 6.7 flows F1-F8; dated evidence files under `docs/compliance/evidence/` | Flows F9-F12 |
| Static gates | `buf lint`, `buf breaking`, SSE taxonomy `--check`, ZDR contract sync, release-contract tests, WS12 gates | + proto add-only checks, matrix rows, gitleaks |

### 6.2 Evidence rule (quoted, with source)

Source file: `apps/Model Plane/docs/MODEL_PLANE_RELEASE_EVIDENCE.md`, `apps/Model Plane/MODEL_PLANE_STATUS.md`, `apps/Model Plane/CLAUDE.md`, root `SYSTEM_STATUS.md` (via `common/commands-and-gates.md` section 7).

- "A claim is not release-ready merely because source code or a unit test exists." (`MODEL_PLANE_RELEASE_EVIDENCE.md:6-7`). Evidence classes `source`, `test`, `integration`, `staging`, `candidate`, `rollback`, `production` (`:9-21`); "Only `candidate` and `rollback` evidence can support a production promotion."
- "Source-verified" = source plus repeatable local tests pass, "this alone is not deployment evidence"; "Live-verified" is dated and not immutable production evidence (`MODEL_PLANE_STATUS.md:90-96`). "The running stack is evidence for this dirty source aggregate only, not a production-release authorization." (`:7-8`).
- "Never claim a feature is 'done' without source + test evidence" (`apps/Model Plane/CLAUDE.md:57`); read `MODEL_PLANE_STATUS.md` before trusting any done claim (`:33`). Root `SYSTEM_STATUS.md:48-49` separates "verified via build/test; most NOT yet deployed" from "fixed + live-verified (this one deployed)".
- Operating form for this plan: every slice DoD names (1) the images rebuilt (`bash "apps/Model Plane/scripts/compose.sh" up -d --no-deps --build <service>` or WS01 `rebuild-service.sh`; Control via `scripts/run-control-plane.sh`, never bare `docker compose build`), (2) the running stack, (3) the named flow with captured artefacts (SSE transcript, psql output, curl body, screenshot). Status files state "source-verified" or "live-verified <date>", never "deployed" without an `docker inspect` image revision. Capability rows stay `source_only` until WS01 S12.

### 6.3 Gates per merge train

Train membership is defined ONLY in 4.6 (T0..T8); this table lists extra gates and must be read against those trains. Where a slice appears in a different train below, 4.6 wins. T0 = WS01.S0 groups 0A-0H plus WS12.S1; WS12.S2 (buf baseline) gates T1, so PR-2 merges between T0 and T2. A train merges only when every gate listed for it, and every earlier train, is green on CI or recorded as a red-then-green manual run.

| Train | Content | Gates that must be green |
|---|---|---|
| T0 Land the live wave | WS01 S0 (hunk-staged groups 0A-0H), WS12 S1 (CI runs on the integration branch); WS12 S2 (buf repaired) gates T1 | `zdr-contract-sync.test.sh` (+ `--self-test`), `zdr-contract-schema.test.mjs`, `cargo test --workspace --no-fail-fast`, `buf lint` + `buf breaking ... --against baseline.binpb`, `gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check`, `git status --porcelain -- "apps/Model Plane"` empty |
| T1 W1 foundations | WS01 S1-S3, S6, S8; WS12 S3-S12; WS02 S1-S5; WS04 S1-S5; WS05 S0-S1; WS06 S0-S1; WS09 S1-S3, S5; WS10 S0-S2; WS11 S1-S4 | T0 gates + `model-plane-rust-ci.yml` (fmt, check, check --all-targets, test) + `session-core-receipts` job [live-only, >= 8 pass] + promtool + `release-contract-test.sh`, `release-runtime-partition-test.sh`, `release-input-security-test.sh` + `verevonv3-ci.yml` (`fabrication-guard`, `gateway-rust` clippy `-D warnings`, `spa-verify` = `pnpm verify`, `docker-image`) + `sse-taxonomy.yml` + `eval-lab.yml` |
| T2 W1 behaviour | WS02 S6-S10; WS03 S1-S9; WS04 S6-S9; WS05 S2-S6; WS06 S2-S8; WS07; WS08; WS10 S3-S7; WS11 S5-S8; WS09 S4, S6-S10 (conditional) | T1 gates + `loop_fold_zdr_matrix.rs`, `durable_loop_contract.rs`, `cross_service_loop_contract.rs`, `autonomy_ladder_contract.rs`, `compaction_parity_contract.rs`, `scrub_checkpoint_test.rs`, DB-gated session-core/audit-core tests, `model-plane-ops-gates.yml` (WS01 S11), migration expand/contract lint (WS01 S9), `manifest_contract_test.go` |
| T3 W1 exit | WS01 S12-S14, WS03 S9, WS04 S9 proof scripts, WS12 S12 bundle budget | W1 exit gate: flows F1-F8 captured; `coresystem-conformance.sh --json --profile runtime` names only real gaps; `MODEL_PLANE_STATUS.md` rows updated |
| T4 W2 | WS13-WS27 in dependency order (WS13 -> WS14 -> WS15/16; WS17/18/19/20; WS21-WS24 after grant identity; WS25-27) | T2 gates + Control Go modules (`org-service.yml`, `user-service.yml`, `auth-service.yml` incl. `principal-registry-parity.test.sh`), `notification-core-service.yml`, `convex-core` `npm run test` + `typecheck` + `lint` [no CI; run locally], `quarry-v2-ci.yml` when Quarry contracts change, `secret-scan.yml` (gitleaks) |
| T5 W2 exit | WS21-24 proofs, WS25 S12, WS27 S8-S10 | Flows F9-F12; every new store has its ZDR matrix row (6.6) |

### 6.4 Exact commands per plane

(From Makefiles, package.json, CLAUDE.md and workflows; windows host: fall back to `--lib` per crate when two pre-existing targets fail; `go test -race` unavailable, no cgo; no `go.work` entry for `.`, build per service.)

- Model Plane Rust (`apps/Model Plane/rust`): `cargo fmt --all -- --check`; `cargo check --workspace --all-targets`; `cargo test -q -p <model-gateway|session-core|inference-core|execution-core|mp-contracts> --all-targets`; `cargo test --workspace --no-fail-fast`; ignored DB: `cargo test -p session-core --bin session-core <filter> -- --ignored --test-threads=1` with `DATABASE_URL`.
- Model Plane Go (`apps/Model Plane/go/services/<svc>`): `go test ./...` and `go vet ./...`; new deps via `GOWORK=off go get`; bindings `buf generate` (not installed on host: use buf image). No CI for these modules today; WS01 S11 adds `go-audit`.
- Proto and contracts: `buf lint "apps/Model Plane/proto"`; `buf breaking "apps/Model Plane/proto" --against "apps/Model Plane/proto/baseline.binpb"`; `python3 "apps/Model Plane/scripts/gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py" --check`; `bash scripts/tests/zdr-contract-sync.test.sh`; `node --test scripts/tests/zdr-contract-schema.test.mjs`.
- Model Plane deploy: `./scripts/compose.sh up -d` (dev); `bash "apps/Model Plane/scripts/compose.sh" up -d --no-deps --build <service>`; replicas `MODEL_PLANE_REPLICAS=1` (WS04 S1); release `scripts/release-artifact.sh {dry-run|build|verify|compose|deploy|validate-deployability}`.
- Control Plane: auth-core `pnpm test`, `pnpm run test:cov`, `pnpm run lint:contract` (15 pre-existing failures in 3 suites: diff failing sets vs HEAD); user-core `make test`; org-core and billing-core `go test -v -p 1 ./...`; audit-core `TEST_DATABASE_URL=... go test ./... && go vet ./...`; deploy `bash "apps/Control Plane/scripts/run-control-plane.sh" up -d --no-deps --build <service>`.
- Application Plane: `go test -v ./...` in notification-core and conversation-core-go; convex-core `npm run test`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`; Convex re-push `docker restart convex-gateway`.
- Frontend (`apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3`): `pnpm lint`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm test`, `pnpm build`, `pnpm verify`; targeted `pnpm exec vitest run <files>`; `pnpm exec playwright test --project=chat <spec>`; gateway `cargo test --manifest-path apps/gateway/Cargo.toml --all-targets`. Quarry SDK must be generated first (`Quarry-v2/sdks/generate.sh`, `pnpm run build` in `sdks/typescript`). `:5173` is the main checkout: use the second dev server and restart for bind-mount edits; Vite caches `global.css` across restart.
- Ingestion: `cd "apps/Ingestion Plane" && make test-endpoints`; `cd Quarry-v2 && cargo test --workspace` (run in a `rust:1` container on Windows); integration-corev2 `go test ./...` (no CI).

### 6.6 ZDR golden-matrix cases to add per new store

Rule (DEC-32): a waived run counts as ZDR for every NEW durable store; recall and fork refuse waived content. Each new store/route adds a case to `api/contracts/zdr-posture-matrix.json` (+ `.sha256`), the Rust matrix `zdr_posture_matrix.rs` [live-only] and a named test. Row names marked (proposed) are for the integrator to align.

| Store / route | WS | Case |
|---|---|---|
| `run_loop_checkpoints`, `ConsumeApproval`, `/continue`, park | WS03 | `loop_fold_zdr_matrix.rs` iterates `enforcement_rows`: fold/consume/park refused unless `persistent`; features `agent_run`, `approval_continuation` |
| Run-control bus, queue, plan grant, client message id | WS04 | `e_zdr_token_queue_input_refused`, `e_zdr_token_plan_grant_refused` (proposed); `zdr_turn_registers_no_run_control_listener`; id omitted for ZDR |
| Shared turn record, shared idempotency claim | WS04 | ZDR turns never register or claim; ids only in Redis |
| Message FTS index, `SearchMessages`, `recall_conversations`, `ForkThread`, message tree | WS17 | owner search includes waived threads; recall and fork refuse waived; ZDR threads absent from index |
| `user_memory_controls`, reset, learned-skill proposals | WS07 | ZDR/waived skip memory and learning review |
| Attachments contract, reference uploads | WS08 | ZDR refuses attachments (no attested provider yet); subscription route typed refusal |
| Audit chain, hash rows | WS06 | rows content-free; replay read shows 0 rows with `details.zdr = true`; ZDR run writes no audit row |
| `run_questions` | WS14 | tool never offered; routes refuse; (feature key proposed) |
| Approval grants (THREAD/RUN), cause, step-up | WS13 | no grant persisted from ZDR turns |
| Attention state, push, per-user stream | WS16 | no push for ZDR threads; temporary chat makes no server call |
| Transcript snapshot throttle | WS09 | `snapshot-throttle` ZDR cases; temporary writes nothing; `use-chat-controller.zdr-refusal.test.ts` stays green |
| Thread workspace tables and blobs | WS22 | ZDR refuse; snapshot destruction shown |
| Skill bundle store | WS19 | confirm at S6 that no conversation content is stored; otherwise add case |
| Scheduled run result and consent contract | WS20 | ZDR refuses unattended runs; waiver clause "never scheduled or unattended" restated |
| Browser grants / Quarry runs | WS21 | browser stays refused under ZDR (`browser_run.rs:307`) with catalogue code |
| Connector read tool | WS23 | refused under ZDR and waived |
| Workspace file tools, action admission | WS24 | refused under ZDR |
| Hook rules, loop guard, run-scoped handles | WS25 | handles run-scoped in memory, never persisted for ZDR |
| Memory/skill write policy, semantic screen | WS26 | model-origin writes limited to `user`/`thread` topics; classifier call counts only |
| Cost meter | WS27 | ZDR states ephemeral cost in-stream, numbers only; ledger query for the run is empty (`zdr-live-proof.sh`) |
| Entitlements | WS10 | refusal path distinct from `zdr_unavailable` |

### 6.7 End-to-end acceptance flows (Waves 1 and 2)

Each flow runs on rebuilt images, flags on, on the proof stack; the evidence column lists what is captured into the dated evidence bundle.

| Flow | Steps | Evidence captured |
|---|---|---|
| F1 Do run carries my instructions (WS02) | Set org instruction "answer in numbered steps" and a persona; Do turn on a thread with prior turns; same with flag `off` | gateway log counts, `runs.metadata.run_context`, answer, SPA screenshot; flag-off byte-identical diff; Usage frame real tokens; `/api/v1/cost/run` non-zero |
| F2 Approve after crash (WS03/WS13) | Do chat, ask posture, gated shell-class call pauses; `docker restart execution-core session-core`; approve; `/continue`; repeat `/continue`; separately `docker kill execution-core` between reserve and finalize | both SSE transcripts, `approvals.consumed_at`, outbox count 0, one terminal receipt, 409 body, ledger `unknown_outcome` row, provider receipt count unchanged |
| F3 Stop and approve from the other replica (WS04) | `MODEL_PLANE_REPLICAS=1`; stream on :8080, Stop and queue via :8081; approve plan on A, Do-run on B; `kill -9` owner; stop NATS | `stopped` frame, `queued_input` delivery, rung in log, SPA `cancelRun`, 503 body, `run_control_requests_total`, `/readyz` |
| F4 Audit delivered (WS01/WS06) | Deploy publisher fix + 0043 from a clean detached worktree; replay `requeue_session_audit_terminal`; run one governed tool turn; later mutate one row via GUC | before/after terminal, pending and durable counts, `audit_events` rows, `GET /api/v1/audit?event=tool_action`, verifier `broken`, firing alert |
| F5 ZDR live (WS01) | Pass 1 fail-closed on ZDR-TEST org; deploy in order session-core, execution-core, model-gateway, inference-core; pass 2 after Z-40 attestation | typed refusal, zero row delta, refused 409 PATCH, streamed answer, `mp:gw:cache:*` unchanged, evidence file, Playwright report |
| F6 MCP read tool in Do (WS05) | Connect fixture MCP server; Do chat calls `echo_read`; `create_item` under `ask`; ZDR run; subscription-route plan-mode | SSE step events, v2 JWS claims, `capability_tools` row, audit rows, refused forced call |
| F7 Long transcript stays smooth (WS09) | Synthetic 300-turn thread, stream a 20-paragraph answer at 40 frames/s; cold open; chunk-load recovery under an open tab | three JSON reports vs `chat-perf-budget.json`, request accounting, Chromium version, production-image run |

### 7b. The first three pull requests

Branches open on day 1, rebase onto `mp-baseline-0`, and merge in order PR-1, PR-2, PR-3. Why these: PR-1 is the precondition of every design; PR-2 makes every later gate real (0-byte buf baseline, red taxonomy gate at HEAD, CI not firing on the integration branch); PR-3 is a small mechanical change whose value is reserving tag 18 early (tag 18; about 17 struct-literal sites, only 2 list every field) and needs no decision, so merging it early shrinks every later rebase.

### PR-1: `chore(model-plane): land ZDR and session-core live baseline`
- **Branch:** `feat/mp-ws01-s0-land-live-baseline` (tag `mp-baseline-0` at merge). Slice WS01.S0, effort M (2-4 d).
- **Files:** the 4.1 groups 0A-0H, eight atomic commits in one PR, staged by hunk in the owning session (non-interactively: `git diff -- <file> > x.patch`, trim hunks by hand, `git apply --cached x.patch`; interactive `git add -p` is not available here). Excluded: Data Plane, Quarry, integration-corev2, FE product-demo and scene files, the `DashboardComposer.tsx` hunk.
- **Tests:** the per-commit gates of 4.1; at the PR tip `cargo test --workspace --no-fail-fast` (Windows host: per-crate `--lib`, two cargo targets fail pre-existing), taxonomy `--check`, `zdr-contract-sync.test.sh --self-test`.
- **Evidence:** commit list, gate output lines, the 4.1 DoD checks (`git status --porcelain`, migrations listing, taxonomy exit 0).
- **Risks:** hunk entanglement with other sessions in `sse.rs`, `grpc.rs`, `ChatMessages.tsx`.

### PR-2: `ci: run gates on the integration branch and repair the buf gate`
- **Branch:** `feat/ci-ws12-s1-s2-reachability-and-buf`. Slices WS12.S1 (S, 1-2 d) and S2 (M, 2-3 d); decisions D1(a), D7. Open after PR-1 lands 0A-0B (the baseline must be built from committed state, never from the dirty tree).
- **Files (S1):** `.github/workflows/verevonv3-ci.yml` (`:11-12` `pull_request` filter and `push:` block only; the live `demo-product-build` job belongs to the demo session: rebase over it, never edit inside jobs), `buf.yml:3-12`, `verevonv3-security.yml`, `quarry-v2-ci.yml` (filters only); NEW `FE/docs/CI_BASELINE_2026-10.md`; NEW `.github/workflows/repo-gates.yml` (trigger shell).
- **Files (S2):** `buf.yml:21,23` (quote the path with spaces); `MP/proto/baseline.binpb` (rebuild with `docker run --rm -v "$PWD:/w" -w /w bufbuild/buf build ...`; buf is not on the host); NEW `scripts/gates/buf-lint-ratchet.py`; NEW `MP/proto/buf-lint-baseline.json` (about 1,502 entries per ledger `:2479`, to be re-counted); `MP/go/pkg/cibuf/workflow_test.go:69-79`; `MP/proto/README.md`; NEW `scripts/tests/test_buf_lint_ratchet.py`.
- **Tests:** `cd "apps/Model Plane/go/pkg/cibuf" && go test ./...`; `python3 -m unittest scripts/tests/test_buf_lint_ratchet.py`; CI self-proof step (delete a field in a proto copy, assert `buf breaking` fails, as `sse-taxonomy.yml:49-62` does); throwaway branch plus `gh run list`; a deliberate `pnpm typecheck` error turns `spa-verify` red.
- **Evidence:** `gh run list` URLs for verevonv3-ci, model-plane-rust-ci, contract-goldens, buf and sse-taxonomy; one red and one green buf run; the baseline-file diff; the CI baseline table with an owner per pre-existing red; request to the repo owner to change the default branch (D1(c), an owner action).
- **Risks:** first runs are red for pre-existing reasons (12 vitest files, `@quarry/client` typecheck: project memory, not re-verified); each gate runs `continue-on-error: true` for 3 runs, then blocks.

### PR-3: `feat(model-plane): run-context contract on RunAgentRequest, no behaviour change`
- **Branch:** `feat/mp-ws02-s4-run-context-contract`. Slice WS02.S4, effort M (3-5 d); decision-free (inert until WS02.S6). Open after PR-1 (live `sessions.proto`, `grpc.rs`); its DoD "buf breaking green" needs PR-2.

## Appendix B. Review amendments (binding)

An adversarial review (three Sonnet 5.5 high skeptics, 81 findings: 12 HIGH, 38 MEDIUM, 31 LOW; source files `.plan-work/review/K1.md`, `K2.md`, `K3.md`) checked the sections above against the code and the architecture rules. About 45 spot-checked code claims held; no audit item was dropped. Findings that were mechanical were fixed in place (RunAgentRequest literal count, `buf breaking` vacuity wording, DEC-31 default, train definition, 4.1 DoD, migration wording, org policy table name, engineer mapping, ADR file name, interactive `git add -p`). Everything else is recorded here as an **amendment: where the section text conflicts with a row below, the row wins** and the owning workstream applies it when its slice is written up for implementation. IDs: Kn-m = review file n, finding m.

### B.1 Standing notes (apply to every workstream section)

- **N1. Evidence carve-out.** The rule "every definition of done needs rebuilt images, a running stack and a verified end-to-end flow" applies to every slice that changes runtime behaviour, including WS02-S5 (flagged prompt order: rebuild model-gateway, run a flagged 3-turn thread, show unchanged output and the date-notice position) and WS11-S1 (inference-core cache key now hashes effort: rebuild inference-core, run the same prompt at `quick` then `deep`, show two distinct keys and a miss). CI-only and doc-only slices (WS12 S1/S6-S11, WS06-S1, WS10-S0) are exempt and state `Evidence: CI run URL` instead. (K2-3)
- **N2. `buf breaking` is vacuous until WS12-S2.** `apps/Model Plane/proto/baseline.binpb` is 0 bytes at HEAD, and `.github/workflows/buf.yml:21,23` pass an unquoted path with a space. Every proto-tag DoD in the workstream sections that says `buf breaking` must read: prove additivity by diffing tag numbers against `git show HEAD:'apps/Model Plane/proto/model_plane/v1/<file>.proto'` and attach the diff; add WS12-S2 to the Depends of every slice that edits a proto. (K2-1)
- **N3. WS01-S0 scope.** S0 lands the Model Plane ZDR/session-core wave and the FE ZDR/typed-refusal hunks only. Data Plane (57), Ingestion/Quarry/integration-corev2 (66), Control (2), `.githooks`, `.gitleaks.toml` and the FE product-demo/home/Space/work-period files belong to other sessions. - **N4. Proto tags and `RunContext.5`.** Per-workstream tags in the slice tables are proposals; section 4.3 holds the finals (WS03 `resume = 19`, WS25 `failure_code = 10`, `org_policy = 24`, `connector_scope = 23`, `ExecuteStepRequest.thread_id = 13`, `skill_mounts = 14`, `QuestionAsked/Answered = 27/28`, `RunUsageProgress = 29`, `ThreadSummary` attention 16-18). `RunContext.5` is free (WS08 keeps its dedicated `RunAgentRequest.attachments = 20`); only 6 is reserved (WS20 run notes). ## Build ledger

Dated record of what has been built against this plan. For each slice the table gives the commits, the default of each switch it adds, and how far it is verified. Source-verified means the DoD tests were run on that commit. Live-verified means a rebuilt running stack showed the behaviour, with the date and where the evidence is kept. Nothing here is production evidence. Switch defaults name the compose service that reads each variable in `apps/Model Plane/deploy/docker-compose.yml`; they cite no line numbers, because those move.

**Live proof, S5, 2026-10-17.** Evidence: [`docs/compliance/evidence/live-proof-ws03-s5-effect-ledger-2026-10-17.txt`](../../docs/compliance/evidence/live-proof-ws03-s5-effect-ledger-2026-10-17.txt). On a developer stack built from b8792341 (migrations 0047 and 0048 applied, all four switches false at deploy), execution-core was killed while a save_memory call's reservation was open: reserved at 23:12:29.181, and the finalize never committed. The recovery worker ended the run as failed (outcome_unknown) at 23:27:45, and the sweep closed the row as unknown_outcome with closed_by = sweep at 23:28:25. The sweep switch was turned on only for that final step, a temporary trigger on the ledger table widened the kill window and was dropped, and every switch is off again afterwards. For the sweep to close a row, the reservation must be older than 900 s, its run terminal or parked, and SESSION_CORE_TOOL_LEDGER_SWEEP_ENABLED=true. This is a developer-stack proof, not production evidence.

**Live proof, S7, 2026-10-19.** Evidence: [`docs/compliance/evidence/live-proof-ws03-s7-in-place-resume-2026-10-19.txt`](../../docs/compliance/evidence/live-proof-ws03-s7-in-place-resume-2026-10-19.txt). On the developer stack (session-core, execution-core and model-gateway built from ad322122 plus the two fixes later committed as e22befde; the BFF image built from the worktree, the previous container kept as `verevon-gateway-rs-pre-fix-rollback`), with the five coupled switches on and the sweep switch on for the last stage, the proof showed: refusals as codes through the BFF; an in-place resume that ran the released step once (fence, round, pre_effect and pause folds in order); two simultaneous continues, one 200 and one 409; a stage-1 cold resume with the fold store off; a continue while the executor was down, with the obligation deadline renewed to consume + 15:00 in the consume transaction and recovery ending the run; a kill between reservation and finalize ending in `continue_outcome_unknown` (never a replay), the fold purged by recovery and the open row closed by the sweep as unknown_outcome. The proof found two defects no unit test could see (the 502 on the first decide of a loop approval; the gateway consuming against a service that does not serve it), both fixed in e22befde. It does not show a countable provider-side effect (`shell` has no executor in this stack). A temporary trigger widened the kill window and was dropped; every switch is off again and all three services were recreated healthy. This is a developer-stack proof, not production evidence.

**Still open in WS03.** S8-A (the executor's park and its resume, steps S8-0 to S8-8) is built behind default-off switches and source-verified, not live-verified (see its 2026-10-20 entry in `apps/Model Plane/docs/decisions/ledger.md`, which also lists the flip and kill order, the accepted limitations and the owner decisions: executor parking stays off until the owner approves, because no UI resumes a park yet). S8-B (crash-park: the recovery worker parking an active fold at its deadline) is split off and needs its own design. The S7 code chain is complete behind default-off switches and live-verified on a developer stack (above). Known follow-ups: two same-claim stage-1 loops are possible when no gateway pre-consume exists; the model-gateway integration tests dial the real dev stack for unmocked calls (a wedged Docker engine makes them hang). The 2026-10-16 entry in the decisions ledger stays proposed: the stage-1 proof on a rebuilt stack is met, the D1 owner ruling is the remaining trigger. The chat UI reaches the agent route only in plan mode, so an approved plan cannot start an effectful run (WS14 S2a is built behind default-off switches and live-verified on a developer stack 2026-10-20; see its 2026-10-19 and 2026-10-20 entries in the decisions ledger).
